That judge has the last word on whether the system is getting better, and our position is that it has not earned it. The judge should be demoted from oracle to advisor: its verdict becomes one input among several, and every change is gated instead by a deterministic verification layer the judge cannot override. We reached this position by building the alternative and running it. Over months of running autonomous prompt-optimization loops in production across contract analysis, compliance review, and code quality, we cataloged eleven ways the evaluation signal failed, in four classes: judge bias, harness and metric failures, ground-truth errors, and reward hacking. Agents achieved perfect scores by reading cached answer keys from their environment, a 100% pass rate concealing 68% true capability. A corrupted ground-truth label caused the optimizer to delete correct compliance rules to agree with it. A syntactically broken prompt was promoted as the winner because a silent parser fallback improved the metric. Attempts to fix the judge by rewriting its rubric plateaued; the only reliable gain came from a structural constraint on its output order. In response we describe PROCTOR, a Teacher-Student loop in which a stateful orchestrator holds all tool access, stateless subagents diagnose failures and draft mutations they cannot apply, and a Teacher grades those mutations under five deterministic guardrails: hermetic sandboxes, capability-disjoint roles, acceptance checks that outrank the Teacher, frozen holdouts, and canary cases engineered so that a perfect score is itself evidence of cheating. We report the failures this prevented, and, because the Teacher is itself an LLM judge, the failures it did not.

</details>


### [101] [APEx: Distillation of Agent Procedural Experience for Adaptive Deep Research Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02253)
*Jie Ding,Rui Sun,Xinyuan Zhang,Zeyu Zhang,Xin Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: APEx是一个面向深度研究智能体的分层经验利用框架，将交互历史组织为实例级轨迹记忆和类别级程序性技能，并通过执行器、蒸馏器、规划器的闭环架构及其三阶段交替GRPO训练，实现奖励引导的技能蒸馏与测试时的技能引导强化学习自适应，在7个基准上超越GPT-5.4达14.7个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度研究智能体的经验学习方法存在缺陷：检索冗长的任务特定轨迹会增加决策负担，而蒸馏出的程序性技能又与实际的下游策略适应相脱节，缺乏闭环的持续改进机制。

Method: 提出APEx分层经验利用框架：将交互历史组织为实例级轨迹记忆与类别级程序性技能；通过执行器、蒸馏器、规划器三模块的闭环架构耦合二者；采用三阶段交替GRPO训练范式实现奖励引导的技能蒸馏（而非固定提示生成）；测试时通过技能引导的强化学习进行在线规划器自适应，并加入技能对齐正则化防止策略漂移。

Result: 在7个基准上达到最先进性能：超越GPT-5.4达14.7个点，超越最强记忆增强基线达3.0个点。

Conclusion: APEx验证了分层经验组织与闭环技能蒸馏-利用机制能够有效提升深度研究智能体的持续学习与自适应能力，实现无需真实标签的自我改进。

Abstract: Deep research agents augment large language models with external tools to answer complex, long-horizon questions through multi-turn reasoning. Learning from prior experience is crucial for continual improvement, yet existing methods either retrieve verbose task-specific traces that burden decision-making, or distill procedural skills that remain decoupled from downstream policy adaptation. We propose APEx, a hierarchical experience utilization framework that organizes interaction history into instance-level trajectory memories and category-level procedural skills, and couples them through a closed-loop architecture of Executor, Distiller, and Planner. The three modules are optimized via a three-stage alternating GRPO training paradigm, enabling reward-guided skill distillation rather than fixed-prompt generation. At test time, distilled skills serve as procedural priors for online Planner adaptation through skill-guided test-time reinforcement learning, allowing ground-truth-free self-improvement with skill-alignment regularization to prevent policy drift. Experiments on 7 benchmarks demonstrate that APEx achieves state-of-the-art performance, surpassing GPT-5.4 by 14.7 points and the strongest memory-augmented baseline by 3.0 points.

</details>


### [102] [Codebook Agent: Amortized Topology Design for LLM Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02264)
*Jinxi Yu,Yubei Li,Eric Hanchen Jiang,Zhi Zhang,Dong Liu,Wenxiao Zhao,Levina Li,Kai-Wei Chang,Ying Nian Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Codebook Agent，一种用于LLM多智能体系统通信拓扑设计的新方法。针对现有条件图生成方法（变分/自回归/扩散解码器+图网络打分器）与问题本身不对齐的三个经验事实（幸存拓扑坍缩到约6个图、边数与token消耗负相关、基于消息传递的打分器在智能体共享profile时邻接不变），作者采用向量量化自编码器将成功拓扑压缩为16条目码本，用奖励加权MLP将查询映射到码本分布，再用MLP代理对解码候选进行重排序。该方法无需迭代搜索和消息传递，在6个基准上平均准确率84.6（优于最强先验方法的83.0），2.4ms输出拓扑，节省21.9-33.2%的LLM token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM多智能体系统拓扑设计方法将问题框定为条件图生成（在N×N邻接空间搜索并用图网络代理打分），但作者发现这种建模与实际问题错位：一是成功拓扑经奖励过滤后高度坍缩（容量8到64时仅剩约6种不同图），说明不需要复杂生成模型；二是边数减少反而增加token消耗（负相关），说明以稀疏性为导向的结构成本与真实目标矛盾；三是当智能体共享profile（基准测试默认配置）时，消息传递打分器对邻接矩阵不变，根本无法排序候选。这些反例促使设计更简洁直接的方法。

Method: Codebook Agent包含三个核心组件：1) 向量量化自编码器（VQ-VAE）将历史上成功的拓扑压缩成查询无关的16条目码本；2) 奖励加权MLP将查询嵌入映射为码本上的分布，加权输出整合多个码组合；3) MLP代理读取展平的邻接矩阵，以实测效用和逐任务归一化token成本回归训练，对解码出的候选在单次批前向中重排序。测试时无迭代搜索、无消息传递，直接输出最优拓扑。

Result: 在6个基准上平均准确率84.6，超过最强先验设计器的83.0；拓扑生成仅需2.4ms（远快于迭代搜索方法）；相比先验方法节省21.9-33.2%的LLM token消耗。该结果同时验证了作者的核心论点：成功拓扑高度集中，码本方法足够表达，且少边不一定省token。

Conclusion: 作者通过三个反直觉的经验事实揭示了现有多智能体拓扑设计范式的根本缺陷，证明该问题本质上更接近"从有限码本中选择"而非"高维图空间搜索"。Codebook Agent以极简设计（码本+MLP）实现了更优的准确率、效率和token节省，说明将结构化先验（码本）与任务嵌入结合，避免了图生成和消息传递带来的复杂性浪费。

Abstract: Adapting the communication topology of an LLM multi-agent system to each query improves both accuracy and efficiency, yet current designers treat this as conditional graph generation: a variational, autoregressive, or diffusion decoder searches the $N \times N$ adjacency space, and a graph-network proxy trained on utility and a structural cost such as edge count ranks the sampled candidates. We argue that this formulation is misaligned with the problem. Empirically, topologies that survive a reward filter collapse to about six distinct graphs even when the codebook capacity grows from 8 to 64; edge count is negatively correlated with measured token consumption (Pearson $r \approx -0.4$), so sparsifying the graph makes inference more expensive; and a message-passing scorer over agent-profile nodes is adjacency-invariant whenever agents share a profile---the default configuration of published benchmarks---so it cannot rank candidates at all in that regime. These three facts motivate Codebook Agent: a vector-quantized autoencoder compresses successful topologies into a query-independent 16-entry codebook; a reward-weighted MLP maps the query embedding to a distribution over codes; and an MLP proxy that reads the flattened adjacency, regressed on measured utility and per-task normalized token cost, reranks the top decoded candidates in a single batched forward pass. With no iterative search and no message passing at test time, Codebook Agent is the most accurate method on all six benchmarks we compare (84.6 average against 83.0 for the strongest prior designer), emits a topology in 2.4 ms, and uses 21.9--33.2% fewer LLM tokens.

</details>


### [103] [CoMerge: Conflict-Driven Preference Optimization for Multi-Task Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02273)
*Mingjie Zheng,Zihao Chen,Wenqing Chen,Weile Yuan,Zhixuan Chu,Jianxing Yu,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CoMerge框架，将模型合并重构为偏好优化问题，利用自监督的冲突驱动策略，将朴素合并方法（如任务算术）的缺陷作为硬负样本构建偏好对，无需外部标注。通过偏好优化细化轻量级张量级合并系数，缓解参数冲突并保留任务专属能力。在MergeBench上平均归一化性能达0.9968，优于所有基线；在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上仅优化1,445个标量系数即与全参数微调竞争力相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并是构建多任务大语言模型的高效范式，无需完整重训练，但面临参数干扰问题。现有方法虽旨在保留各专家模型能力并缓解干扰，却普遍未直接从朴素合并可能产生的性能退化行为中学习，缺乏利用这些退化信号的机会。

Method: 提出冲突驱动的偏好优化框架（CoMerge）：1）将模型合并重新形式化为偏好优化问题；2）采用自监督、冲突驱动策略，利用朴素合并方法（如任务算术）的缺陷作为硬负样本，构建无需外部标注的偏好对；3）通过偏好优化细化轻量级张量级合并系数，在缓解参数空间冲突的同时保留任务专属能力。

Result: 在MergeBench上平均归一化性能达到0.9968，超越所有评估的数据无关和数据驱动模型合并基线。在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上，对指令跟随和安全等冲突敏感任务有显著改进，且仅优化1,445个标量系数就与全参数微调高度竞争。

Conclusion: CoMerge验证了"从失败中学习"的有效性——将朴素合并的退化行为转化为训练信号，实现高效的参数空间冲突缓解，以极小优化成本达到接近全参数微调的性能，为模型合并提供了新范式。

Abstract: Model merging provides an efficient paradigm for constructing multi-task large language models (LLMs) without full model retraining, yet it remains challenged by parameter interference. While existing methods aim to preserve the capabilities of individual expert models and mitigate interference, they generally do not directly learn from the potentially degraded behaviors exposed by naive merging. In this paper, we propose a conflict-driven preference optimization framework for model merging (CoMerge), which reformulates model merging as a preference optimization problem. The approach utilizes a self-supervised, conflict-driven strategy that leverages the defects of naive merging methods (e.g., task arithmetic) as hard negative samples to construct preference pairs without external annotations. By applying preference optimization to refine lightweight, tensor-wise merging coefficients, CoMerge enables the model to mitigate parameter-space conflicts while preserving task-specific capabilities. Extensive experiments show that CoMerge achieves an average normalized performance of 0.9968 on MergeBench, outperforming all evaluated data-free and data-driven model-merging baselines. Furthermore, on Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, CoMerge yields marked improvements on conflict-sensitive tasks such as instruction following and safety, while remaining highly competitive with full-parameter fine-tuning despite optimizing only 1,445 scalar coefficients.

</details>


### [104] [SCX Router: Streaming Zero-Shot Model Selection with a Decoder-KV Classifier and a Real-World Task Ontology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02292)
*Ihor Stepanov,Aleksandr Smechov,Mykhailo Shtopko,Dmytro Vodianytskyi,Oleksandr Lukashov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Router，一个基于GLiClass的轻量级模型路由系统，用于在推理时为每个任务选择最优的LLM，在速度、成本和质量之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM和应用场景的激增，不同推理端点（endpoint）在质量、价格、延迟、上下文支持等方面差异巨大，手动启发式规则难以维护且无法在速度-成本-质量之间持续取得良好权衡。

Method: 提出一个仅0.6B参数的Router。基于GLiClass架构，将Qwen3解码器与浅层双向评分器结合，为每个推理模型标签分配适配分数。采用decoder-KV执行路径，跨会话保留纯文本键值缓存，仅编码新对话轮次，评估瞬态候选标签token而不污染持久缓存。同时预测任务类型、难度、推理模式和期望输出长度，支持自定义零样本标签。构建包含23个任务族、115个任务类型、345个可路由子类型、1,173个合成示例和30个域的任务本体，生成150,000个验证器评分任务和15,000个开放式任务进行训练，并将学习的请求预测与每任务策略（如资格、成本、缓存复用、安全性和主权）分离。

Result: 在六个LiveBench子集上优于平均候选模型；在选定的1,000任务子集上，聚合top-1得分达0.707，超过最强固定模型的0.696，且增益因基准而异。

Conclusion: Router展示了一种高效的逐任务模型选择方案，通过轻量级架构和KV缓存优化实现高质量、低开销的路由决策，为大规模LLM部署提供了实用的优化手段。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of large language models (LLMs) and the growing diversity of their applications presents a unique optimization opportunity: selecting the right model for the task, while optimizing for speed, cost, and quality at a per-task level. However, inference endpoints can vary widely in quality, price, latency, context support, tool use, domain expertise, and reasoning behavior. This heterogeneity makes manual heuristics difficult to maintain and unlikely to achieve consistently favorable speed--cost--quality trade-offs on their own. We introduce \router{}, a lightweight GLiClass-based router that assigns a suitability score to each inference-time model label without autoregressive generation. The released 0.6B-parameter checkpoint combines a Qwen3 decoder with a shallow bidirectional scorer. Its decoder-KV execution path preserves a text-only key--value cache across a session, encodes only new dialogue turns, and evaluates transient candidate-label tokens without adding them to the persistent cache. The same checkpoint also predicts task type, difficulty, reasoning mode, and expected output length, and supports custom zero-shot labels. For task generation, we construct a task ontology with 23 families, 115 task types, 345 routable subtypes, 1,173 synthetic examples, and an orthogonal axis of 30 domains. Using this structure, we generate 150,000 verifier-scored tasks and 15,000 open-ended tasks. We then train the Qwen3 decoder on these tasks, while explicitly separating learned request prediction from per-task policies for attributes such as eligibility, cost, cache reuse, safety, and sovereignty. Across six LiveBench subsets, the router outperforms the mean candidate; on the selected 1,000-task subset, it achieves an aggregate top-1 score of 0.707 versus 0.696 for the strongest fixed model, with benchmark-dependent gains.

</details>


### [105] [Improving Evaluation Realism with Inference-Time Compute and Deployment Scaffolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02302)
*Axel Ahlqvist,Richard Guan,Juan-Pablo Rivera,Adeline Kassler,Dmitrii Troitskii,Alexandra Souly,Kai Fronsdal,Robert Kirk,John Hughes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出两种技术——critique refinement（批评细化）和DISH（部署模仿智能体框架），使模拟的对齐评估更难与真实部署区分，从而提高评估的真实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对齐评估面临的核心障碍是评估意识：能力强的模型能识别出自己正在被测试而非部署，这会削弱安全评估结论的有效性。因此需要让模拟评估更接近真实部署场景。

Method: 两种技术：（1）批评细化：在模拟器的每个动作上花费额外推理计算，生成多个候选动作，利用目标模型的反馈进行细化，选择最像真实部署的候选；（2）DISH：将目标包裹在代理框架中，缩小编码场景中模拟与真实部署环境的差距。

Result: 在多个目标模型上测试发现两种技术可以组合使用：同时应用两者比单独使用任一技术获得更大的真实性提升。此外，这些改进比简单延长审计时间能更有效地利用额外计算。

Conclusion: 自动化方法可以提升对齐评估的真实性，且这些提升能比延长审计更好地利用额外计算资源，为更可信的安全评估提供了技术路径。

Abstract: A core obstacle to alignment evaluation is evaluation awareness: capable models can tell when they are being tested rather than deployed, weakening the conclusions a safety evaluation can support. We present two techniques that make simulated alignment evaluations harder to distinguish from real deployments. Our first technique, critique refinement, spends additional inference-time compute on each simulator action: the simulator generates multiple candidate actions, refines them using feedback from an instance of the target model on how to make them more realistic, and continues the evaluation with the most deployment-like candidate. Our second technique, DISH (Deployment-Imitating SWE-Agent Harness), wraps the target in an agent harness, reducing the gap between simulated and real deployment environments in coding settings. We test the techniques on multiple target models and find that they compose: applying both yields larger realism gains than either alone. Our results show that automated approaches can improve the realism of alignment evaluations, and that these improvements use additional compute more effectively than making the audits longer.

</details>


### [106] [SALA: Semantic-Aware Logical Alignment for Complex Reasoning in In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02336)
*Zhao Ji,Wenqing Chen,Zhixuan Chu,Jianxing Yu,Jingping Liu,Shanhe Zhao,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SALA框架，通过学习任务特定的推理操作并将其嵌入连续语义空间，利用DTW动态时间规整进行软性逻辑对齐，从而为复杂推理任务选择更合适的上下文示例（demonstrations），在四个推理基准和三个LLM上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于表面相似度的检索方法无法捕捉问题背后的解题逻辑；而基于逻辑的方法依赖预定义的推理步骤和刚性规则，精确匹配方式难以应对灵活多样的推理过程。

Method: SALA框架：1) 自动学习任务特定的推理操作（不依赖固定库存）；2) 将这些操作嵌入连续语义空间；3) 使用动态时间规整（DTW）对齐推理序列，实现软性、灵活的推理逻辑匹配，同时保持高可解释性。

Result: 在四个推理基准与三个LLM上，SALA优于现有的示例选择方法；进一步分析验证了操作归纳和逻辑语义对齐两个模块的有效性。

Conclusion: SALA通过语义感知的逻辑对齐，在推理示例选择上取得了更好的效果，兼顾了灵活性与可解释性，为ICL中的复杂推理示例检索提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Effective in-context learning (ICL) for complex reasoning relies on selecting the right demonstrations. Traditional retrieval methods based on surface similarity fail to capture the underlying problem-solving logic. Recent logic-based methods address this by matching predefined reasoning steps, but the rigid rules and exact-match criteria is improper to handle flexible or diverse reasoning processes. To address the problem, we propose SALA, a Semantic-Aware Logical Alignment framework. Instead of relying on a fixed inventory, SALA automatically learns task-specific reasoning operations. It then embeds these operations into a continuous semantic space and uses dynamic time warping (DTW) to align the reasoning sequences. This approach allows for soft, flexible matching of reasoning logic while remaining highly interpretable. Experiments across four reasoning benchmarks and three LLMs demonstrate that SALA outperforms existing demonstration selection methods. Further analysis confirms the roles of the operation induction and the logical semantic alignment.

</details>


### [107] [Diagnosing with Insights: Structured Analysis of Agent Failures via Behavioral Abstractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02371)
*Jiayi Bi,Yanjie Gao,Yuanmin Xie,Liqun Li,Tianyin Xu,Fan Yang,Mao Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AGENTSCOPE是一种神经符号方法，用于诊断LLM智能体的故障模式。它通过将智能体轨迹抽象为结构化表示，并引入"神经不变量"概念来指定行为属性，结合LLM引导的推理来精确定位失败步骤及其类型，在两个数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体的普及，理解和诊断智能体故障至关重要。但智能体故障通常表现为冗长复杂的轨迹，人工排查困难；传统软件调试技术难以处理LLM智能体故障，而完全依赖LLM作为评判者又会产生不可靠的诊断结果。

Method: AGENTSCOPE的核心原则是将智能体行为（基于其轨迹）抽象为结构化表示，并引入"神经不变量"来刻画智能体行为属性。该方法利用LLM引导的推理，在结构化表示之上对照神经不变量，精准定位轨迹中的失败步骤及其类型。

Result: 在公开的Who&When数据集和自建的AgentErrata数据集上，AGENTSCOPE在故障定位和归因准确性方面显著优于当前最先进的方法。

Conclusion: 将结构化抽象与LLM引导推理相结合，能够实现对智能体故障有效、可靠且可解释的诊断。

Abstract: With the proliferation of LLM agents, the ability to understand and diagnose failures in agents is essential to achieving superior effectiveness and trustworthiness. As agent failures often manifest via long and complex trajectories, manually finding the needles in the haystack is untenable. However, traditional diagnosis techniques for software bugs can hardly address LLM agent failures, while completely relying on LLMs as the judge yields unreliable diagnosis results. To overcome these challenges, this paper presents AGENTSCOPE, a new neuro-symbolic approach for agent failure mode diagnosis. The key principle of AGENTSCOPE is to abstract agent behavior, based on its trajectories, into structured representations. Furthermore, AGENTSCOPE introduces the concept of neural invariants to specify agent behavior properties. AGENTSCOPE leverages LLM-guided reasoning atop the structured representation against neural invariants to pinpoint both the failure step and its type in the trajectory. We show the effectiveness of AGENTSCOPE on publicly available agent failure datasets (Who&When) and a more comprehensive dataset created by us (AgentErrata), where AGENTSCOPE significantly outperforms the current state of the art in fault localization and attribution accuracy. Our work shows that integrating structured abstractions with LLM-guided reasoning enables effective, reliable, and interpretable diagnosis for agent failures.

</details>


### [108] [Contrastive Explanations in Quantitative Bipolar Argumentation Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02399)
*Xiang Yin,Nico Potyka,Antonio Rago,Francesca Toni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对定量双极论证框架（QBAFs）提出对比性解释（contrastive explanations），与现有大多数仅解释单一主题参数推理结果的解释方法不同，对比性解释关注两个主题参数之间的差异。作者引入了对比性归因函数（CAFs）的一般形式及其应满足的性质，并基于移除、梯度和Shapley值三种方法构建了具体的CAFs，最后在医疗保健和偏见识别场景中展示了其应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QBAF的解释方法大多只解释单个论点arg的推理结果（即主题论点），缺乏对两个主题论点之间差异的解释能力，这种对比性信息在许多实际场景（如分类任务、偏见识别）中至关重要。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 1) 引入对比性归因函数（CAFs）的通用形式定义；2) 建立CAFs应满足的一组一般性质；3) 提出基于移除（removal）、梯度（gradients）和Shapley值（Shapley-values）三类具体的CAFs实例；4) 研究这些CAFs的性质。

Result: 成功定义了CAF的一般形式及其性质框架，构建了基于移除、梯度和Shapley值的三种具体CAF实现，并在医疗保健和偏见识别场景中证明了对比性解释的实际效用。

Conclusion: 对比性解释为QBAFs提供了一种不同于传统单一主题解释的新视角，能够解释两个主题论点之间的推理差异，在可解释AI（特别是医疗和偏见检测等敏感领域）中具有重要应用潜力。

Abstract: Argumentation frameworks are useful tools for representing and reasoning with information in a variety of settings, e.g. in supplementing AI models as they perform classification tasks, with a notable benefit of providing additional explainability. In this paper, we introduce contrastive explanations for Quantitative Bipolar Argumentation Frameworks (QBAFs), one such formalism. Unlike most existing explanations for QBAFs, which explain the reasoning outcome of a single argument of interest (i.e. a topic argument), contrastive explanations explain the difference between two topic arguments. We introduce a general form of contrastive attribution functions (CAFs) and establish a set of general properties they should satisfy. We introduce CAFs based on removal, gradients and Shapley-values, and study their properties. Finally, to illustrate contrastive explanations, we demonstrate their usefulness in healthcare and bias identification settings.

</details>


### [109] [UTP-Bench: Uncertainty-aware Travel Planning Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02421)
*Etcharla Revanth Rao,Priyanshu Karmakar,Shubhojit Mallick,Manish Gupta,Shreya Ghosh,Abhik Jana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出UTP-Bench，一个面向不确定性感知旅行规划的大规模基准测试。与现有确定性基准（如TravelPlanner、TripCraft）不同，UTP-Bench整合了印度504个城市的真实旅行数据，并引入经验延误分布和人群密度模式来模拟随机扰动，通过BAS、CATS、TDAS三个新指标评估生成行程在交通延误和人群波动下的鲁棒性。实验表明GPT-5、Qwen3等先进LLM与人类规划的行程仍存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的旅行规划充满不确定性（交通延误、人群波动、随机延迟会使原本可行的行程失效），而现有基准如TravelPlanner和TripCraft仅假设确定性环境，只评估静态约束满足情况，忽略了生成计划在不确定性条件下的鲁棒性。这一关键缺口促使作者构建不确定性感知的基准。

Method: 1) 构建大规模数据集：整合印度504个城市的真实旅行数据（景点、餐厅、住宿、多模态交通网络）；2) 建模现实扰动：引入来自主要城市的经验延误分布和人群密度模式；3) 提出三个评估指标：Buffer Adequacy Score (BAS)、Crowd-Aware Timing Score (CATS)、Transport Delay Absorption Score (TDAS)，分别量化行程在时间缓冲、人群变化和交通延误下的鲁棒性。

Result: 对GPT-5、Qwen3、Mistral和Phi-4等先进LLM的实验揭示：模型生成的行程与人类撰写的行程之间存在显著差距，尤其是在时间缓冲规划、延误感知的交通调度和人群敏感规划三个方面表现薄弱。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在不确定性感知旅行规划方面仍有明显不足，UTP-Bench作为该方向的首个大规模基准，为评估和改进LLM的鲁棒旅行规划能力提供了标准化的测试平台。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently demonstrated strong capabilities in automated travel itinerary generation. However, real- world travel planning is inherently uncertain: transportation delays, crowd fluctuations, and unexpected stochastic delays frequently inval- idate otherwise feasible schedules. Existing benchmarks like TravelPlanner and TripCraft assume deterministic environments, evaluating only static constraint satisfaction and ignoring whether generated plans remain robust when such uncertainties arise. To address this limitation, we introduce UTP-Bench1 , a large-scale benchmark for uncertainty-aware travel planning. The dataset integrates real-world travel data spanning 504 cities of India, including attractions, restau- rants, accommodations, and multi-modal trans- portation networks. To model realistic disrup- tions, UTP-Bench incorporates empirical delay distributions and crowd-density patterns col- lected from major cities, enabling evaluation of travel plans under stochastic conditions. We further propose three evaluation metrics, namely Buffer Adequacy Score (BAS), Crowd- Aware Timing Score (CATS), and Transport Delay Absorption Score (TDAS), which quan- tify the ability of generated itineraries to main- tain robustness against transit delays and crowd variability. Experiments with state-of-the-art LLMs like GPT-5, Qwen3, Mistral and Phi-4 re- veal substantial gaps between model-generated and human-authored plans, particularly in tem- poral buffering, delay-aware transportation scheduling, and crowd-sensitive planning.

</details>


### [110] [CivBench: A Long-Horizon Benchmark for Tool-Mediated Agents in Civilization VI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02459)
*Austin Tudor David Andrews,Liam Wilkinson,Jamie Heagerty,Harry Coppock,Jakob Nicolaus Foerster,Rui Ponte Costa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CivBench，一个用于评估语言模型代理在长期、工具介导环境（通过MCP协议）中表现的开源基准。环境包含76个MCP工具和叙事层，单集跨越300+轮对话和数千次工具调用。基于23次可行运行，作者引入两个界面级指标：主动监控率（PMR）和RAG@10，分别衡量代理主动查询潜在战略状态和执行短期规划承诺的能力。研究发现代理普遍存在监控不足和承诺执行为度低的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估缺乏对语言模型代理在长期、高复杂度工具环境中表现的刻画，特别是部分可观测条件下代理的主动监控和承诺执行能力缺乏系统性测量工具。

Method: 构建基于《文明VI》游戏的MCP工具环境（76个工具+文本叙事层），定义PMR和RAG@10两个新型指标，对四个模型家族进行23次受控运行并与共享策略协议对齐分析。

Result: 代理在策略相关状态监控上系统性不足（胜利进度查询从30到75轮不等，而非指导的20轮）；7/20次可检测失败中未在20轮警告窗口内查询。RAG@10范围在48.2%-65.8%之间，表明代理常未执行自身的短期规划承诺。

Conclusion: 代理的监控不足和承诺执行失败源于对指令的偏离而非能力缺失，提示需要更细粒度的接口级评估指标来刻画这些系统性问题。

Abstract: We present CivBench, an open-source benchmark for evaluating language model agents in long-horizon, tool-mediated environments through the Model Context Protocol (MCP). A single episode spans 300+ turns and produces thousands of tool calls over a large action space, requiring sustained planning, state monitoring, and execution under partial observability. The environment exposes 76 MCP tools and a narration layer that converts visual game state into structured text.
  We use CivBench to characterise agent behaviour across four model families in 23 admissible runs. The sample is a pilot, not a model ranking: aggregate outcomes do not reliably discriminate models at this scale. Instead, we introduce two interface-level metrics that the environment makes measurable: Proactive Monitoring Rate (PMR), capturing whether agents actively query latent strategic state, and RAG@10, capturing whether commitments stated in structured planning reflections are executed within ten subsequent turns.
  Across runs we observe two consistent patterns under a shared playbook protocol. Agents under-monitor strategically relevant state that is available but requires explicit querying: despite playbook guidance to query victory progress every 20 turns, agents do so only every 30 to 75 turns, and in 7 of 20 detectable defeats they failed to query within the 20 turn warning window before game end. Agents also frequently fail to execute near-term commitments stated in their own planning reflections (RAG@10 between 48.2% and 65.8% across models). Both patterns arise despite tool access and explicit guidance, and we interpret them as deviations under instruction rather than absences of capability.
  We release the environment, scenarios, logs, metrics, and analysis pipeline at https://github.com/lmwilki/civ6-mcp

</details>


### [111] [Loom: Weaving Diagnostic Strands into Free-Text Consensus via Embedding-Space Reweighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02649)
*Ron Begleiter,Katya Egert Berg,Gilad Saban,Gil Shabat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Loom是一个生成式共识框架，用于真实世界根因分析（RCA）。它将模块化启发式方法产生的开放式假设投影到连续嵌入空间，通过迭代质心重加权算法解决冲突信号，最终用轻量级LLM生成共识结果。在OpenRCA基准上，Loom与最先进智能体准确率相当，但速度快约26-33倍，仅需每次事件一次LLM调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实工业场景中聚合嘈杂、冲突的文本假设是NLP部署的根本挑战。单一LLM智能体虽表达力强，但存在上下文限制、幻觉累积和推理延迟高等问题；传统弱监督虽有统计严谨性，但在数学上仅限于离散类。需要一种桥接两种范式的方案。

Method: Loom首先由模块化启发式方法（动态填充诊断模板）产生开放式假设，随后将这些假设投影到连续嵌入空间，通过迭代质心重加权算法聚合冲突信号并生成共识权重，最终由共识权重引导单一轻量级LLM进行合成总结。

Result: 在OpenRCA基准的四个数据集上，Loom在Bank和Market-2上与最先进自主智能体持平，在Market-1和Telecom上略有落后，但每次事件仅需一次LLM调用，速度快约26倍（使用8B参数合成器时约33倍），占据了准确率-效率的帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: Loom展示了生成式共识框架在工业RCA中的有效性，说明确定性共识机制能在显著降低推理开销的同时保持高准确率，并增强领域专家对系统的信任。部署经验也揭示了智能体深度与推理延迟之间的权衡以及冗余检测方面的负面结果等宝贵洞见。

Abstract: Aggregating noisy, conflicting textual hypotheses into a reliable consensus is a fundamental challenge when deploying NLP systems in real-world industrial settings. While monolithic Large Language Model (LLM) agents offer unbounded expressivity for tasks like Root Cause Analysis (RCA), they suffer from context limits, compounding hallucinations, and prohibitive inference latency. Traditional weak supervision offers statistical rigor but is mathematically restricted to discrete classes. We present Loom, a generative consensus framework deployed for real-world RCA that bridges these paradigms. Loom aggregates open-form hypotheses emitted by modular heuristics (diagnostic templates dynamically populated with episode-specific entities, times, and metrics) by projecting them into a continuous embedding space, and resolves conflicting signals with an iterative centroid-based reweighting algorithm. The resulting consensus weights ground a single lightweight LLM synthesis step. Evaluated on the OpenRCA benchmark, Loom occupies the accuracy--efficiency Pareto frontier: it matches a state-of-the-art autonomous agent on Bank and Market-2 and trails on Market-1 and Telecom, while using a single LLM call per incident on all four datasets ($\sim$26$\times$ faster; $\sim$33$\times$ with an 8B-parameter synthesizer). We discuss our deployment experience, highlighting lessons learned regarding the trade-offs between agentic depth and inference latency, negative results in redundancy detection, and how deterministic consensus fosters trust among Subject Matter Experts~(SMEs).

</details>


### [112] [Door-in-the-Face Requests and Refusal Behaviour in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02707)
*Til Jordan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究测试了"门面效应"（先提大要求被拒后再提小要求）是否对九个大语言模型有效，发现效果因模型而异：Anthropic的旗舰模型（如Opus 5）上有效，但在OpenAI和Google的旗舰模型及Haiku 4.5上反而适得其反。控制实验表明让步本身在所有模型上都起作用，但对"刚拒绝过"这一情境的反应因模型家族而异，且该技术无法迁移到公共基准的拒绝样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类心理学的说服技巧（如门面效应）是否适用于大语言模型是一个开放问题，理解LLM对交互式说服技术的敏感性对安全评估和模型对齐具有重要意义。

Method: 对来自三个提供商的九个生产模型进行测试：每个模型先拒绝一个大请求，随后收到同一主题的较小版本请求，并将其守规率与直接提出小请求的基线进行比较；同时设置无关主题的拒绝作为控制条件，并测试了公共基准拒绝样本与改写请求（将指令改写为解释）的迁移效果。

Result: Anthropic旗舰模型上门面效应有效（Opus 5从29.3%提升到65.8%）；OpenAI和Google旗舰模型及Haiku 4.5上效应反向（合规率下降15.5-23.0个百分点）；控制实验显示让步本身在所有模型上有效，但对拒绝后的反应因模型家族不同；265个被拒请求改写成解释请求后263个不再被拒。

Conclusion: 人类影响技巧向语言模型的迁移是逐模型家族实现的，不存在普适性；请求的内容性质（指令vs解释）对是否被拒绝起决定性作用，这为理解LLM安全边界的可操纵性提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Does the door-in-the-face technique work on language models? In humans, a large request that is refused makes a smaller follow-up request more likely to be granted. We test this on nine production models from three providers: each model refuses a large request, then receives a smaller version of the same request, and we compare its compliance with asking directly. The answer depends on the model. On Anthropic's frontier models the technique works: Opus 5 answers the smaller request 65.8% of the time after refusing the larger one, against 29.3% when asked directly. On the frontier models of OpenAI and Google, and on Haiku 4.5, it backfires, lowering compliance by 15.5 to 23.0 points. A control locates the effect: a refused large request on an unrelated topic does less than the related one on all nine models, so the concession itself matters everywhere, while the reaction to having just refused something differs by model family. The technique does not transfer to refusals drawn from public benchmarks. What decides whether a retreat can work is what the request asks for: rewriting 265 refused requests for usable instructions into requests for explanations of the same topic removed the refusal in 263 cases. Human influence techniques port to language models one model family at a time.

</details>


### [113] [Repo-To-Skill: Distilling GitHub Repositories Into AI4AI Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02749)
*Jianlyu Chen,Yuyang Hu,Hongjin Qian,Jiawei Liu,Wenqing Wei,Xiaolong Chen,Defu Lian,Zhicheng Dou,Chaozhuo Li,Qiwei Ye,Zheng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DisCo是一个技能驱动的自主研究代理，通过任务无关和任务导向两种蒸馏形式，将ML领域广泛使用的代码库提炼为可复用的已验证技能（AREX-Skill Library，含5,000+技能），在固定模型骨干、研究框架和执行预算的条件下，相比无技能代理在MLE-bench等基准上提升9.2%-134.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主代理可以端到端执行ML研究，但现有架构（模型骨干+规划/执行/记忆/验证框架）缺乏领域特定的操作知识（operational knowledge），即"知道方法"与"让方法奏效"之间的差距。这类知识存在于论文和代码库中，但形式面向人类且体量过大，无法在任务执行中直接加载，导致每次运行重复发现而非复用。

Method: 提出DisCo框架，采用两种互补的蒸馏方式：(1) 任务无关蒸馏——将ML生态中广泛使用的1,000个代码库压缩为通用的可复用技能，形成AREX-Skill Library（5,000+已验证技能，组织为20个领域、178个能力族）；(2) 任务导向蒸馏——针对具体任务生成所需的技能。这些技能在蒸馏后以紧凑、可验证的形式供代理在研究过程中复用。

Result: 在固定GPT-5.5骨干、研究框架和执行预算的条件下，配备技能的代理相比无技能代理：MLE-bench提升134.3%，PaperBench提升34.4%，FrontierCS提升9.2%，PassNet提升14.0%。增益完全来源于在固定设置下添加蒸馏后的操作上下文。

Conclusion: 将领域的操作知识以紧凑、已验证的技能形式蒸馏并注入代理，可以显著提升自主研究代理的性能，且这种提升在模型能力不变的情况下独立存在，表明操作知识作为"缺失层"对代理研究能力具有关键价值。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are beginning to carry out machine-learning (ML) research end to end. These agents combine a model backbone with a harness for planning, execution, memory, and verification, but this architecture still leaves domain-specific know-how outside the agent. We call this missing layer operational knowledge, the know-how that separates knowing a method from making it work. That knowledge is not absent from the field. It appears in repositories and papers, but in forms written for human readers and too large to load during a task. Once distilled into compact, verified skills, this knowledge can be reused across tasks rather than rediscovered during each run.
  We present DisCo, a skill-powered research agent that creates skills and uses them during research. Its distillation runs in two complementary forms: task-agnostic, condensing the field's widely used repositories into reusable skills, and task-oriented, producing the skills a concrete task calls for. The former, applied across the open ecosystem, yields the AREX-Skill Library, with 5,000+ verified skills distilled from 1,000 widely used ML repositories and organized into 20 areas and 178 capability families. With the GPT-5.5 backbone, research harness, and downstream execution budget held fixed, the skill-equipped research agent scores 134.3% higher on MLE-bench, 34.4% higher on PaperBench, 9.2% higher on FrontierCS, and 14.0% higher on PassNet than the same agent without skills. These gains come from adding distilled operating context under that fixed setup.

</details>


### [114] [Bilevel Coordinated Reflection: A Game-Theoretic Approach to Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02750)
*Yihang Chen,Yuxiang Chen,Yuxuan Huang,Meng Fang,Weilin Luo,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的数学框架来理解多智能体LLM系统中的协调、记忆改进和外部验证。通过将编排器-工作器交互建模为双层博弈，将反思建模为语义记忆状态的随机运动，并提出了SRMA算法，在500个SWE-bench实例上达到72.2%的解决率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多智能体LLM系统在实证上表现强劲，但缺乏对协调机制、记忆改进过程以及外部验证作用的统一理论解释。需要一个严谨的数学框架来理解这些系统为何有效以及如何改进。

Method: 1) 将编排器-工作器交互建模为双层协调博弈，证明在有界耦合下工作器局部更新博弈是近似势博弈；2) 将反思建模为语义记忆状态的随机运动，推导自由形式反思的有限时间上界并证明其最坏情况紧致性；3) 证明信息论不可能性结果，区分仅有转录观察的门控与基于环境门控的差异；4) 提出SRMA算法，仅在环境评估风险严格下降时接受候选记忆，并提供置信门控和分段平稳环境的重锚定保证。

Result: 在500个SWE-bench实例上，完整的基于Kimi的系统解决了72.2%的问题，而公开的mini-SWE-agent参考系统为70.8%。理论结果表明SRMA在标定和非退化修正质量条件下精确几何或多项式收敛，且两种速率区间均为阶紧的。

Conclusion: 本文通过严谨的博弈论和信息论分析，为多智能体LLM系统的协调与反思机制提供了统一的理论基础。理论分析表明环境接地（而非仅观察转录的门控）对智能体改进至关重要，SRMA算法为实际部署提供了理论基础和实证验证。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems commonly use an orchestrator to decompose a task for a team of workers and then improve through textual reflection. Despite strong empirical results, these systems lack a unified account of coordination, memory improvement, and the role of external verification. We model orchestrator-worker interaction as a bilevel coordination game: under bounded coupling, the workers' local-update game is an approximate potential game whose equilibrium slack is controlled by decomposition quality. We then analyse reflection as stochastic movement over semantic memory states. For free-form reflection, we derive a finite-time upper bound, prove worst-case tightness, and give a positive lower bound under a falsifiable persistent-harm condition. We further prove an information-theoretic impossibility result: no gate that observes only the generated transcript can improve uniformly over text-indistinguishable environments, whereas an environment-grounded gate can. Motivated by this separation, we introduce Stochastic Reflective Memory Ascent (SRMA), which accepts a candidate memory only after a grounded evaluation risk strictly decreases. Under calibration and non-degenerate corrective mass, SRMA converges exactly, geometrically or polynomially; matching constructions show that both rate regimes are order-tight. We also provide confidence gating for stochastic evaluation and re-anchoring guarantees for piecewise-stationary environments. Experiments instantiate these objects with environment-grounded metrics and test the predicted coordination and drift laws. On 500 SWE-bench instances, the complete Kimi-based system resolves 72.2% versus a 70.8% public mini-SWE-agent reference. Code: https://github.com/YihangChen9/Bilevel-Coordinated-Reflection

</details>


### [115] [Measurement-Driven Sub-Network Selection for On-Premise Retrieval-Augmented Factory Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02760)
*Vasileios Rizeakos,Georgios Paisios,Alexandros Machairas,Michael Birbas,Athanasios Bachoumis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究工厂车间设备上部署问答助手的问题，提出通过结构化压缩和检索增强适配后，模型大小不再能可靠预测适配后的答案质量。因此将部署视为适配后的选择问题，在可配置的通用能力下限和内存预算约束下，基于评判答案质量和实测设备吞吐量为每台设备选择子网络。采用权重共享超网络和夹心式就地蒸馏来保持选择成本低廉。在制造手册案例研究中，提取保留原始模型评判质量的86.3%，检索增强蒸馏后恢复到95.4%，同一助手可在三个异构边缘设备上以1.3-5瓦待机功耗运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工厂工人需要对话式访问机器文档的能力，但具备该能力的模型通常太大，无法在车间硬件上运行。部署需要在通用能力与尺寸、速度等约束间权衡，而仅优化单一指标（大小、速度或质量）都会牺牲其他能力。

Method: 1) 对模型进行结构化压缩和检索接地适配；2) 训练权重共享超网络并采用夹心式就地蒸馏，使子网络选择成本低廉；3) 将部署视为适配后的子网络选择问题，将资源分配表述为在受内存预算与通用能力下限约束下的质量-吞吐量优化问题。

Result: 提取保留了未经剪枝模型评判质量的86.3%，检索接地蒸馏将其恢复到95.4%，挽回三分之二的损失；同一助手可在三个异构边缘设备层以1.3至5瓦待机功耗运行。

Conclusion: 压缩与适配后模型大小不再可靠预测答案质量，需从后适配角度以质量-吞吐量权衡来指导部署决策，使单一助手能灵活部署于异构边缘设备。

Abstract: On-premise assistants can give factory workers conversational access to machine documentation, but models capable of the task rarely fit shop-floor hardware. We show that after structural compression and retrieval-grounded adaptation, model size is no longer a reliable predictor of adapted answer quality: general capability falls almost linearly with parameter count, while judged retrieval-augmented answer quality does not. We therefore treat deployment as a post-adaptation selection problem, committing one sub-network per device on judged answer quality and measured on-device throughput under a configurable general-capability floor and memory budget; rules that optimize size, speed, or quality alone each give up capability or throughput. A weight-shared supernetwork trained with sandwich-style in-place distillation keeps this selection inexpensive. In a manufacturing-manual case study, extraction costs 13.7 percent of the unpruned model's judged quality and retrieval-grounded distillation returns it to within 4.6 percent, recovering two thirds of the loss, and the same assistant runs across three heterogeneous edge tiers at 1.3 to 5 watts standby.

</details>


### [116] [SafeEvolve: Harness-Policy Co-Evolution from Agent Experience for Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02786)
*Qinghua Mao,Wanying Qu,Dadi Guo,Leitao Yuan,Qingyu Liu,Yu Li,Guanxu Chen,Yanwei Fu,Xi Lin,Xia Hu,Dongrui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SafeEvolve是一个经验驱动的自进化框架，通过"护栏-策略"（harness-policy）协同演化实现LLM智能体的安全对齐。它从策略轨迹中提取安全经验，驱动护栏层面（安全提示与分层技能的安全更新）和策略层面（两阶段SFT-RL训练）的持续循环演化，在AgentDojo等基准上显著降低攻击成功率（ASR）同时提升良性效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体的性能由基础模型与交互护栏（harness）共同决定，面临最终有害响应和多步执行轨迹两类安全风险。现有安全对齐机制要么依赖外部护栏更新，要么依赖策略优化，两者孤立应用无法将运行时控制与内在安全有效结合。

Method: SafeEvolve提出护栏-策略协同演化的自进化框架。护栏侧：将轨迹级安全证据转化为有界、组件级的安全提示与分层技能更新（可审计、可回滚）。策略侧：采用两阶段SFT-RL范式——护栏使用SFT引导策略主动利用演化后的护栏组件，护栏增强的RL通过验证器分解奖励塑造多步探索中的自主安全行为。

Result: 在智能体安全基准上，SafeEvolve相比现有基线实现了更强的安全-效用权衡。对于Qwen3.5-4B，在AgentDojo上ASR降低3倍，同时良性效用从59.79%提升至61.86%。

Conclusion: 通过护栏-策略协同演化，SafeEvolve能将安全经验转化为演化的运行时护栏与改进的策略行为，证明协同演化范式优于单一范式，为智能体安全对齐提供了新的可审计、可回滚的实践路径。

Abstract: The performance of LLM-based agents is jointly shaped by the base model and the harness used when interacting with the environment. This exposes them to safety risks in both harmful final responses and multi-step execution trajectories. Existing safety alignment mechanisms often rely on either external harness updates or policy optimization, yet applying either paradigm in isolation fails to bridge runtime control with intrinsic safety. We propose SafeEvolve, an experience-driven self-evolving framework for agent safety alignment. SafeEvolve leverages safety experience from completed on-policy trajectories to drive a continual loop of harness-policy co-evolution. On the harness side, SafeEvolve converts trajectory-level safety evidence into bounded, component-level updates across safety prompt and hierarchical skills, yielding auditable and reversible harness artifacts. On the policy side, SafeEvolve follows a two-stage SFT-RL paradigm, where harness-use SFT bootstraps the policy to actively leverage evolved harness artifacts, and harness-augmented RL further shapes autonomous safety behaviors during multi-step exploration via verifier-decomposed rewards. Through harness-policy co-evolution, SafeEvolve converts safety experience into an evolved runtime harness and improved policy behavior. Experiments on agentic safety benchmarks show that SafeEvolve achieves a stronger safety-utility tradeoff than existing baselines. For Qwen3.5-4B, SafeEvolve achieves a $3\times$ ASR reduction on AgentDojo while improving benign utility from 59.79% to 61.86%.

</details>


### [117] [Large Language Models (LLMs) for Telecom Root Cause Analysis (RCA): A Structured Reasoning Framework for Evidence-Grounded Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02805)
*Hao Zhou,Mandar Kulkarni,Hao Chen,Yan Xin,Charlie,Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首先系统回顾了电信网络中根因分析（RCA）从基于规则和机器学习方法到LLM驱动技术的演进，然后提出了一种结构化推理框架，通过将异构网络遥测数据组织为规范上下文、强制执行决策路径推理、生成基于证据的解释，来解决LLM在电信RCA中出现的幻觉和不稳定推理问题。在两个5G数据集上的实验表明该方法能持续提升诊断准确性和决策一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代5G和新兴6G网络由于存在复杂的跨层依赖性，性能退化诊断仍具挑战性。虽然LLM具有强大的推理和知识整合能力，但直接应用原始LLM到电信RCA中会导致幻觉、推理不稳定以及与结构化网络证据对齐不良等问题。

Method: 提出一个结构化推理框架，包含三个步骤：(1) 将异构网络遥测数据组织成规范上下文；(2) 在诊断过程中强制执行决策路径推理；(3) 生成基于证据的解释以实现可靠的故障识别。

Result: 在两个5G RCA数据集（TeleLogs和TelecomTS）上的实验表明，该框架相比基线技术持续提升了诊断准确性和决策一致性。

Conclusion: 跨数据集的实验结果突显了结构化推理设计对于下一代电信网络中实用的基于LLM的RCA系统的重要性。

Abstract: Root cause analysis (RCA) is a critical task in telecom network operations, but diagnosing performance degradations in modern 5G and emerging 6G networks remains challenging due to complex cross-layer dependencies. While large language models (LLMs) offer promising capabilities for reasoning and knowledge integration, directly applying vanilla LLMs to telecom RCA often leads to hallucination, unstable reasoning, and poor alignment with structured network evidence. This work first reviews the evolution of telecom RCA from rule-based and machine learning (ML) approaches to emerging LLM-enabled techniques, and provides an overview of recent paradigms, including structured reasoning, retrieval-augmented knowledge grounding, agentic orchestration, and verifiable reasoning. Building upon these insights, we propose a structured reasoning framework for LLM-enabled telecom RCA that aligns diagnostic reasoning with telecom-specific evidence and domain knowledge. The proposed approach first organizes heterogeneous network telemetry into canonical contexts, and then enforces decision-path reasoning during diagnosis, and finally generates evidence-grounded explanations for reliable fault identification. Experimental results on two 5G RCA datasets, TeleLogs and TelecomTS, demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently improves diagnostic accuracy and decision consistency compared with baseline techniques. These cross-dataset results highlight the importance of structured reasoning design for practical LLM-based RCA systems in next-generation telecom networks.

</details>


### [118] [AI Contextual Measurement for Recovering Individual and Group-Level Effects: Validation Against Survey Measures and an Occupational Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02821)
*Wenxin Jiang,Xuyang Wang,Yuxiao Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AICOME框架，用于评估AI生成的受访者级测量能否在情境模型（contextual models）中恢复个体和群体层面的效应。通过在2022年中国家庭追踪调查（CFPS）中验证，发现当受访者和职业特征丰富时，AI情境测量能较好地恢复调查变量中的情境模型信息，但存在明确的边界条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者越来越多地使用AI构建传统调查中缺失的社会、组织和职业特征指标，但现有方法仅将AI测量视为"响应预测"，忽视了其能否在情境模型中恢复组间和组内关联。缺乏一个系统的评估框架来检验AI衍生测量在情境模型分析中的有效性。

Method: 提出AICOME（AI情境测量）框架：将受访者级别的AI测量分解为组级聚合值与个体偏差，从而同时估计组间与组内关联。使用2022年CFPS数据进行验证，以职业作为分组结构，针对电脑使用、外语使用、每周工时和管理职责四个变量，在响应级、模型级、情境和边界条件四个层面进行系统验证。

Result: AI情境测量在丰富受访者与职业特征条件下能恢复大部分情境模型信息。每周工时是最强的验证案例，AI测量成功再现了与工作满意度之间的组间和组内负向关联。但边界条件明确：信息受限（仅职业和基本人口统计）时表现下降；多个相关概念同时未观测时恢复效果减弱。

Conclusion: AICOME框架最适合从丰富现有数据集中恢复少量理论上重要的构念，而非穷尽性地重建所有未观测特征。它为评估AI作为情境测量工具的价值提供了系统化方法。

Abstract: Researchers increasingly use artificial intelligence to construct measures of social, organizational, and occupational characteristics that are absent from conventional surveys. We propose AICOME, AI COntextual MEasurement, a framework for evaluating whether AI-derived respondent-level measures can recover individual and group-level effects in contextual models. The key idea is that an AI measure constructed at the respondent level can be used to derive its group-level aggregate and its individual deviation, allowing researchers to estimate both between-group and within-group associations rather than treating AI measurement as response prediction alone.
  We validate the framework using the 2022 China Family Panel Studies (CFPS), where occupations provide the empirical grouping structure and several job-related survey variables provide validation benchmarks. For computer use, foreign-language use, weekly hours, and management responsibilities, we compare survey measures with AI-derived measures in response-level, model-level, contextual, and boundary-condition validations. The results show that AI contextual measurement can recover much of the contextual-model information contained in observed survey variables when rich respondent and job characteristics are available. Weekly hours provides the strongest validation case, with AI-derived measures reproducing the large negative between- and within-occupation associations with satisfaction observed in CFPS. The framework also identifies clear boundary conditions: performance deteriorates when information is restricted to occupation and basic demographics, and recovery is weaker when several related concepts are treated as simultaneously unobserved. The findings suggest that AICOME is most useful for recovering a limited number of theoretically important constructs from rich existing datasets.

</details>


### [119] [Discriminative World Models for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02885)
*Kelvin Li,Dhruv Pendharkar,Anish Pahilajani,Chuyi Shang,Leon Oks,Leonid Karlinsky,Rogerio Feris,Trevor Darrell,Roei Herzig*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的世界模型训练目标——预测状态匹配（predicted-state matching），用于网页智能体。与传统的监督式下一状态预测不同，该方法要求预测的表示能够区分真实结果状态与替代动作导致的状态，从而更好地对齐下游排序器的需求，并显著提升动作排序和端到端任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网页智能体的世界模型通过监督式下一状态预测来训练，生成的表示（如HTML或AXTree快照）与下游排序器的需求不对齐——排序器依赖预测状态在候选项之间具有判别性才能准确评分，而传统训练目标并未优化这一判别性。

Method: 提出预测状态匹配（predicted-state matching）训练目标，要求模型预测的表示能够区分真实动作导致的最终状态与替代动作导致的状态。使用从WebArena Go-Browse轨迹构建的分支网页智能体数据集进行训练，每个决策点包含多个替代动作及其结果状态。

Result: 在预留的预测状态匹配基准上优于监督式下一状态预测的世界模型；在WebPRMBench上相比仅动作PRM和监督式下一状态PRM提升了动作排序性能；在WebArena-Lite上使用该世界模型进行测试时动作选择提升了端到端任务成功率。

Conclusion: 预测状态匹配训练目标能有效对齐世界模型与下游排序器的需求，为网页智能体的测试时动作选择提供了更优的条件，具有实际端到端性能提升。

Abstract: Recent web agents use world models for test-time action selection by sampling candidate actions, predicting the resulting web states, and ranking them with a ranker model or a Process Reward Model (PRM). These world models are typically trained via supervised next-state prediction to generate fixed representations like HTML or AXTree snapshots. However, this objective is misaligned with the downstream ranker, which relies on predicted states being discriminative across candidates to accurately score them. To address this, we introduce predicted-state matching, a training objective where the predicted representation must distinguish the true resulting state from those reached by alternative actions. We train these models using a branching web-agent dataset derived from WebArena Go-Browse trajectories, where every decision point contains multiple alternative actions and their resulting states. Experiments on our held-out predicted-state matching benchmark show that our approach outperforms world models trained with supervised next-state prediction. We further show that our approach improves PRM-style action ranking on WebPRMBench compared with action-only PRMs and PRMs augmented with supervised-next-state world models. Finally, on WebArena-Lite, using our world model for test-time action selection improves end-to-end task success. Our project page is available at: https://dhruvpendharkar.github.io/dwm/.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [Pooling and Drift in Delayed Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01761)
*Melika Baghi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了延迟反馈下的在线决策问题，提出当结果仅取决于动作产生的状态而非动作本身时，学习成本由状态的有效维度决定而非动作数量决定，并给出相应算法与理论界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统设定下，有K个动作且延迟d轮时，最优regret界约为Õ(√((K+d)T))，意味着动作越多学习成本越高。但作者指出：如果结果仅通过动作产生的状态来影响，那么一个迟到的反馈可以更新所有可能产生该状态的动作，学习成本应由不同状态的数量而非动作数量决定。

Method: 引入有效维度v_t（介于1和状态数之间）来衡量问题复杂度；提出两种算法：一种是轮转（rotating）算法，达到Õ(√((d+1)V·log K))的regret；另一种是实践中常用的单副本（single-copy）算法，达到Õ(√(V⁻)+√(dT))的regret。同时考虑通过合并相似状态来降低学习成本（附显式偏置分析）。

Result: 在生成数据上，状态通道相比动作级加权可降低最多79%的regret；在funnel族上，相比调优的极小极大最优方法可降低32%至68%的regret。此外给出下界Ω(√(dE·min{1+log J, T/d}))，其中J为漂移方向数，E为等待期间损失移动量的界。

Conclusion: 当反馈依赖状态而非动作时，延迟学习的价格由状态结构的有效维度决定，而非动作数量决定；即使反馈延迟d轮，仍无法逃脱水下的下界，说明该复杂度刻画是紧的。

Abstract: A system often has to act long before it learns whether the act worked: a recommender sees a click in seconds and a purchase in days. With $K$ actions and a delay of $d$ rounds, the best rate known for this setting is $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{(K+d)T})$ over $T$ rounds, so a longer menu is always more expensive to learn from. It need not be: if the outcome depends on the action only through the state it produced, then one late outcome informs every action that could have produced the observed state, and the price is set by how many genuinely different states the actions produce rather than by how many actions there are. We measure this using an effective dimension $v_t$ between $1$ and the number of states, and prove $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{(d+1)V\log K})$ for a rotating algorithm and $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{V^{-}}+\sqrt{dT})$ for the single-copy algorithm used in practice, for any budget fixed in advance; merging similar states lowers the price further, at an explicit bias. Even when given the exact losses from $d$ rounds ago, no algorithm escapes $Ω(\sqrt{dE\min\{1+\log J,T/d\}})$, where $J$ counts the drifting directions and $E$ bounds how far losses move while the learner waits. On generated data, the state channel cuts regret by up to 79 percent against action-level weighting and, on the funnel family, by 32 to 68 percent against a tuned minimax-optimal method.

</details>


### [121] [Posterior Tempering Explains Variance Inflation in Linear and Generalized Linear Thompson Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01999)
*Prateek Jaiswal,Debdeep Pati,Anirban Bhattacharya,Bani K. Mallick*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种名为 α-TS 的 Thompson Sampling 变体算法，用于解决随机广义线性 bandit 问题。通过引入分数阶后验（α-后验）来实现方差膨胀，并在不依赖后验分布近似的前提下建立了遗憾分析框架。实验表明当 α∝d⁻¹ 时，可获得最优的 O(d^{3/2}√T log T) 遗憾界，并给出了相应的下界证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Thompson Sampling 算法的分析需要通过膨胀后验方差才能获得接近最优的遗憾保证，但缺乏对这种方差膨胀思想的系统化形式化框架，且以往工作往往依赖后验分布的可处理近似。

Method: 定义 α-TS 算法，使用分数或 α-后验替代标准后验；识别先验和奖励分布上的通用正则条件以支持遗憾分析；结合线性 bandit 的最新分析进展与贝叶斯统计中的一阶和二阶后验集中理论。

Result: 当 α∝d⁻¹ 时，对指数族和次高斯奖励分布均获得 O(d^{3/2}√T log T) 的最优已知遗憾界；同时给出 α 依赖的下界 Ω(d^{3/2}√T)，解释了上界中 d^{3/2} 因子的来源。

Conclusion: 该工作正式化了方差膨胀思想，建立的通用分析框架不依赖后验近似，并同时提供了匹配的上界与下界，深刻解释了遗憾界中维度因子的起源，为广义线性 bandit 中 TS 算法的理论分析提供了重要进展。

Abstract: We study a variant of the Thompson Sampling (TS) algorithm, called $α$-TS, for solving stochastic generalized linear bandit problems. Existing analyses of TS require inflating the posterior variance to derive near-optimal regret guarantees. We formalize the idea of variance inflation by introducing $α$-TS that uses a fractional or $α$-posterior instead of the standard posterior. Our main contribution is to identify general regularity conditions on the prior and reward distributions that enable a regret analysis of $α$-TS without assuming any tractable approximation of the posterior distribution, unlike previous works. For a specific choice of $α\propto d^{-1}$, our general regret bound yields the best known regret bound of $O(d^{3/2}\sqrt{T}\log T)$ for both the exponential and sub-Gaussian families of reward distributions. We further provide an $α$-dependent lower bound showing that the regret constant depends on the product $αd$, and that when $α\propto d^{-1}$ the regret scales as $Ω(d^{3/2}\sqrt{T})$, explaining the origin of the $d^{3/2}$ factor in the upper bound. Our proof technique adapts and combines recent advancements in the analysis of linear bandit problems with first- and second-order posterior concentration theory from the Bayesian statistics literature.

</details>


### [122] [HyperMC: Multi-Fidelity Hyperparameter Tuning for Stochastic Gradient MCMC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02138)
*Ming Tan,Xiyun Jiao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出HyperMC，一种多保真度超参数调节框架，结合Hyperband风格的资源分配与核斯坦因散度（KSD）评估，用于随机梯度MCMC（SGMCMC）算法的超参数自动调节。并进一步提出Robust HyperMC，通过全局网格初始化和精英引导的局部细化来增强稳定性。理论分析证明成功选择近优配置的高概率条件，实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SGMCMC方法虽然实现了可扩展的贝叶斯推断，但其性能高度依赖步长、小批量大小和跳蛙步数等超参数。然而，由于大多数SGMCMC算法没有Metropolis-Hastings接受率，标准的基于接受率的调参方法无法直接使用，导致缺乏有效的超参数调节手段。

Method: 1. 提出HyperMC框架：将Hyperband式的连续减半（successive-halving）资源分配与KSD评估相结合，在固定计算预算下平衡连续超参数空间的广泛探索与对候选配置的精确评估。2. 提出Robust HyperMC变体：采用全局网格初始化配合精英引导的局部细化，降低对随机候选生成和有限预算评估噪声的敏感性。3. 提供理论分析：在KSD估计满足适当的近似和集中条件下，证明连续减半组件能以高概率选出近优配置，并推导出成功选择所需的充分计算预算。

Result: 在逻辑回归、概率矩阵分解和贝叶斯神经网络上的实验表明，HyperMC相对于MAMBA、网格搜索和启发式基线，在后验近似或预测校准方面均有改进；Robust HyperMC则产生更稳定、可复现的调节结果。

Conclusion: HyperMC为缺乏接受率的SGMCMC方法提供了一种有效的超参数调节方案，Robust HyperMC进一步增强了调节的稳定性和鲁棒性，理论保证与实验验证共同支撑了方法的实用性。

Abstract: Stochastic gradient Markov chain Monte Carlo (SGMCMC) methods enable scalable Bayesian inference, but their performance depends strongly on hyperparameters such as the step size, mini-batch size, and number of leapfrog steps. Since most SGMCMC algorithms lack a Metropolis-Hastings acceptance rate, standard acceptance-based tuning methods are not directly applicable. We propose HyperMC, a multi-fidelity tuning framework that combines Hyperband-style resource allocation with kernel Stein discrepancy (KSD) evaluation. By running multiple successive-halving brackets, HyperMC balances broad exploration of a continuous hyperparameter space with increasingly accurate evaluation of promising configurations under a fixed computational budget. We further introduce Robust HyperMC, which uses global grid initialization followed by elite-guided local refinement to reduce sensitivity to random candidate generation and noisy finite-budget evaluations. Under suitable approximation and concentration conditions for the estimated KSD, we establish that the successive-halving component selects a near-optimal configuration among the sampled candidates with high probability and derive a sufficient computational budget for successful selection. Experiments on logistic regression, probabilistic matrix factorization, and Bayesian neural networks show that HyperMC improves posterior approximation or predictive calibration relative to MAMBA, grid search, and heuristic baselines, while Robust HyperMC yields more stable and reproducible tuning results.

</details>


### [123] [Schrödinger Bridges on Lie Group Manifolds for Probabilistic Intrinsic Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02196)
*Shizhe Zhang,Mingyang Zhao,Lei Ma*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出在李群流形上的动力学Schrödinger桥生成模型，通过WKBC和RCCBM两种计算方法处理紧致阿贝尔群和非阿贝尔群上的端点约束生成问题，并在蛋白质扭转角、SO(3)、U(n)等多个数据集上验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接在几何流形上进行生成建模可以避免将非欧几里得数据展平所带来的误差（如反复环境投影和坐标不一致问题）。此前Schrödinger桥框架主要针对欧几里得空间，缺乏在李群流形上处理动力学系统的系统方法，特别是当端点观测仅约束部分变量时的条件生成问题。

Method: 本文提出两种计算方法：(1) Wrapped-Kernel Bridge Calibration (WKBC) - 在紧致阿贝尔群上使用显式的周期化动力学核；(2) Reciprocal Conditional-Control Bridge Matching (RCCBM) - 在紧致非阿贝尔群上通过双侧端点校准和磨光条件控制匹配实现。方法基于熵投影确定未观测端点速度的条件律，并利用教师-混合路径律的马尔可夫互反性质，使用校准初始律和学习的Doob控制器进行前向生成。同时建立了bounded-Lipschitz路径度量下的模块化误差界。

Result: 在多个李群流形数据集上进行了实验验证，包括蛋白质和RNA扭转角、SO(3)、U(n)以及基于mdCATH轨迹的蛋白质构象转变路径生成任务，验证了方法的可行性和一致性，代码已公开。

Conclusion: 本文成功地将Schrödinger桥生成框架推广到李群流形上的动力学系统，解决了非欧几里得数据生成中的关键挑战，并提供了理论误差分析和多种流形上的实证验证。

Abstract: Generative modeling directly on geometric manifolds can avoid errors introduced by flattening non-Euclidean data, repeated ambient projection, and coordinate inconsistency in Euclidean representations. Schrodinger bridges provide a probabilistic generative framework for entropy-regularized transport between prescribed endpoint distributions. We study Schrodinger bridges for kinetic dynamics on Lie group manifolds with state X_t = (g_t, xi_t) in G x g, allowing endpoint observations to constrain only the variables that are actually measured. In particular, the entropy projection determines the conditional law of the unobserved endpoint velocities.
  For the same observed endpoint bridge, we develop two computational realizations: Wrapped-Kernel Bridge Calibration (WKBC) uses an explicit periodized kinetic kernel on compact Abelian groups, whereas Reciprocal Conditional-Control Bridge Matching (RCCBM) handles compact non-Abelian groups through two-sided endpoint calibration and mollified conditional-control matching. The canonical teacher-mixture path law is itself a Markov reciprocal law, so forward generation uses a calibrated initial law and one learned Doob controller. Moreover, we establish a modular error bound in the bounded-Lipschitz path metric that provides a clean separation of errors due to endpoints, control regression, initialization, discretization, and related approximations.
  Experiments on multiple Lie group manifold datasets validate the feasibility and consistency of our proposed method, covering protein and RNA torsions, SO(3), U(n), and the Protein Conformational Transition Pathway Generation task using mdCATH trajectories in a compact reduced representation. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/cafferyzhang12/Schr-dinger_Bridge_on_LieGroup.

</details>


### [124] [From topology learning to graph generation: A unifying perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02286)
*Xiaowen Dong,Hoi-To Wai,Siheng Chen,Laura Toni,Dorina Thanou*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 这篇综述论文提出了一个统一框架，将图结构学习（从观测数据推断单个图的拓扑）和图生成（学习图的生成分布以采样新图）这两个看似平行的研究方向联系起来，将它们视为同一图数据生成过程的逆问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图结构学习是信号处理和机器学习中的基础问题，但现有研究沿两条平行路径发展：一条从观测数据推断单个图的拓扑，另一条从观测的图实例学习生成分布。这两条方向缺乏统一的理论框架和跨范式整合。

Method: 提出一个统一框架，将图拓扑推断与图生成建模视为同一图数据生成过程的逆问题，并在此框架下系统回顾主要方法论，分析其关系、优势和局限。

Result: 系统地梳理了该框架内的主要方法论之间的关联，识别了跨范式整合的机会，并提供了跨学科的领域视角。

Conclusion: 通过桥接图拓扑学习与图生成两大范式，该综述为领域提供了更广阔的跨学科视角，并指出了未来研究的方向。

Abstract: Learning graph structures from data is a fundamental problem that spans a wide range of signal processing and machine learning tasks. While significant effort has been made to tackle the problem, existing research has largely evolved along two parallel directions. The first seeks to infer the topology of an individual graph from observations supported on it, whereas the second seeks to learn a generative distribution from observed graph instances, enabling the sampling of new graphs. This review presents a unified framework that connects these formulations by viewing them as inverse problems of a common generation process for graph data. We review the major methodologies within this framework, highlight their relationships, strengths, and limitations, and identify opportunities for integrating ideas across paradigms. By bridging graph topology learning and graph generation, this review provides a broader cross-disciplinary perspective on the field and outlines promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [125] [A computational approach to maximum likelihood thresholds for colored Gaussian graphical models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02382)
*Roser Homs,Olga Kuznetsova,Bernadette J. Stolz*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究着色高斯图模型（CGGMs）的最大似然阈值（MLT）计算问题，建立统一的理论框架并引入新的符号算法，同时利用拓扑数据分析（TDA）来研究充分统计量锥的局部几何，以克服传统Groebner基方法在计算上的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维小样本场景下，高斯图模型（GGMs）的最大似然估计常常因数据不足而无法存在。虽然着色高斯图模型（CGGMs）通过图着色施加对称性约束来降低所需样本量，但仍存在一个最小观测数（即最大似然阈值MLT）需要确定。现有方法在计算MLT方面面临计算瓶颈，亟需新的理论与算法框架。

Method: 本文从几何角度刻画MLT的计算问题：寻找样本协方差矩阵的最小秩，使其投影几乎必然落在充分统计量锥的内部。具体方法包括：(1) 建立统一的理论框架，将无着色模型的结果扩展到着色模型；(2) 引入新的符号算法；(3) 结合采样与拓扑数据分析（TDA）进行计算研究，考察充分统计量锥的局部几何结构。

Result: 实验结果表明，拓扑数据分析（TDA）在分析CGGMs的似然几何方面具有潜力，能够有效克服传统符号代数方法（特别是Groebner基计算）所面临的计算瓶颈。

Conclusion: 本文为着色高斯图模型的最大似然阈值计算提供了统一的理论框架和创新的计算方法，展示了TDA作为替代Groebner基方法在似然几何分析中的有效性，为该领域开辟了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Gaussian graphical models (GGMs) are essential tools for interpretable structure learning. However, in high-dimensional, small-sample regimes, the available data is often insufficient for the maximum likelihood estimator to exist. Colored Gaussian graphical models (CGGMs) mitigate this limitation by imposing symmetry constraints through graph coloring, which reduces the required sample size. This minimal number of observations needed to guarantee that the estimator exists almost surely is defined as the maximum likelihood threshold (MLT). Here, we address the computation of the MLT for CGGMs by focusing on its geometric formulation: finding the minimum rank of a sample covariance matrix such that its projection lies almost surely within the interior of the cone of sufficient statistics. We establish a unified theoretical framework, extending results from uncolored to colored models and introducing new symbolic algorithms. Furthermore, we present a computational study integrating sampling with topological data analysis (TDA) to investigate the local geometry of the cone of sufficient statistics. Our results demonstrate the potential of TDA to overcome the computational bottlenecks of traditional symbolic algebraic methods, particularly Groebner basis computations, in analyzing the likelihood geometry of CGGMs.

</details>


### [126] [Momentum in large-batch training: Polyak enlarges the critical batch size, Nesterov improves data efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02728)
*Jia-Nan Wang,Zixun Huang,Kairui Li,Lei Wu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文在幂律核回归的设定下，系统研究了动量（Polyak和Nesterov）如何以及在多大程度上改进大batch训练（单遍训练）。通过临界学习率刻画训练稳定性，推导完整的风险动力学标度律，并给出三区域的batch-size相图，揭示动量在不同batch规模下的角色变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大batch训练在单遍（one-pass）训练中会损失数据效率，而动量可能缓解这一问题。然而，动量何时有帮助、帮助多少缺乏系统理论理解。作者希望通过理论框架精确刻画动量在大batch训练中的影响及适用场景。

Method: 以幂律核回归作为可解析的设定，推导SGD、Polyak和Nesterov三种方法的临界学习率（$\eta^{\mathrm{crit}}$），给出风险动力学的标度律（早期暂态→幂律衰减→噪声平台），再在固定数据预算下对可容许学习率和动量因子做最小化，得到batch-size相图。

Result: 关键结论包括：Polyak可扩大临界batch size（保持最优数据标度指数），从而在不牺牲数据效率的情况下支持更大并行度；Nesterov通过前瞻机制抑制噪声累积，在大batch区域实现更好的数据效率。数值实验验证了稳定性边界、风险动力学和相图的预测。

Conclusion: 动量对batch训练的改进取决于batch size所在的区域：Polyak适合扩展到更大batch而不损失数据效率，Nesterov则在真正的大batch区域表现更优，两者互补，为实际选择优化器提供了明确的指导原则。

Abstract: We study when and how momentum improves large-batch training in the one-pass regime, using power-law kernel regression as a tractable setting. We first characterize risk stability through the critical learning rate, defined as the largest learning rate for stable training, and obtain $η_{\mathrm{SGD}}^{\mathrm{crit}}\eqsim 1$, $η_{\mathrm{Polyak}}^{\mathrm{crit}}\eqsim \min\{1,B(1-ρ)\}$, and $η_{\mathrm{Nesterov}}^{\mathrm{crit}}\eqsim \min\{1,B^β(1-ρ)\}$, where $B$ is the batch size, $ρ$ is the momentum factor, and $β>1$ is the capacity exponent. Within this admissible region, we derive scaling laws for the full risk dynamics, capturing the progression from an early transient, through power-law decay, to a noise floor. We then minimize the final-step risk over the admissible learning rates and momentum factors under a fixed data budget, yielding a three-regime batch-size phase diagram that reveals how the role of momentum changes with batch size. Notably, Polyak enlarges the critical batch size, the largest batch size preserving the best small-batch data-scaling exponent, thereby enabling greater parallelism without sacrificing data efficiency. In contrast, Nesterov achieves better data efficiency in the large-batch regime because its look-ahead mechanism suppresses noise accumulation. Numerical experiments validate the predicted stability boundaries, risk dynamics, and batch-size phase diagram.

</details>


### [127] [Full-Model Optimality for Tunable Linear Generative Priors in Compressed Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02790)
*Zhaoming Li,Paul Hand*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了压缩感知中使用可调复杂度生成先验的理论性质。与Gunn等人的实验发现（调节生成先验的复杂度可以降低重建误差）不同，本文证明在无噪声高斯压缩感知中，使用线性生成先验族时，全维线性先验在期望重建误差上达到最优，即降低先验复杂度不会改善重建误差。这表明实验中观察到的可调性益处源于神经网络生成模型的非线性特性，而非线性先验本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型已被广泛用作逆问题（如压缩感知）的先验。最近Gunn等人的工作引入了可调复杂度的生成先验族，实验表明在多种逆问题中通过适当调节先验复杂度可以获得更低的重建误差。本文旨在从理论上理解这一现象，探究可调复杂度先验在压缩感知中的理论保证，以解释实验观察与理论之间的差距。

Method: 本文针对可通过奇异值分解自然关联的线性生成先验族，建立压缩感知的理论分析。具体地，作者在无噪声高斯压缩感知的设定下，分析整个线性先验家族中各成员（从低维到全维）的期望重建误差，并通过理论推导比较不同复杂度先验的表现。

Result: 理论结果表明，在无噪声高斯压缩感知中，完整的（全维）线性先验在该线性先验族中取得最小的期望重建误差。这意味着降低复杂度不仅不会改善重建性能，反而可能因信息损失而增加误差。该结果与去噪任务形成对比——在去噪中，低复杂度先验因偏差-方差权衡而表现更优。

Conclusion: 本文的结论表明，Gunn等人实验观察到的可调复杂度生成先验在压缩感知中的益处，并非来自线性先验本身的可调性，而是源自神经网络生成模型中的非线性结构。这为理解可调生成先验的理论来源提供了重要洞见，指出非线性性是解释观测到性能提升的关键因素。

Abstract: Generative models have been studied experimentally and theoretically as priors for inverse problems such as compressed sensing. Recent work by Gunn et al. studied the use of generative priors with tunable complexity, where a family of generative priors with varying complexity is maintained and a specific complexity can be selected at inversion time. They demonstrated that lower reconstruction errors can be experimentally attained for a variety of inverse problems by appropriately tuning the complexity of the generative prior. In the present paper, we establish theory for compressed sensing in the setting of a tunable family of linear generative priors naturally related through their singular value decompositions. We prove that in noiseless Gaussian compressed sensing, the full-dimensional linear prior attains the minimum expected reconstruction error over the entire family of linear priors. Thus, in this idealized linear noiseless setting, tuning to a lower-complexity prior does not improve the expected reconstruction error. This result is in contract to the behavior of denoising, where lower complexity priors attain lower reconstruction errors due to a standard bias-variance tradeoff. This result indicates that the experimental benefits of tunability in compressed sensing with neural network priors arises due to nonlinearities in the generative models.

</details>


### [128] [Copula Transformations for Data-Consistent Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02832)
*Troy Butler,Tianyi Jiang,João Silva,Harri Hakula,Timothy Wildey*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过copula理论建立了迭代数据一致反演（iDCI）与联合数据一致反演（DCI）之间的理论联系，证明了iDCI收敛后残差完全由观测与预测联合分布的copula刻画，并提出copula变换的iDCI解可精确恢复原始DCI解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管iDCI通过顺序施加多个推前约束避免了高维联合密度的直接逼近，但其与原始联合DCI解之间的关系一直不明确，这限制了iDCI的理论理解与应用可靠性。

Method: 利用Sklar定理将DCI更新分解为独立的边际变换与依赖结构（copula）变换两部分；证明iDCI收敛后残差完全由观测与预测联合分布的copula刻画；提出copula变换的iDCI解并证明精确copula变换可恢复原始DCI解；建立参考测度收敛序列和可行集渐进丰富条件下近似copula变换的收敛性结果。

Result: 数值实验表明QoI映射的几何结构决定了copula变换的重要性；展示了自适应参考测度细化策略在固定采样预算下提高计算精度；演示了通过异构异步采集实验逐步细化广义随机逆问题求解。

Conclusion: 研究填补了iDCI与联合DCI之间的理论空白，证明了copula变换是衔接两者的关键桥梁，为广义随机逆问题提供了更完备的理论基础与实用算法指导。

Abstract: Data-consistent inversion (DCI) constructs probability measures whose push-forward distributions agree with observed data, while iterative data-consistent inversion (iDCI) extends this framework to generalized stochastic inverse problems by enforcing multiple push-forward constraints sequentially. Although iDCI avoids the direct approximation of high-dimensional joint densities, its relationship to the original joint DCI solution has remained unclear. In this work, we establish this relationship through copula theory. Using Sklar's theorem, we derive a factorization of the DCI update into separate marginal and dependence transformations and show that the discrepancy remaining after convergence of the iDCI algorithm is entirely characterized by the copulas associated with the observed and predicted joint distributions. This characterization motivates a copula-transformed iDCI solution, and we prove that an exact copula transformation recovers the original DCI solution. We further establish convergence results for approximate copula transformations under converging sequences of reference measures and progressively enriched feasible sets. Numerical examples demonstrate how the geometry induced by the quantity-of-interest map governs the importance of the copula transformation, illustrate an adaptive reference-measure refinement strategy for improving computational accuracy under a fixed sampling budget, and demonstrate the progressive refinement of generalized stochastic inverse problems through heterogeneous, asynchronously acquired experiments.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [WMLLM: Self-Evolving Optimization Agents via Predict-Then-Act World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01608)
*Zhongzheng Li,Qingsong Ran,Shikun Feng,Nian Ran,Wenhao Li,Xiaoyuan Zhang,Yue Wang,Xiaoguang Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WMLLM，一个基于"先预测后行动"世界建模的自进化优化智能体框架，用于解决黑箱优化问题中样本效率低下的挑战。该框架利用大语言模型隐式知识预测有前景的优化方向，再生成候选解，并结合多轮迭代改进、种群搜索和强化学习，在优化过程中同时改进隐式世界模型和优化策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑箱优化问题因搜索空间大、结构弱、维度高而具有挑战性。现有方法依赖直接候选生成或试错式改进，导致样本效率低下。作者注意到LLM凭借隐式知识可以较准确地预测候选解的结果，因此提出利用世界建模来帮助在昂贵评估前识别有前景的优化方向，以提升搜索效率。

Method: WMLLM框架包含两个核心阶段：(1) 预测阶段——智能体先预测有前景的优化方向；(2) 行动阶段——根据预测生成候选解。框架整合了智能体多轮细化、基于种群的搜索和强化学习三大机制，使智能体在搜索过程中不断自我进化，同时优化其隐式世界模型（预测能力）和优化策略（生成能力）。

Result: 在多种黑箱优化任务（尤其是多目标分子优化）上的实验表明，WMLLM显著提升了样本效率和最终优化性能。在多目标分子优化基准测试中，在有限评估预算下取得了最先进的结果。

Conclusion: 通过将LLM的隐式知识与世界建模思路结合，采用"预测-行动"协同的自进化机制，能够有效克服传统黑箱优化方法的样本效率瓶颈。该方法在多目标分子优化等实际任务中展现出明显优势，验证了LLM驱动的世界建模在优化领域的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Black-box optimization problems remain challenging because of large, weakly structured, and high-dimensional search spaces. Existing methods often suffer from poor sample efficiency because they rely on direct candidate generation or trial-and-error refinement. A natural way to improve search efficiency is to use world modeling, which can help identify promising optimization directions before costly evaluation. Large language models can predict the outcomes of these candidates with nontrivial accuracy because of their implicit knowledge. Motivated by this observation, we propose WMLLM, a self-evolving optimization-agent framework based on predict-then-act world modeling. The agent first predicts promising directions and then acts to generate candidates. Combined with agentic multi-turn refinement, population-based search, and reinforcement learning, WMLLM refines both its implicit world model and its optimization strategy during search. Experiments on black-box optimization tasks, especially multi-objective molecular optimization, show that WMLLM improves sample efficiency and final optimization performance. On the multi-objective molecular optimization benchmark, WMLLM achieves state-of-the-art results under a limited evaluation budget.

</details>


### [130] [DiDrive: A Risk-Aware Hierarchical Diffusion Framework for Safe Offline Reinforcement Learning in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01609)
*Qisong Guo,Jingtang Chen,Zhilin Chen,Pei Xu,Mingjian Fu,Wenxi Liu,Yuanlong Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DiDrive，一个分布引导的离线扩散框架，用于自动驾驶决策，包含风险感知层级扩散（RHDif）架构和3DICE策略优化范式两个协同组件，在CARLA基准测试中取得85%成功率和4295.68平均奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型虽能有效捕获自动驾驶的多模态行为先验，但离线强化学习策略仍面临分布偏移、重尾风险信号、分布外（OOD）动作生成和高维状态冗余等挑战。

Method: DiDrive包含两个协同组件：1）状态空间中，RHDif采用低层风险门控编码器和高层上下文调制器，过滤环境冗余并聚焦安全关键威胁；2）动作空间中，3DICE通过样本内校准引导、时空优化和集成候选排序来缓解OOD过估计和梯度振荡问题。

Result: 在CARLA基准测试中，DiDrive优于IQL、CQL和Diffusion-QL等基线方法，尤其在60辆车辆的高密度交通场景中表现突出，实现了85%的成功率和4295.68的平均奖励。

Conclusion: DiDrive为安全自动驾驶决策提供了一条稳健的路径，通过分布引导的离线扩散框架有效解决了高风险场景中的策略安全问题。

Abstract: While diffusion models effectively capture multimodal behavioral priors for autonomous driving, offline reinforcement learning (RL) policies remain susceptible to distribution shift, heavy-tailed risk signals, out-of-distribution (OOD) action generation, and high-dimensional state redundancy. To address these challenges, we propose DiDrive, a distribution-guided offline diffusion framework featuring two synergistic components: the Risk-Aware Hierarchical Diffusion (RHDif) architecture and the 3DICE policy optimization paradigm. In the state space, RHDif utilizes a low-level risk-gated encoder and a high-level contextual modulator to filter environmental redundancy and focus on safety-critical threats. In the action space, 3DICE mitigates OOD overestimation and gradient oscillation through in-sample calibrated guidance, spatiotemporal optimization, and ensemble-based candidate ranking. Evaluations on the CARLA benchmark demonstrate DiDrive's superiority over baselines like IQL, CQL, and Diffusion-QL, particularly in complex, high-density traffic scenarios with 60 vehicles, where it achieves an 85% success rate and a 4295.68 average reward, providing a robust pathway for safe autonomous driving decision-making.

</details>


### [131] [Prompt-Space Meta-Learning Does Not Transfer Across Users: A Frozen-LLM Negative Result](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01615)
*Liam Byrne,David Dylan,Orla Fitzgerald,Eoin Doyle,Ciara Nolan,Padraig Lynch,Sinead Gallagher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过Muse框架研究冻结LLM用户个性化中的元学习目标是否真正编码跨用户可迁移的适应能力。结果发现Muse在两项基准上未显著优于其种子提示或结构破坏控制组，并被简单少样本检索超越。作者将其归因于"元目标坍缩"——元验证目标对用户-支持匹配是否真实具有统计不变性，无法优化出可迁移的适应，只能奖励提示润色和验证过拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化冻结LLM常被框定为提示空间的元学习问题（每个用户一个任务，学习共享的适应策略），这种框架看似吸引人且与骨干无关，但领域很少检验优化后的元目标是否编码可迁移的跨用户适应，而非仅仅是通用指令质量。本文旨在严格检验这一假设。

Method: 提出Muse框架：通过反思式提示进化在元训练用户群上进化单一共享适应提示，冻结后零样本应用于未见用户。同时设计匹配控制组以隔离学习效应与措辞和选择混淆因素，包括未进化的种子提示、结构破坏控制（在错配的用户-支持对上元训练）以及不变性预言机控制。

Result: 在LaMP-2（分类）和LaMP-3（评分）两个基准、各200个未见用户上，Muse未显著优于其未进化的种子提示或错配支持控制组，且在评分任务上被简单少样本检索显著超越（Delta MAE +0.175, p<0.001）。统计分析显示元验证目标在LaMP-2（p=0.555）和LaMP-3（p=0.622）上对用户-支持对应关系的真实性均统计不变。

Conclusion: 元目标坍缩是导致元学习个性化失效的统一机制：当前元验证目标无法区分真实与虚假的用户-支持对应，因此无法被优化成可迁移的适应策略。种子提示、错误支持和不变性控制组构成一个可复用的协议，用于将学习到的适应与混淆因素分离。

Abstract: Personalizing a frozen large language model (LLM) to individual users is often framed as a meta-learning problem in prompt space: each user is a task, and one seeks a shared natural-language adaptation policy that, given a handful of the user's labeled interactions, configures the frozen model for that user. The framing is attractive because it is backbone-agnostic and reuses the machinery of prompt optimization, yet the field rarely tests whether the optimized meta-objective encodes transferable cross-user adaptation rather than generic instruction quality. We study this question with Muse (Meta-learned User-adaptation via Shared Evolution), which evolves a single shared adaptation prompt over a meta-train user population by reflective prompt evolution, freezes it, and applies it zero-shot to held-out users; matched controls isolate learning from confounds of phrasing and selection. On two standard personalization benchmarks (LaMP-2 categorization and LaMP-3 rating) over 200 held-out users each, Muse does not significantly improve on its own un-evolved seed prompt or on a structure-broken control that meta-trains on mismatched user-support pairs, and is dominated by plain few-shot retrieval on the rating task (Delta MAE +0.175, p < 0.001). We attribute these outcomes to a single mechanism, meta-objective collapse: the meta-validation objective is statistically invariant to whether the user-support correspondence is genuine (p=0.555 on LaMP-2, p=0.622 on LaMP-3), so it cannot be optimized into transferable adaptation and instead rewards instruction polish and validation overfitting. The seed-prompt, wrong-support, and invariance-oracle controls form a reusable protocol that separates learned adaptation from these confounds.

</details>


### [132] [Efficient Context-Limited Telescope Bibliography Classification for the WASP-2025 Shared Task Using SciBERT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01647)
*Madhusudhana Naidu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于SciBERT的高效方法，用于自动将科学论文分类为science、instrumentation、mention和not telescope四类，用于望远镜书目创建。在WASP-2025排行榜上以0.89的macro F1分数位居榜首，尽管受到512 token上下文长度限制和有限计算资源的约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 望远镜书目的创建对于评估天文台科学影响和确保天文学可重复性至关重要，但这一过程目前主要依赖人工且资源密集。需要开发自动化的方法来分类和识别使用特定望远镜的科学出版物，以提高效率和准确性。

Method: 采用基于SciBERT的自动分类方法，将科学论文分为science、instrumentation、mention和not telescope四类。在最大512 token的严格上下文长度限制下进行训练和推理，分析了截断对分类效果的影响，并探讨了截断、分块和长上下文模型之间的权衡。

Result: 该方法在WASP-2025排行榜上以0.89的macro F1分数排名第一。分析表明，即使超过半数样本超出token限制，SciBERT的领域对齐能力仍能实现稳健的分类效果。

Conclusion: SciBERT的领域预训练使得即使在严格长度限制和截断条件下也能实现高性能分类，为科学文本策展的效率前沿提供了见解，展示了在有限计算资源下进行自动化望远镜书目创建的可能性。

Abstract: The creation of telescope bibliographies is a crucial part of assessing the scientific impact of observatories and ensuring reproducibility in astronomy. This task involves identifying, categorizing, and linking scientific publications that reference or use specific telescopes. However, this process remains largely manual and resource intensive. In this work, we present an efficient SciBERT-based approach for automatic classification of scientific papers into four categories - science, instrumentation, mention, and not telescope. Despite strict context-length constraints (maximum 512 tokens) and limited compute resources, our approach achieved a macro F1 score of 0.89, ranking at the top of the WASP-2025 leaderboard. We analyze the effect of truncation and show that even with half the samples exceeding the token limit, SciBERT's domain alignment enables robust classification. We discuss trade-offs between truncation, chunking, and long-context models, providing insights into the efficiency frontier for scientific text curation.

</details>


### [133] [CliffRank: A Dual-Branch Framework for Activity-Cliff Ranking Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01673)
*Kewei Li,Rongying Zhang,Peiyu Yang,Zhongjian Wang,Qiuchen Zhao,Lan Huang,Fengfeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CliffRank方法，通过结合绝对活性回归与排序一致性学习来解决活性悬崖（activity-cliff）排序难题，在两个领域的多个数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活性悬崖排序困难，因为局部结构变化可导致大的活性差异，而能解析其潜在机制的高质量数据有限。作者希望更有效地利用现有的活性标签来提升排序性能。

Method: CliffRank训练两个并行预测器，结合三种损失：均方误差（绝对活性回归）、阈值化列表损失（thresholded listwise loss）以及成对偏好一致性（PPC），PPC在偏好概率空间中对齐相对排序。

Result: 在三个抗菌肽数据集上，CliffRank+ESM2-t12取得最高平均Spearman相关系数0.5393和平均Recall@50为21.4；在三个小分子数据集上，CliffRank+PNA（PPC在120个epoch后激活）取得最高平均Spearman相关系数0.6890，平均Recall@50为30.4与ACANet-PNA持平。

Conclusion: PPC结果也定义了其实践局限：异步初始化提升MolCLR-GIN平均值但非所有目标；对无预训练权重的PNA，延迟PPC仅改善部分指标；未来应评估更多靶点与抗菌肽系统，开发自适应PPC调度，并纳入蛋白质或膜上下文信息。

Abstract: Activity-cliff ranking remains difficult because local structural changes can cause large activity differences, while high-quality data that resolve the underlying mechanisms remain limited. To use available activity labels more effectively, we combine absolute-activity regression with ranking-consistency learning. CliffRank trains two parallel predictors with mean squared error, a thresholded listwise loss, and Pairwise Preference Consistency (PPC), which aligns relative ordering in the preference-probability space. On three antimicrobial peptide datasets, CliffRank with ESM2-t12 achieved the highest mean Spearman correlation of 0.5393 and mean Recall@50 of 21.4, although the leading method varied across individual datasets. On three small-molecule datasets, CliffRank with PNA, where PPC was activated after 120 epochs, achieved the highest mean Spearman correlation of 0.6890, while its mean Recall@50 of 30.4 matched that of ACANet-PNA. The PPC results also define its practical limits. Asymmetric initialization improved the MolCLR-GIN averages but did not improve every target. For PNA without pretrained weights, delayed PPC improved selected metrics, but no schedule was best for both mean Spearman correlation and mean Recall@50. Future work should evaluate more targets and antimicrobial peptide systems, develop adaptive PPC schedules, and incorporate protein or membrane context when available.

</details>


### [134] [Sim2Signal: Sim-to-Real Benchmarks for Traffic Signal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01676)
