*Ferdous Al Rafi,Susrik Mukherjee,Latika Liladhar Dekate,Jennifer Yawa Lavoe,Huaiyuan Yao,Shlok Mohanty,Longchao Da,Xuesong Zhou,Hua Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Sim2Signal基准，系统性地将交通信号控制中的Sim-to-Real差距分解为观测、动作、转移和奖励四种间隙（对应MDP四个组成部分），并在33种间隙设置、10个校准网络和5个真实场景上评估了18种缓解方法，发现直接迁移会导致性能全面下降，但缓解方法的有效性严重依赖网络和间隙设置，最有效的方法通常是估计间隙带来的变化而非使策略对间隙不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在交通信号控制模拟中表现优异，但实际部署时因Sim-to-Real差距而失效。目前对这一差距各来源（传感、动作执行、交通动态、控制目标）的相对影响缺乏理解，也缺乏标准基准来系统测量差距并评估现有缓解方法的可靠性。

Method: 将Sim-to-Real差距分解为观测、动作、转移和奖励四种间隙（对应MDP中四个组成部分），在共享协议下隔离诱导每种间隙；基于5个真实地点的数据构建10个校准网络，在2个基础控制器上评估18种缓解方法，覆盖33种间隙设置。

Result: 直接迁移在全部四种间隙源上都持续降低性能，但退化严重程度不能预测缓解方法的有效性；缓解效果强烈依赖网络和间隙设置——除动作间隙外，某种方法在一个场景有效可能在另一场景失效；有效方法大多通过估计间隙变化来实现，而非依赖域随机化或不变表示。

Conclusion: Sim-to-Real差距在交通信号控制中是一个多维问题，缓解方法需要针对具体间隙源和网络场景来选择和设计；未来工作应关注估计间隙变化的鲁棒方法，而非追求与间隙无关的通用策略。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning achieves strong traffic signal control performance in simulation, yet policies trained in simulators often fail once deployed in the real world, a failure known as the Sim-to-Real gap. When RL is applied to traffic signal control, this gap arises from several sources: sensing, action execution, traffic dynamics, and the control objective. Their relative impact and the reliability of existing Sim-to-Real mitigation methods remain insufficiently understood, and the field lacks a standard benchmark for systematically measuring the gap and evaluating mitigation methods. We present Sim2Signal, a benchmark that decomposes the Sim-to-Real gap into observation, action, transition, and reward gaps, corresponding to mismatches in the four components of the underlying MDP, and induces each gap in isolation under a shared protocol. We evaluate 18 mitigation methods on 2 base controllers, across 33 gap settings and 10 calibrated networks built from 5 real-world locations. We find that direct transfer consistently degrades performance across all four gap sources, but the severity of the degradation does not predict the effectiveness of mitigation. Instead, mitigation effectiveness depends strongly on the network and gap setting: outside the action gap, a method that helps in one case may fail in another. The most effective methods generally estimate what the gap changes, rather than make the policy insensitive through domain randomization or invariant representations. Our code is available at https://github.com/Red-Pheonix/Sim2RealTSCBenchMark

</details>


### [135] [A Survey on Self-Improving Test-Time Intelligence: Feedback-Driven Adapting, Learning, and Scaling at Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01679)
*Shuaicheng Niu,Guohao Chen,Yaofo Chen,Zhiquan Wen,Jinwu Hu,Zeshuai Deng,Deyu Chen,Shuhai Zhang,Renjie Chen,Zihao Lian,Shoukai Xu,Gang Dai,Yunbei Zhang,Wei Luo,Yifan Zhang,Mingkui Tan,Cheng Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文献综述提出"反馈驱动的测试时智能（TTI）"作为统一视角，将测试时自适应、测试时学习和测试时扩展这三类部署期模型改进方法整合起来，以弥合不同研究社区之间的术语隔阂，并建立自我改进AI系统的概念基础与研究路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统在部署期间自我改进的能力日益重要，但相关研究沿两个方向分散发展：一是通过测试时信号修改模型状态（测试时自适应/学习），二是通过额外推理资源改进预测（测试时扩展/工具使用）。这些方向由不同社区研究、使用不同术语，导致它们之间的内在联系和日益增长的交叉难以被看清，缺乏统一视角。

Method: 提出反馈驱动的"测试时智能（TTI）"作为统一框架，在此框架下关联三大范式：测试时自适应（TTA）、测试时学习（TTL）和测试时扩展（TTS）；阐明三者之间的区别、联系以及在混合系统中的重叠；并系统综述主要的方法论范式、代表性应用以及跨视觉、语言、多模态、生成模型、机器人学和医疗健康领域的开放挑战。

Result: 成功构建一个统一的TTI概念框架，将以往碎片化的研究思想连接起来，理清了测试时自适应、测试时学习与测试时扩展之间的边界与交叉，并给出了覆盖多个领域的综述及研究路线图。

Conclusion: TTI统一框架为研究部署期（测试时）自我改进的AI系统提供了更清晰的概念基础和系统性的研究路线图，有助于推动该方向在未来形成更连贯、更具协作性的研究社区。

Abstract: The ability of AI systems to improve their behavior during deployment is becoming increasingly important. As inference moves beyond the static execution of a fixed trained model, a growing body of work studies how models can refine their behavior on the fly by exploiting test-time information and additional computation. These developments have largely evolved along two directions: methods that modify the model's state using test-time signals, and methods that improve predictions through extra inference-time resources such as more sampling and tool use. However, these directions are often studied in separate communities with different terminology, making their connections harder to see. In this survey, we present feedback-driven Test-Time Intelligence (TTI) as a unified perspective for understanding such deployment-time improvement. We use this view to relate test-time adaptation, test-time learning, and test-time scaling, highlighting both their distinctions and their growing overlap in hybrid systems. This unified framework helps connect previously fragmented ideas and provides a clearer conceptual foundation for studying inference-time self-improvement. We review major methodological paradigms, representative applications, and open challenges across vision, language, multimodal learning, generative models, robotics, and healthcare. Our goal is to provide a coherent foundation and research roadmap for the study of self-improving AI systems at test time.

</details>


### [136] [Reinforcement Learning and Rule-Based Peer-to-Peer Pricing in Residential PV-BES Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01680)
*Pablo Benalcazar,Maciej Kalka,Wilian Guamán,Jacek Kamiński*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了基于规则和基于学习的P2P电力交易定价机制。在纯光伏配置下，基于规则的基准方法优于最佳RL策略；加入电池储能后，最优RL策略的社区节省从EUR 734.23提升至EUR 978.52。SDR形状定价在所有学习模式中表现优于乘数法参数化，但基于规则的方法在直接比较中仍极具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 住宅光伏社区的P2P电力交易需要有效的定价机制，但现有研究缺乏对基于规则和基于学习（强化学习）定价机制的系统性比较，因此需要评估哪种方法能带来更高的社区经济效益。

Method: 比较三类基于规则的定价基准（账单分摊、中间市场价、供需比定价）与基于深度Q网络的强化学习定价；RL在乘数型和可学习的SDR形状定价下评估，设固定参数SDR为非学习对照组；通过社区节省及财务运营指标评估，并分别在纯光伏和含电池储能配置下测试。

Result: 纯光伏配置下规则基准优于最佳RL策略；加入储能后最佳RL策略社区节省从EUR 734.23增至EUR 978.52；两种配置下SDR形状定价均优于乘数型参数化；储能显著改善学习型结果，但家庭间收益分配不均。

Conclusion: 基于规则的定价机制在直接比较中仍保持高度竞争力；电池储能能大幅提升基于学习的定价效果，但需关注家庭间收益分配的异质性问题。

Abstract: This paper compares rule-based and learning-based pricing mechanisms for peer-to-peer (P2P) electricity trading in residential photovoltaic communities. The rule-based benchmarks comprise bill-sharing as an ex post allocation mechanism, the mid-market rate, and supply-demand-ratio pricing. The reinforcement-learning (RL) formulation is implemented through a Deep Q-Network and evaluated under multiplier-based and learnable SDR-shaped pricing, with a fixed-parameter SDR variant as a non-learning control. Performance is assessed through community savings together with complementary financial and operational indicators. In the base PV-only configuration, the rule-based benchmarks outperform the best RL policy. With battery energy storage, evaluated for the RL policies only, community savings under the best RL policy increase from EUR 734.23 to EUR 978.52. Across the learning-based modes and in both configurations, SDR-shaped pricing outperforms the multiplier-based parameterization considered. The results indicate that rule-based pricing remains highly competitive wherever the two families are compared directly, and that storage substantially improves the learning-based outcomes under this accounting, while the distribution of benefits remains heterogeneous across households.

</details>


### [137] [Median-of-Means as an Extremal Convex Estimator and a Nonconvex Route to the Trimmed Oracle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01689)
*Angshul Majumdar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从确定性优化视角重访中位数均值估计，提出一族block-Lp估计器用于重尾数据和对抗性污染数据的鲁棒学习。在分块污染模型下证明了凸block M-估计器的最坏情况鲁棒常数下界，并提出非凸block-Lp族（p在0到1之间）可逼近修剪分块oracle常数，同时具有良好景观性质，结合分块级集中可获得次高斯偏差界及高维扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典中位数均值估计（median-of-means）在鲁棒学习中表现优异，但存在修剪分块oracle常数与凸类估计器可实现常数之间的gap。作者希望从确定性优化角度理解这一差距，并设计能逼近oracle最优性的非凸估计器族，同时保证优化景观的可处理性。

Method: 1) 证明所有凸block M-估计器的最坏情况鲁棒常数至少为1/(1-2ε)，匹配经典中位数均值界限，说明修剪oracle常数1/(1-ε)在凸类内不可达。2) 引入非凸block-Lp估计器族（p∈(0,1)），对所有全局最小化解导出有限样本确定性鲁棒界。3) 证明当p从1降至0时，界连续逼近修剪oracle常数；对充分小的p，在温和分离条件下全局最小化解与oracle解重合。4) 验证block-Lp目标函数具有良性景观（所有局部极小值接近真值，无坏吸引盆）。5) 结合分块级集中获得有限2+δ阶矩下的次高斯偏差界，并扩展到鲁棒均值估计和稀疏回归的高维场景。

Result: 凸block M-估计器的最坏情况鲁棒常数下界为1/(1-2ε)，凸类无法达到修剪oracle常数；非凸block-Lp族通过调节p可在全局最小化解上连续逼近oracle常数，且充分小的p下全局最小化与oracle解一致；该目标函数景观良性，无坏局部极小；在2+δ阶矩条件下实现次高斯偏差，并成功推广至高维鲁棒均值估计和稀疏回归。

Conclusion: 本文确立了凸性在鲁棒分块估计中的本质限制，并通过非凸block-Lp框架弥合了凸估计器与修剪oracle之间的理论gap，同时保证了优化可行性。该工作为鲁棒统计估计提供了确定性优化视角的完整理论，展示了非凸方法在鲁棒学习中的优势与可行性。

Abstract: We revisit median-of-means estimation from a deterministic optimization viewpoint and develop a family of block-Lp estimators for robust learning with heavy-tailed and adversarially corrupted data. In a block contamination model with at least a fraction 1 minus epsilon of good blocks, we first show that every convex block M-estimator has worst-case robustness constant at least 1 divided by 1 minus 2 epsilon. This matches the classical median-of-means bound and proves that the trimmed-block oracle constant 1 divided by 1 minus epsilon cannot be attained within the convex class. We then introduce a nonconvex block-Lp family for p between 0 and 1 and derive finite-sample deterministic robustness bounds for all global minimizers. As p decreases from 1 toward 0, these bounds continuously approach the trimmed-block oracle constant. For sufficiently small p, the global minimizers coincide with those of the oracle under a mild separation condition. We also show that the block-Lp objectives have a benign landscape, with all local minima remaining close to the truth and no bad basins. Combining these results with block-level concentration yields sub-Gaussian deviation bounds under finite 2 plus delta moments and high-dimensional extensions to robust mean estimation and sparse regression.

</details>


### [138] [Tri-Band Channel Measurement-Enabled Multi-Layer Digital Twin for Terahertz Wireless Data Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01699)
*Mingjie Zhu,Ziming Yu,Guangjian Wang,Chong Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向太赫兹无线数据中心的多层数字孪生框架，包含物理层、信道层、评估层和操作用户层。基于140/220/300 GHz三频段实测数据构建校准物理孪生，并开发了基于LoS感知隐式神经场的AI信道孪生实现实时信道重建，最终通过系统级评估验证了天花板AP部署在10 dB SINR阈值下可达到90%以上覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI计算的快速增长对数据中心互连提出了高容量和灵活性的需求，太赫兹通信凭借超宽带宽和高空间复用能力成为未来无线数据中心的候选方案，而数字孪生技术可实现高效无线规划和实时优化。

Method: 提出测量驱动的多层DT框架，自底向上构建四个层次：1) 基于140/220/300 GHz三频段实测数据，联合优化几何、材料、天线和混合传播模型构建测量校准的物理孪生；2) 在物理孪生之上，开发LoS感知隐式神经场构建AI信道孪生，从校准孪生中学习位置相关的信道统计量，实现接收功率和LoS概率的实时预测；3) 构建系统级评估层分析AP-to-rack和rack-to-rack通信的覆盖与干扰。

Result: 实验表明，提出的AI孪生在功率重建误差上优于现有神经场基线方法，同时保持实时推理能力；天花板安装的AP部署在10 dB SINR阈值下实现超过90%的覆盖率。

Conclusion: 该数字孪生框架有效支持太赫兹无线数据中心的规划和优化，验证了测量驱动多层DT方法在无线数据中心场景中的可行性。

Abstract: The rapid growth of AI computing has driven increasing demands for flexible and high-capacity data-center interconnections. Owing to its ultra-wide bandwidth and high spatial reuse capability, terahertz (THz) communication has emerged as a promising solution for future wireless data centers, while digital twins (DTs) enable efficient wireless planning and real-time optimization. In this work, a measurement-driven multi-layer DT framework is proposed for THz wireless data centers, where the physical, channel, evaluation, and manipulation layers are progressively constructed from bottom to top. First, extensive channel measurements are conducted at 140, 220, and 300 GHz to characterize frequency-dependent propagation behaviors. Based on the tri-band measurements, a measurement-calibrated physical twin is established by jointly optimizing the geometry, material, antenna, and hybrid propagation models. On top of the physical twin, a line-of-sight (LoS)-aware implicit neural field is developed to construct an AI channel twin for efficient channel reconstruction. The proposed AI twin learns location-dependent channel statistics from the calibrated twin, enabling real-time prediction of received power and LoS probability. Building upon the reconstructed channel field, a system-level evaluation layer is derived to analyze coverage and interference for both AP-to-rack and rack-to-rack communications. Experimental results show that the proposed AI twin achieves lower power reconstruction error than existing neural-field baselines while maintaining real-time inference capability. Moreover, the ceiling-mounted AP deployment achieves over 90% coverage under a 10 dB signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR) threshold, demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed DT framework for THz wireless data-center planning and optimization.

</details>


### [139] [Online Non-Monotone DR-Submodular Maximization Matching the Offline $0.401$ Factor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02145)
*Vaneet Aggarwal,Yiyang Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在d维单位立方体的紧凸下闭子集上，非负、非单调DR-子模函数的在线最大化问题。作者证明离线最优近似因子0.401也可以在在线设置中实现，提出了基于加权在线学习器替代离线目标相关盒子步骤的算法，在条件无偏有界查询的完整信息价值预言机模型下达到0.401因子与次线性近似遗憾，并给出了多种遗憾—查询复杂度权衡方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前该问题最好的构造性离线近似因子为0.401（在相应的元可解性假设下），但对抗性在线设置下的保证一直停留在1/e ≈ 0.368。这一差距表明在线设置可能无法达到与离线相同的性能，作者希望证明0.401的因子同样可以在在线场景中实现，从而填补离线与在线结果之间的空白。

Method: 作者提出的在线算法并不直接对变化的目标函数运行离线构造方法，而是用一个加权在线学习器替代离线中依赖目标函数的盒子步骤，以累积方式控制所需的残差项。关键的精确非对称平衡定理使得在面对对抗性变化时仍能保持离线系数。此外，通过分批（batching）技术可实现查询次数与遗憾之间的权衡；在正锚点条件下，采用随机分块（randomized blocking）方法可在单点bandit设置中保持0.401的因子。

Result: 直接实现在每轮使用O(dT^{1/4})次预言机查询的情况下达到O(T^{3/4})的遗憾。更一般地，对任意δ∈[0,1/4]，分批给出每轮O(T^δ)次查询和O(T^{4/5-δ/5})遗憾的权衡，包括一个极端情形：每轮一次查询的O(T^{4/5})遗憾。在正锚点条件下，随机分块保持0.401因子并达到O(T^{5/6})的单点bandit遗憾。

Conclusion: 本文首次在对抗性在线学习框架下实现与最优离线构造方法相同的0.401近似因子，消除了在线与离线保证之间的理论差距。通过精确的平衡定理和创新的在线学习器设计，算法在面对对抗性的目标函数变化时仍能保持离线最优系数，并提供了丰富的复杂度权衡选择以适应不同查询预算场景，为DR-子模函数在线最大化提供了几乎匹配的理论保证。

Abstract: We study online maximization of nonnegative, non-monotone DR-submodular functions over compact convex down-closed subsets of the $d$-dimensional unit cube. The best known constructive offline approximation factor is $0.401$ under the corresponding meta-solvability assumptions, whereas comparable adversarial online guarantees had remained at $1/e$. We show that this factor is also achievable online. In the post-decision full-information value-oracle model, our algorithm attains factor $0.401$ with sublinear approximate regret when oracle feedback is conditionally unbiased and bounded.
  The online algorithm does not run the offline construction on a changing objective. Instead, it replaces the offline objective-dependent box step by a weighted online learner that controls the required residual terms cumulatively. An exact asymmetric balance theorem preserves the offline coefficients despite adversarial variation. The direct implementation has $O(T^{3/4})$ regret and uses $O(dT^{1/4})$ oracle calls per round. More generally, for every $δ\in[0,1/4]$, batching gives $O(T^δ)$ calls per round and $O(T^{4/5-δ/5})$ regret, including a one-call $O(T^{4/5})$ endpoint. Under a positive-anchor condition, randomized blocking retains factor $0.401$ with $O(T^{5/6})$ one-point bandit regret.

</details>


### [140] [Generative Diffusion Surrogates with Analytical Variance Schedule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01705)
*Patrick Reichherzer,Gianluca Gregori,David N. Hosking,Subir Sarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将生成式扩散模型的噪声调度与物理传输系统的方差（均方位移）时间导数相关联，从而将生成时间转化为校准的传输时钟，使代理模型能够精确模拟从弹道到扩散的湍流等离子体传输过程，无需启发式调度调参。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机传输系统的替代模型需要具备概率性、时间分辨能力和非高斯分布表征能力，而生成扩散模型虽具备这些特性，但其噪声调度通常是启发式选择的，缺乏物理时钟。在传输过程中，方差/均方位移往往已知，这为校准生成时间提供了物理依据。

Method: 将前向加噪速率设定为方差的时间导数，使生成时间成为校准的传输时钟。方差路径通过构造强制执行，而学习的得分场则表征从入口数据继承的非高斯结构在该路径上的平滑过程，无需中间时刻的物理传输数据。

Result: 在湍流等离子体的弹道-扩散传输中，该替代模型与测试粒子分布匹配，复现实验室测量的方差尺度，并追踪模拟的峰度演化，无需调度调参，实现校准模拟和基于似然的推断。

Conclusion: 通过将扩散模型的噪声调度锚定到物理已知的方差动力学上，构建了一种物理校准的时间分辨概率替代模型，弥合了生成模型的启发式时间与物理传输系统的真实时间之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Stochastic transport describes physical systems in which an initially structured distribution spreads under unresolved forcing, scattering, or heterogeneous media. Useful surrogates for such systems should be probabilistic, time-resolved, and able to represent non-Gaussian distributional structure. Generative diffusion models, which corrupt data with Gaussian noise and learn a reverse flow back to structured states, have these properties. Their noise schedules, however, are usually chosen heuristically: image and audio generation---the canonical use cases---provide no physical clock. In transport, by contrast, the variance, or mean-square displacement, is often known from macroscopic theory or empirical scaling even when the full distribution is not. Here we prescribe the forward noising rate as the time derivative of this variance, turning generative time into a calibrated transport clock. The variance path is enforced by construction, while the learned score field represents how non-Gaussian structure inherited from entrance data is smoothed along that path, requiring no intermediate-time physical transport data. For ballistic-to-diffusive transport in turbulent plasmas, the surrogate matches test-particle distributions, reproduces the laboratory-measured variance scale, and tracks the simulated kurtosis evolution without schedule tuning, enabling calibrated emulation and likelihood-based inference.

</details>


### [141] [Bayes-Optimal BER and AUC: Estimation and Evaluation of Estimators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02304)
*Ryota Ushio,Takashi Ishida,Masashi Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对机器学习中衡量模型最优性能的关键问题展开研究。已有工作表明可以通过软标签估计二分类中的贝叶斯误差（最优准确率），但在类别严重不平衡或标注含噪的场景下，准确率并不是合适的度量指标，平衡错误率（BER）和ROC曲线下面积（AUC）更为适用。作者提出两个互补贡献：(i) 基于软标签的最优BER和AUC估计器——先考虑干净设定（真实软标签和类先验已知），再扩展到类先验未知且软标签被未知保序变换及加性噪声污染的现实中，通过等渗回归结合辅助硬标签近似恢复干净软标签，用硬标签的裁剪均值估计类先验，并给出有限样本误差界；(ii) 评估方法——将原本用于评估贝叶斯误差估计器的FeeBee框架扩展到最优BER和AUC，无需真实最优值即可提供实用评估分数，适用于任何同类估计器。合成与真实数据实验验证了所提方法与评估流程的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确率在类别严重不平衡或标注含噪的场景下无法充分反映模型性能，而BER和AUC更为合适；但此前学界仅解决了贝叶斯误差（最优准确率）的可估计性问题，最优BER和AUC的可估计性问题尚未被解决，这导致实践中无法区分任务固有误差与模型缺陷，也就无法判断改进空间。

Method: (i) 估计：设计软标签估计最优BER和AUC。在干净设定下直接构造估计器；在含噪且类先验未知的设定下，利用等渗回归（isotonic regression）配合辅助硬标签近似恢复干净软标签，用硬标签的裁剪均值（clipped mean）估计类先验，形成plug-in估计器并推导有限样本误差界。(ii) 评估：将FeeBee框架从贝叶斯误差估计器扩展至最优BER和AUC估计器，无需最优值真值即可给出评估分数，并提出一个可适用于任意同类估计器的通用评估流程。

Result: 作者在合成数据集和真实数据集上进行了实验，验证了所提出的最优BER和AUC估计器以及扩展后的FeeBee评估流程的有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: 本文填补了最优BER和AUC估计这一研究空白，将贝叶斯误差的可估计性理论推广到更贴近实际的不平衡/含噪场景，并提供了不依赖未知最优值的通用评估框架，为实践中判断模型改进空间提供了有力工具。

Abstract: A fundamental quantity in machine learning is the optimal performance achievable by any model on a given task. Estimating this quantity allows us to distinguish the irreducible part of the error from a deficiency of the model, telling us how much room for improvement remains. Recent work has shown that the Bayes error, or equivalently the optimal accuracy, can be estimated from soft labels in binary classification. However, accuracy is often a poor summary of performance in settings with severe class imbalance or noisy annotations, where metrics such as the balanced error rate (BER) and the area under the ROC curve (AUC) are more appropriate. We address this gap with two complementary contributions. (i) Estimation. We propose soft-label-based estimators for the optimal BER and AUC. We first consider the clean setting in which true soft labels and the class prior are known, and then extend the estimators to a more realistic setting in which the class prior is unknown and the observed soft labels are corrupted by an unknown order-preserving transformation, possibly followed by additive noise. In the latter setting, we approximately recover the clean soft labels via isotonic regression with auxiliary hard labels, estimate the class prior with a clipped mean of the hard labels, and derive finite-sample error bounds for the resulting plug-in estimators. (ii) Evaluation. Since the optimum is unobservable on real datasets, evaluating any such estimator is itself nontrivial. We extend the FeeBee framework, originally proposed for evaluating Bayes-error estimators, to the optimal BER and AUC. The resulting procedure provides practical evaluation scores without requiring knowledge of the optimum, and applies to any estimator of the optimal BER or AUC, not only our proposed ones. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets validate both the estimators and the evaluation procedure.

</details>


### [142] [RecKAN: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks with a Learnable Recursive Polynomial Basis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01729)
*Amirhosein Azarpour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RecKAN，一种新型Kolmogorov-Arnold网络变体，其核心创新在于用二阶多项式递推关系定义可学习的基函数，而非固定使用B样条、Chebyshev等经典基函数。该递推能恢复多种经典多项式族（Chebyshev、Fibonacci、Pell、Jacobsthal等），且五系数可联合学习。实验表明RecKAN在多个分类和预测任务上优于参数匹配的KAN基线，并展现出可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KAN变体虽然将固定标量权重替换为边缘上的可学习单变量函数，但仍然固定基函数（如B样条、Chebyshev多项式、小波、Jacobi多项式），仅学习组合权重。这种固定的基函数选择限制了网络的表达能力，作者希望让基函数本身也能被学习和适应任务需求。

Method: RecKAN用二阶多项式递推R_{n+1}(x)=(ax²+bx+c)R_n(x)+(dx+e)R_{n-1}(x)定义基函数，五个系数(a,b,c,d,e)与网络联合学习。该递推可恢复Chebyshev(两种)、Fibonacci、Pell、Jacobsthal多项式等经典族作为特例。作者证明在该递推的子族中，多项式度数随n线性增长，从而能在经典基之上灵活移动。

Result: 在图像、文本、生物医学时序分类及时序预测等基准上，RecKAN在所有分类任务上优于三个参数匹配的KAN基线（Chebyshev、Jacobi、样条），并在ETTh1预测基准上取得最低MSE。作为卷积骨干的分类头时，在Fashion MNIST、CIFAR-10和SVHN上优于标准MLP头。在合成函数拟合中能追踪MLP欠拟合的尖锐振荡目标。学习到的递推系数具有可解释性。

Conclusion: RecKAN证明了让KAN的基函数本身可学习是有效且可行的方向。学习到的基函数能超越任何固定的经典基函数选择，并能在需要局部结构的任务上通过远离线性度增长区（包含所有经典族的区域）来实现更强的表达力，与理论分析一致。这为KAN的架构设计提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Kolmogorov--Arnold Networks (KANs) replace the fixed scalar weights of a standard network with learnable univariate functions on each edge, but existing variants still fix the \emph{basis} that those functions are built from: B-splines, Chebyshev polynomials, wavelets, or Jacobi polynomials, and learn only the combination weights over it. We introduce RecKAN, which instead defines the basis itself by a second order polynomial recurrence, $R_{n+1}(x) = (ax^2+bx+c)R_n(x) + (dx+e)R_{n-1}(x)$, whose five coefficients are learned jointly with the network. We show this recurrence recovers several classical polynomial families including both kinds of Chebyshev polynomials, Fibonacci, Pell, and Jacobsthal polynomials as special cases, and prove that its degree grows linearly in $n$ exactly on the sub-family containing all of them, giving a concrete sense in which the learned basis can move beyond any fixed classical choice. Across multiple benchmark datasets spanning image, text, biomedical time series classification, and time series forecasting, RecKAN outperforms three parameter-matched KAN baselines (Chebyshev, Jacobi, and spline based) on all classification tasks and achieves the lowest MSE on the ETTh1 forecasting benchmark. Additionally, when used as a classifier head with a convolutional backbone, RecKAN achieves higher accuracy than standard MLP heads on Fashion MNIST, CIFAR-10, and SVHN. On a synthetic function fitting benchmark it tracks a sharply oscillatory target that a parameter comparable MLP under fits. We further show that the learned recurrence coefficients are interpretable: on the task requiring the most local structure, training moves the basis away from the linear degree growth regime that contains every classical family we identify, consistent with our theoretical analysis of what that structural shift enables.

</details>


### [143] [CAT-Flow: Curvature-Adaptive sTeps for Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01746)
*Qinchan Li,Pedro Cisneros-Velarde,Keru Fu,Samuel Antunes Miranda,Sharan Vaswani,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种轻量级、无需训练的步长自适应算法（CAT-OV和CAT-OT），用于Flow Matching生成模型的推断阶段采样。通过建立Flow Matching采样与梯度流之间的新联系，在无需额外神经网络评估的情况下动态调整步长，可将达到同等图像质量所需的生成步数减少最多40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flow Matching虽已成为生成建模的前沿框架（支撑FLUX、Stable Diffusion 3.5等系统），但其基于ODE的迭代采样过程存在根本性效率瓶颈：生成质量对步长选择高度敏感，通常需要20-30步才能获得良好效果。

Method: 提出CAT-OV和CAT-OT两种算法。CAT-OV通过向量场的梯度来近似状态空间上的曲率；CAT-OT通过向量场时间导数的有限差分近似来估计随时间变化的曲率。两者均在推断时自适应调整步长，且无需额外神经函数评估，在适当条件下具有常数阶截断误差界。

Result: 在四个文生图Flow Matching模型上，CAT-OV和CAT-OT在图像质量指标上优于现有步长启发式方法，将达到可比质量所需的生成步数减少高达40%。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了通过梯度流视角的理论洞察，可以在不增加计算成本的前提下显著提升Flow Matching模型的采样效率，为高效生成推理提供了实用且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Flow Matching has emerged as a leading framework for generative modeling, powering state-of-the-art systems such as FLUX and Stable Diffusion 3.5. However, the iterative nature of its ODE-based sampling process creates a fundamental efficiency bottleneck: the quality of generated samples is highly sensitive to the choice of step-sizes, and current models typically require 20 to 30 steps for good quality. In this work, we propose two lightweight, training-free algorithms, CAT-OV and CAT-OT that adapt step-sizes at inference time based on a novel connection between Flow Matching sampling and gradient flow. Our algorithms are computed efficiently by not requiring additional neural function evaluations. Specifically, CAT-OT estimates curvature over time via a finite-difference approximation of the time-derivative of the vector field, while CAT-OV approximates curvature over the state space via a gradient of the vector field. Under suitable conditions, both methods have truncation error bounds of constant order. Empirically, CAT-OV and CAT-OT outperform existing step-size heuristics in image quality metrics across four text- to-image Flow Matching models, reducing the number of generation steps required to reach comparable quality by up to 40%.

</details>


### [144] [A Study of Conditional Diffusion Models for Open-Loop Control under Dry Friction and Stiction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01756)
*Eric Aislan Antonelo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究扩散模型（Action Diffusion）作为开环动作序列生成器，用于含干摩擦和静摩擦的点质量系统控制。通过条件1D U-Net生成有界控制序列，与随机射击和CEM等方法对比，发现Action Diffusion在低样本情况下能显著降低终端误差和卡滞步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的随机射击方法在存在静摩擦（stiction）的系统上效率低下，因为有效控制只占据动作序列空间中一个小的、时间结构化的子集。需要一种能生成时间连贯控制序列的方法来克服静摩擦障碍。

Method: 采用Action Diffusion框架：将动作序列扩散作为开环提议分布，使用紧凑的条件1D U-Net，以初始状态和目标状态为条件生成有界控制序列。并将其与均匀随机射击、基于结构化数据集先验的随机射击以及交叉熵方法（CEM）进行对比。

Result: Action Diffusion在终端误差和卡滞步数方面均优于其他基准方法，尤其在低采样数场景下优势更为明显。

Conclusion: 条件扩散模型能够通过条件化以及从训练先验中重组结构化控制基元，有效生成时间连贯的控制序列，从而克服静摩擦实现状态间开环控制。

Abstract: Diffusion models have recently emerged as expressive generative priors for planning and control. This paper studies Action Diffusion, an action-sequence diffusion formulation used as an open-loop proposal distribution for a point-mass system with dry friction and stiction. In this benchmark, motion starts only when the applied input exceeds a static-friction threshold, so effective controls occupy a small and temporally structured subset of the action-sequence space. A compact conditional 1D U-Net generates bounded control sequences conditioned on initial and target states. We compare it with uniform random shooting, random shooting from the same structured dataset prior, and the Cross-Entropy Method (CEM). Results show that Action Diffusion reduces terminal error and stuck steps, especially in low-sample regimes. These results indicate that conditional diffusion provides an effective mechanism for generating temporally coherent control sequences that overcome stiction by conditioning and recombining structured control primitives from the training prior for state-to-state open-loop control.

</details>


### [145] [Differentiable Electricity-Market Clearing for Gradient-Based Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02646)
*Luca Mungo,Maarten P. Scholl,Arnau Quera-Bofarull*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将市场出清建模为可微优化层，使数据中心选址规划问题能够通过梯度下降求解。在50MW负载分配问题上，与穷举法对比，其最坏目标差距仅为2.3%和8.5%，验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型数据中心规划困难在于：设施规模足以影响其支付的电力价格，而电价由市场出清决定（每个运行工况下都要重新求解的约束优化问题）。传统仿真只能评估候选方案性能，却无法指导如何改进方案。

Method: 将市场出清视为可微优化层：前向传播求解市场，反向模式自动微分将规划成本通过清算价格回传到规划变量。先用有限差分验证梯度正确性，然后应用于具体问题：在两个合成网络中将50MW数据中心负载分配给六个候选母线，每个活跃站点有固定成本，在36个运行状态下评估。

Result: 与穷举所有站点组合相比，梯度优化几乎精确恢复了连续分配方案，最坏情况下目标差距仅为成本差异（最佳与最差单站点之差）的2.3%和8.5%。唯一的系统误差出现在站点应关闭的成本阈值附近：由于离散站点数量的平滑松弛会使站点收缩而非关闭，导致离散转变发生较晚。

Conclusion: 可微市场出清将市场感知的规划问题转化为梯度可搜索的问题，为大型数据中心规划提供了一种基于梯度优化的新方法，尽管在离散决策边界处存在固有的松弛误差，但整体性能接近穷举最优。

Abstract: Planning a large data center is difficult because a facility big enough to matter changes the electricity prices it will pay. Those prices are set by market clearing, a constrained optimization problem solved anew in every operating condition. However, simulating the market tells a planner how a candidate plan performs but not how to improve it. Here we treat market clearing as a differentiable optimization layer: each forward pass solves the market, and reverse-mode automatic differentiation propagates the planning cost back through the cleared prices to the plan. After validating these gradients against finite differences, we apply them to a concrete problem: allocating 50 MW of data-center load across six candidate buses in two synthetic networks, under a fixed cost per active site, evaluated over 36 operating states. Judged against exhaustive enumeration of all site combinations, gradient optimization recovers the continuous allocations almost exactly, with worst-case objective gaps of 2.3\% and 8.5\% of the cost difference between the best and worst single site. Its one systematic error is instructive: near the costs at which a site should close, the smooth relaxation of the discrete site count shrinks the site rather than closing it, so discrete transitions arrive late. Differentiable market clearing thus turns market-aware planning into a problem gradients can search.

</details>


### [146] [Toward Explainable and Policy-Aware AI for Carbon Credit Price Prediction: A Research Framework for Emerging Carbon Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01765)
*Summaiya Unnisa Begum,Mohammed Nadeem Ullah,Mohammed Abdul Ghani Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EPA-CarbonNet架构，融合碳市场价格序列与政策文本进行跨注意力预测，并在11年碳指数数据上测试。结果大部分为负面：随机游走模型在RMSE上击败该模型，SHAP解释稳定性中等偏低，政策注意力与真实监管事件不吻合。唯一亮点是方向准确率达58.6%，超过所有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碳市场价格难以预测；现有研究集中在EU和中国碳市场，将监管文本压缩为情感分数，且报告准确率时缺乏校准和解释稳定性分析。作者提炼出十个反复出现的缺口并整理为影响-可行性矩阵，旨在改进碳价预测的可靠性与可解释性。

Method: 提出六层架构EPA-CarbonNet，通过跨注意力机制将市场时间序列与政策文本融合，并输出经校准的预测区间以及基于政策归因的解释。实验使用11年每日S&P碳指数数据。

Result: 结果整体负面：五日前向RMSE上随机游走(0.0365)优于模型(0.0475)；SHAP排名在不同重采样背景间仅rho=0.54的一致性；政策注意力从未与已记录的监管事件重合。但方向准确率58.6%超过所有基线。所有代码、数据和结果均开源。

Conclusion: 该研究如实报告负面结果，说明融合政策文本的复杂架构未必优于简单基准，但方向预测仍有优势。这为碳价预测领域提供了关于方法有效性、校准、解释稳定性及负面结果透明报告的重要参考。

Abstract: Carbon markets put a price on emissions, yet that price remains hard to forecast. Work in this area clusters on the EU and Chinese schemes, compresses regulatory text into a sentiment score, and reports accuracy without calibration or explanation stability. We distil ten recurring gaps into an impact-feasibility matrix and propose EPA-CarbonNet, a six-layer architecture that fuses market series with policy text by cross-attention and calibrated intervals alongside policy-attributed explanations. We then build and test it on eleven years of daily S and P carbon index data. The findings are largely negative, and reported as measured: a random walk beats the model on five-day RMSE (0.0365 against 0.0475), SHAP rankings agree at rho = 0.54 across resampled backgrounds, and policy attention never coincides with documented regulatory events. Directional accuracy, at 58.6 percent, leads every baseline. Code, data documentation and all result artifacts are available at https://github.com/Kimalice/Toward-Explainable-and-Policy-Aware-AI-for-Carbon-Credit-Price-Prediction

</details>


### [147] [Emergence of Fibrations, Compression, and Symmetry Breaking in Artificial Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01768)
*Osvaldo M Velarde,Lucas C Parra,Alireza Hashemi,Hernan A Makse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明深度神经网络在训练过程中会产生图论中称为"纤维化与覆盖"的局部对称性，这些覆盖对称性是随机梯度下降的稳定吸引子，可被利用实现高达83%的模型压缩而不损失性能，并且通过受控打破覆盖对称性可克服持续学习中的塑性损失问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工神经网络常被视为强大但不可解释的黑盒。作者希望从图论对称性的新视角出发，揭示深度学习中隐含的结构规律，从而将黑盒转换为可解释的彩色图，并为模型压缩和终身学习提供理论基础。

Method: 作者从理论上证明了深度神经网络中的学习会产生图论意义上（纤维化fibrations和覆盖coverings）的局部对称性，并证明覆盖对称性是随机梯度下降（SGD）的稳定吸引子。

Result: 实验验证了覆盖对称性在多层感知机、卷积网络、循环网络和Transformer等多种主流架构中均会出现。利用这些对称性可将模型压缩至原始大小的17%而不损失性能；受控打破覆盖对称性可克服塑性丧失问题，在持续学习任务中取得最先进性能。

Conclusion: 理论结果提供了基于对称性的AI系统新基础，能够将黑盒网络转化为可解释的有色图，实现更高效的推理和终身学习能力。

Abstract: Artificial neural networks are often regarded as powerful yet opaque black boxes. Here, we demonstrate that learning in deep neural networks generates local symmetries known in graph theory as fibrations and coverings. We prove that covering symmetries are stable attractors of stochastic gradient descent. Consistent with this theory, we report the emergence of covering symmetries across major network architectures, including multilayer, convolutional, recurrent, and transformer networks. Exploiting these symmetries enables drastic model compression - reducing networks to 17% of their original size without sacrificing performance. Furthermore, controlled breaking of covering symmetry overcomes the loss of plasticity, achieving state-of-the-art performance in continual learning. The theoretical results provide a new foundation for AI systems based on symmetries that convert black boxes into interpretable colored graphs and enable more efficient inference and lifelong learning.

</details>


### [148] [D-FROST: Decentralized Federated pRompt-tuning via Optimal tranSporT for Non-IID and Imbalanced Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01802)
*Quan Minh Nguyen,Hoang M. Ngo,Trong Nghia Hoang,My T. Thai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了去中心化联邦学习（DFL）中的提示词微调问题。针对局部数据异构导致提示词集合无法按索引对齐的挑战，作者将去中心化提示词微调形式化为基于Wasserstein距离的提示词测度优化问题，提出D-FROST算法——一种基于最优传输的去中心化提示词微调算法，通过传输匹配将邻域提示词合并为紧凑的代表性提示词集合，并提供了理论收敛性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示词微调只需冻结预训练骨干网络并更新少量可学习提示词，非常适合去中心化联邦学习（DFL）中全量模型交换代价高昂的场景。然而，异构局部数据学习得到的提示词集合在索引上不对齐，导致标准的去中心化平均方法失效；此外算法需要具备共识达成与收敛的理论保证。当前缺乏对DFL中提示词微调的系统研究。

Method: 将去中心化提示词微调形式化为基于Wasserstein距离的提示词测度优化问题，以刻画提示词的集合结构。提出D-FROST算法，利用最优传输（OT）匹配将邻域客户端的提示词合并为紧凑的代表性提示词集合，实现去中心化聚合。

Result: 理论上界定了各客户端间的Wasserstein共识误差，并建立了网络级提示词质心收敛到驻点邻域的结论。在异构客户端数据上的实验验证了D-FROST在去中心化提示词微调中的有效性。

Conclusion: D-FROST通过最优传输框架有效解决了异构数据下提示词索引不对齐的去中心化聚合难题，为提示词微调在去中心化联邦学习中的理论与算法提供了初步系统化研究。

Abstract: Prompt tuning provides a parameter-efficient way to adapt foundation models (FMs) by freezing the pretrained backbone and updating only a small set of learnable prompts. This property makes prompt tuning especially suitable for decentralized federated learning (DFL), where exchanging full-model updates can be prohibitively expensive. However, prompt tuning in DFL introduces new challenges. Prompt sets learned from heterogeneous local data may not be index-wise aligned, making standard decentralized averaging unsuitable. In addition, the algorithm should be theoretically guaranteed to achieve consensus and make progress toward the shared objective. In this work, we provide the first study of prompt tuning in DFL. We formulate decentralized prompt tuning as a Wasserstein-based optimization problem over prompt measures, which captures the set-valued structure of prompts. We then propose D-FROST, an optimal-transport-based (OT-based) decentralized prompt-tuning algorithm that merges neighborhood prompts into compact representative prompt sets through transportation-based matching. We further analyze D-FROST by bounding the Wasserstein consensus error across clients, and establishing convergence of the network-level prompt barycenter to a neighborhood of stationarity. Experiments under heterogeneous client data demonstrate the effectiveness of D-FROST for decentralized prompt tuning.

</details>


### [149] [hLLM: Single Pass Decoding for Generative Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01807)
*Emil Laftchiev,Prachi Agrawal,Moe Kayali,Bixing Yan,Qi Xu,Zijie Lei,Chen Qiu,Zhi Hua,Ke Li,Luke Simon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出hLLM（Hungarian LLM），一种针对排序任务的格式特化解码策略。传统LLM生成排序需要自回归逐token解码，而hLLM仅需O(1)次前向传播即可解码全部N个序数。方法核心是从LLM的prefill隐状态中读取N×K的item-position分数矩阵，再通过匈牙利算法求解最优二分匹配，直接得到合法排列。通过LoRA微调与教师排序蒸馏，实现28ms端到端推理，较基线提速64倍且排序质量与教师持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在生成式排序中达到最先进水平，但其自回归解码每个token都需要一次顺序前向传播，效率极低。作者观察到排序任务只需输出N个序数值（按排序后的项目顺序），这种窄且具有置换结构的输出格式可以支持比从左到右生成更高效的解码策略。

Method: (1) 利用轻量级自注意力头从LLM的prefill隐状态中一次性读出N×K的item-position分数矩阵；(2) 使用匈牙利算法对该矩阵求解最优二分分配，直接解码出全部N个序数；(3) 实现O(1)次前向传播完成解码，构造出天然合法的排列而非事后修复；(4) 结合LoRA微调和教师排名蒸馏作为训练信号，系统研究了训练信号与骨干适配的影响。

Result: LoRA微调配合教师排名蒸馏达到28ms端到端推理，相比基线提速64倍，同时保持与教师相当的排序质量。作者提供了完整消融实验，分解了架构、训练信号和骨干适配各自贡献。

Conclusion: 该框架将生成式排序与组合优化联系起来，证明了置换结构的输出无需逐token自回归解码，可通过匹配算法一次求解。这为实时排序场景以及其他O(1)解码机制开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve state-of-the-art generative ranking quality, but the ranking they produce must be decoded, and autoregressive decoding spends one sequential forward pass per emitted token. We observe that the only tokens a ranker must emit are the $N$ ordinal values naming the items in ranked order, and that this narrow, permutation-structured output format admits decoding strategies which are much more efficient than left-to-right generation. We introduce hLLM (Hungarian LLM), a format-specialized decoding strategy that decodes all $N$ ordinals in $O(1)$ forward passes. hLLM reads an $N \times K$ item-position score matrix off the LLM's prefill hidden states with a lightweight self-attention head, then decodes the ordinals as the optimal bipartite assignment of that matrix via the Hungarian algorithm, yielding a valid permutation by construction rather than by repair. Through a systematic study of training signals and backbone adaptation, we show that LoRA-based fine-tuning combined with teacher ranking distillation reaches 28 ms end-to-end inference, a speed-up of $64\times$ while maintaining ranking quality on par with the teacher. We provide a complete ablation decomposing the contributions of architecture, training signal, and backbone adaptation. Our framework connects generative ranking to combinatorial optimization, opening a path toward other $O(1)$-decode mechanisms for real-time ranking.

</details>


### [150] [Import What You Need: Learning When and How to Augment EHR Graphs with External Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01839)
*Chen Chen,Mohsen Nayebi Kerdabadi,Dongjie Wang,Mei Liu,Zijun Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReTA提出了一种基于强化学习的动态拓扑增强框架，用于电子健康记录（EHR）的时间序列预测。它通过分诊次、预算感知的策略从外部知识图谱（KG）中进行知识导入，解决现有方法固定且上下文无关的拓扑增强问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EHR的数据稀疏且不规则，知识图谱增强虽能缓解该问题，但现有方法采取固定、上下文无关的拓扑增强方式，即无论患者的病情演化状态如何，都加入相同的KG节点和边，缺乏针对性和动态适应性。

Method: ReTA包含三个关键组件：(1)离线构建KG模板的精炼池；(2)学习策略每次访问从三种动作中选择一个：Soft Import（仅丰富节点特征不修改拓扑）、Hard Import（将紧凑KG子图嫁接到访问图上创造消息传递捷径）、Skip（当基础编码器已足够置信时不做增强）；(3)采用解耦编码器分别处理语义和结构信号，并通过自适应门控融合，以稳定学习。

Result: 在MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV上进行诊断预测、死亡率和再入院实验，ReTA持续优于强基线且保持高效，能在数据集和知识图谱间迁移，产生可解释的增强模式。在稀疏监督下的稳健增益凸显了动态知识导入决策的优势。

Conclusion: ReTA通过强化学习实现每次就诊动态、预算感知的知识导入决策，相比固定拓扑增强显著提升了准确率同时控制了成本，展示了动态决策在知识增强中的核心价值。

Abstract: Longitudinal prediction from electronic health records (EHRs) is limited by the sparsity and irregularity in patient trajectories, and knowledge augmentation with external knowledge graphs (KGs) offers a promising way to alleviate these issues. However, most existing methods perform fixed, context-agnostic topology augmentation by adding the same KG nodes and edges regardless of a patient's evolving state. We propose ReTA, a Reinforcement learning-based dynamic Topology Augmentation framework that casts KG import as a per-visit, budget-aware policy. ReTA first constructs an offline refined pool of KG-grounded templates, then learns a policy to select one augment action per visit from three options: Soft Import, which enriches node features without modifying graph topology, Hard Import, which grafts a compact KG subgraph onto the visit graph to create message-passing shortcuts, and Skip, which leaves the visit unaugmented when the base encoder is already confident. To stabilize learning, ReTA employs a decoupled encoder that processes semantic and structural signals in separate channels and fuses them via adaptive gating. Experiments on MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV across diagnosis prediction, mortality, and readmission show that ReTA consistently outperforms strong baselines while remaining efficient, transfers across datasets and knowledge graphs, and yields interpretable augmentation patterns. The robust gains under sparse supervision highlight the advantage of ReTA's dynamic decision to import knowledge, boosting accuracy while curbing costs.

</details>


### [151] [OutageDiT: A Generative Foundation Model for Power Outage Forecasting and Scenario Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01896)
*Yunqin Zhu,Feng Qiu,Yao Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OutageDiT，一个基于美国全国停电与气象数据训练的深度生成式基础模型，用于生成七天、15分钟分辨率的停电轨迹，支持点预测、不确定性量化与条件事件模拟，并具备零样本迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 停电规划需要在事件发生前生成场景，以表征停电幅度、时间与持续时长的不确定性，同时保持时间依赖性。然而严重停电事件稀少，单一区域数据中极端停电与恢复模式的样本极少，难以支撑可靠的情景生成。

Method: OutageDiT包含两个关键组件：条件编码器（condition encoder）仅需处理一次历史上下文与已知未来协变量，生成与预测时域对齐的状态；浅层流解码器（shallow flow decoder）复用这些对齐状态，生成完整的七天停电轨迹。

Result: 在停电预测基准测试中，OutageDiT在预测准确性与场景质量上均超越强基线方法，并支持对未见过区域的零样本迁移。

Conclusion: 条件停电模拟可作为从停电预测到不确定性下运维规划的桥梁，为停电事件前的场景规划提供统一且可行的深度生成框架。

Abstract: Power-outage planning requires scenarios before an event occurs. These scenarios must represent uncertainty in magnitude, timing, and duration while preserving temporal dependence. However, severe events are rare, and data from any single region contain few examples of extreme outage and restoration patterns. To address this challenge, we introduce OutageDiT, a foundation model for generating seven-day outage trajectories at quarter-hour resolution, trained on outage and weather records across the United States. Specifically, a condition encoder processes the historical context and known future covariates once per forecast, and a shallow flow decoder reuses the resulting horizon-aligned states to generate complete trajectories. The resulting samples support point forecasting, uncertainty quantification, and conditional event simulation within one deep generative model. Across outage forecasting benchmarks, OutageDiT improves forecast accuracy and scenario quality over strong baselines and supports zero-shot transfer to held-out regions. Together, these results position conditional outage simulation as a bridge from outage forecasting to operational planning under uncertainty.

</details>


### [152] [CRISP: Cliff-awaRe Input-adaptive Sparse Prefilling with Structural-Mass-Motivated Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01925)
*Huu Huy Nguyen,Chien Van Nguyen,Franck Dernoncourt,Ryan A. Rossi,Linh Ngo Van,Jieyang Chen,Thien Huu Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CRISP提出了一种输入自适应的稀疏注意力预填充方法，通过结构代理C_struct取代JSD路由以消除开销，并引入基于噪声底层的sink-aware阈值来应对长上下文中softmax后的累积背景噪声问题，在多个基准上超越现有稀疏方法并匹配甚至超过密集注意力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM推理中，注意力预填充阶段的计算复杂度随序列长度呈二次方增长，成为严重瓶颈。现有稀疏注意力方法要么依赖固定模式或离线分析缺乏自适应性，要么采用动态路由但依赖间接代理且预算分配机制忽视了softmax后的质量层次结构（mass hierarchy）。

Method: 1) 提出C_struct结构代理，直接从句点注意力图的结构读取路由决策，替代JSD路由，消除池化矩阵乘法和KL散度开销；2) 形式化softmax后的质量悬崖（mass cliff），从理论上证明严格累积覆盖阈值在长上下文中会累积O(n)的背景噪声；3) 提出sink-aware阈值，基于噪声底层来导航质量悬崖。

Result: 在InfiniteBench、RULER和LongBench上跨两个模型家族的实验中，CRISP是最强的稀疏方法，在检索密集型基准上匹配或超过精确密集注意力，相比基线在检索任务上恢复高达+28.0个百分点，在512k token下实现高达5.30倍的注意力加速。

Conclusion: CRISP通过O(n)噪声消除策略在选择过程中保持结构完整性的同时实现了显著的加速效果，验证了直接从代理注意力图读取路由决策和基于噪声底层的阈值设计的有效性。

Abstract: The attention prefilling phase of long-context LLM inference scales quadratically, making self-attention a severe computational bottleneck. Traditional sparse attention methods mitigate this through fixed patterns or offline profiling, but lack the flexibility to adapt to input-dependent attention structure. Recent dynamic methods address this by routing heads to sparse patterns in real-time, but rely on indirect routing proxies with overhead and budget allocation mechanisms that overlook the post-softmax mass hierarchy. We present CRISP (Cliff-awaRe Input-adaptive Sparse Prefilling), which identifies and addresses two structural challenges in this dynamic routing paradigm. First, we show that the routing decision can be read directly off the structure of the proxy attention map. We replace the Jensen-Shannon Divergence (JSD) routing with C_struct, a structural proxy that measures mass at Vertical-Slash compatible positions and reproduces JSD's routing decisions while eliminating both the pooled matmul and subsequent KL divergence overhead. Second, we formalize the post-softmax mass cliff and demonstrate theoretically that strictly cumulative coverage thresholds accumulate O(n) background noise at long contexts. CRISP navigates this via a sink-aware threshold grounded in the noise floor. Empirically, across InfiniteBench, RULER and LongBench on two model families, CRISP is the strongest sparse method overall and matches or exceeds exact dense attention on retrieval-heavy benchmarks, recovering up to +28.0 pp on retrieval tasks over baselines and achieving up to a 5.30x attention speedup at 512k tokens, driven primarily by our O(n) noise elimination during selection while preserving structural integrity.

</details>


### [153] [OR-Transformer: Scaling Real-Time Decision-Making to 1,000 Items](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01933)
*Shuze Daniel Liu,David Simchi-Levi,Claire Chen,Chutong Gao,Shangtong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OR-Transformer，一种深度强化学习框架，用于随机需求下的联合补货问题。该方法采用物品置换等变的Transformer架构和路径梯度训练，处理高达1024种库存物品的规模，相比MILP求解器在线决策时间缩短400万倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代供应链运营需要协调成千上万种异构物品的补货，涉及相关随机需求、异构交货时间和共享固定订货成本，观测空间超过10^4维。在此规模下，滚动时域随机混合整数线性规划(MILP)速度过慢，而标准强化学习在高层动作空间中面临信用分配的严峻挑战。

Method: 提出OR-Transformer深度强化学习框架，包含：(1) 物品置换等变的Transformer架构，利用注意力机制建模物品间的相关性和交互；(2) 通过库存动态的路径梯度(pathwise-gradient)训练方式，解决高维动作空间中的信用分配问题；(3) 针对联合补货问题的专门设计，处理随机需求、异构提前期和共享固定订货成本。

Result: 在多达1024个库存物品的问题规模下，随着规模增大，OR-Transformer相比基于学习的方法和滚动时域MILP基线表现越来越好。同时，相比MILP求解器，在线决策时间减少了超过400万倍，实现了实时的大规模深度RL供应链运营。

Conclusion: OR-Transformer证明了深度强化学习在超大规模(10^3级别物品)供应链补货优化中的可行性，能够同时实现优于MILP基线的性能和极具优势的计算效率，为实际供应链运营中的实时决策提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Modern supply chain operations can require coordinating replenishment across thousands of heterogeneous items under correlated stochastic demand, heterogeneous lead times, and shared fixed ordering costs, yielding observation spaces exceeding $10^4$ dimensions. At this scale, rolling-horizon stochastic mixed-integer linear programs (MILPs) become prohibitively slow, while standard reinforcement learning (RL) methods face increasingly challenging credit assignment in high-dimensional action spaces. We introduce OR-Transformer, a deep reinforcement learning framework for joint replenishment under stochastic demand, with an item-permutation-equivariant Transformer architecture and pathwise-gradient training through the inventory dynamics. Across problem sizes up to 1,024 inventory items, OR-Transformer increasingly outperforms learning-based and rolling-horizon MILP baselines as scale grows. It also reduces online decision-making time by over 4 million times relative to MILP solvers, enabling real-time, large-scale deep RL in supply chain operations.

</details>


### [154] [Refining Heuristic-Based Bitcoin Address Clustering with Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01942)
*Hugo Schnoering,Roman Bresson,Michalis Vazirgiannis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于对比学习和图神经网络的方法，用于精炼比特币交易中启发式聚类得到的结果。通过嵌入学习和层次聚类，该方法能识别出启发式方法可能存在的错误合并，并提供更细粒度的地址分析。论文还发布了包含大量聚类的公开比特币交易图数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比特币的伪匿名性使得用户级活动分析具有挑战性，因为一个用户可能控制多个地址。现有基于启发式的方法虽然在识别同一用户地址方面有所尝试，但往往产生模块性有限的扁平聚类分配，且容易出错（如错误地将不同用户合并在一起），因此需要更精细、更可靠的聚类精炼方法。

Method: 该方法包括三个部分：(1) 发布包含大量聚类的比特币交易图公开数据集；(2) 提出基于图神经网络对比嵌入（contrastive embeddings）的学习方法论，使地址嵌入与启发式规则保持一致，并辅以理论指导直觉；(3) 通过层次聚类（hierarchical clustering）实现启发式聚类的更精细分析，并提供用于标记可疑合并的定量判据。

Result: 论文的主要贡献包括：公开发布了包含大量聚类的比特币交易图数据集；提出了与启发式算法一致的对比嵌入学习方法论并提供了理论基础；通过层次聚类实现了对启发式聚类的细化分析，并给出了定量标准来标记可疑的错误合并。

Conclusion: 该工作为比特币用户聚类分析提供了一种结合图神经网络和层次聚类的精炼框架，能够有效改善启发式聚类的准确性，并为链上实体分析提供了更可靠的工具和公开数据资源。

Abstract: Bitcoin's pseudonymous nature makes it challenging to analyze user-level activity, since a single user may control multiple identifiers (addresses). Existing heuristic-based methods attempt to identify addresses belonging to the same user, but they often produce flat cluster assignments with limited modularity and are prone to errors such as merging different users together. In this work, we propose a method for refining heuristic-obtained clusters by grounding our clustering on contrastive embeddings yielded by graph neural networks. Our contributions are threefold: (i) we release a publicly available dataset of Bitcoin transaction graphs containing a substantial number of clusters; (ii) we propose a methodology for learning address embeddings consistent with heuristics, and back it up with theoretical guiding intuitions; (iii) through hierarchical clustering, we enable a finer analysis of heuristic clusters and provide a quantitative criterion for flagging suspicious merges.

</details>


### [155] [On-Policy Distillation Meets Off-Policy GRPO: Training Compact Instruction-Following Rerankers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01947)
*Vignesh Prabhakar,Jialing Pan,Anil Babu Ankisettipalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段强化学习框架用于指令跟随重排序器（reranker）的蒸馏：第一阶段使用离策略GRPO和LLM-judge反馈强化4B教师模型；第二阶段让1B学生模型从自身策略采样排序，并接受教师软奖励进行在策略蒸馏。实验表明该方法在分布偏移场景下显著优于离线列表式KD、离线RankNet KD和在策略GKD，在MAIR-11和MAIR-Full上均取得最佳性能，同时超越两个7B RL训练的重排序器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统重排序器蒸馏依赖于在固定示例集上对教师输出的离线模仿，其监督信号被限制在教师已观察的排序空间中，无法充分探索学生的能力边界。作者希望通过强化学习视角重新审视蒸馏过程，解决离线蒸馏监督空间受限的问题。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：(1) 第一阶段，用离策略GRPO和LLM-judge反馈在88K指令跟随样本上强化4B教师重排序器；(2) 第二阶段，紧凑的1B学生模型从自身策略采样排序，并在这些学生采样的排序上获得教师派生的软奖励，将学生探索与知识迁移耦合。

Result: 在MAIR-11上达到0.7670 nDCG@6，比离线列表式KD高+4.6点；在MAIR-Full的126个任务、9,356个查询上取得最高任务宏平均（0.6808 nDCG@6和0.7865 MRR@6）；超过两个7B RL训练的重排序器；该训练程序对三种不同架构的学生骨干均有一致提升；在9,861查询验证基准上达到0.7624 nDCG@6。

Conclusion: 基于奖励的、作用于学生自采样排序的在策略蒸馏能够获得比离线蒸馏目标或单纯将教师分布匹配迁移到在策略更优的效果，表明学生主动探索结合教师软奖励是紧凑重排序器蒸馏的有效范式，且具有更好的质量-效率权衡。

Abstract: Compact instruction-following rerankers are attractive for deployment, but conventional distillation pipelines typically train students by offline imitation of teacher outputs on a fixed set of examples, constraining supervision to the teacher's observed ranking space. We revisit reranker distillation through the lens of reinforcement learning.
  We propose a two-stage framework combining off-policy teacher optimization with on-policy student distillation. In Stage 1, a 4B teacher reranker is strengthened with off-policy GRPO using LLM-judge feedback on 88K instruction-following examples. In Stage 2, a compact 1B student samples rankings from its own policy and receives soft teacher-derived rewards on those rankings, coupling student exploration with knowledge transfer.
  Our strongest gains appear under distribution shift. On MAIR-11, the original 11-subset, 869-query evaluation, the proposed student reaches 0.7670 nDCG@6, outperforming offline listwise KD by +4.6 points. Controlled comparisons against offline pairwise RankNet KD and on-policy GKD show that neither changing the offline distillation objective nor moving teacher-distribution matching on-policy reproduces the performance of reward-based on-policy distillation over student-sampled rankings. The advantage persists on MAIR-Full: across all 126 tasks and 9,356 queries, the proposed method obtains the highest task-macro point estimates among the evaluated distillation variants, reaching 0.6808 nDCG@6 and 0.7865 MRR@6. It also exceeds two released 7B RL-trained rerankers on the comparable MAIR-11 evaluation, while the same Stage 2 training procedure consistently improves three architecturally distinct alternative student backbones.
  On the 9,861-query validation benchmark, the resulting 1B reranker achieves 0.7624 nDCG@6 while providing a favorable quality-efficiency tradeoff relative to larger alternatives.

</details>


### [156] [Convergence Theory of Knowledge Distillation in Asynchronous P2P Gossip Learning Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01952)
*Lucas Qingyang Fang,Tiyao Liu,Jinhao Jing,Zeji Li,Kaijie Chen,Harikrishna Kuttivelil,Katia Obraczka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为完全去中心化、异步的点对点(P2P)知识蒸馏(KD)提供了收敛性理论。作者将共识从参数空间迁移到函数(输出)空间，证明KD事件是logit空间中预测分布的几何收缩算子，并在参考测度上的预测Hilbert空间中分析。在标准光滑性/方差假设和两个可实现性假设下，时间平均函数平稳性和函数空间不一致性以O(1/(ηT))速率收敛到O(η)+O(B_f²)+O(ζ_f²)邻域。实验表明KD在异构网络中将函数不一致收缩40-61倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化无服务器学习中，异构设备架构不同导致参数维度不同，标准去中心化SGD因参数无法平均而失效。KD通过交换软预测而非权重来规避此问题，但完全去中心化、异步P2P KD缺乏收敛性理论，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 将共识从参数空间重新定位到函数(输出)空间；将KD事件建模为logit空间中对等体预测分布的几何收缩算子，在参考测度的Hilbert预测空间中进行理论分析；基于标准光滑性/方差假设，外加两个可实现性假设(一个桥接参数SGD到函数步骤，一个控制受限任务/KD对齐)，推导收敛界。

Result: 时间平均函数平稳性和函数空间不一致性以O(1/(ηT))速率收敛到O(η)+O(B_f²)+O(ζ_f²)邻域；其中B_f为任务最优点到对等体可达类别的距离，ζ_f衡量持久局部任务异质性。实验覆盖同构、宽度异构和混合家族网络，KD将函数不一致收缩40-61倍而隔离训练不收缩；采样平稳性诊断在共享骨架主运行中晚期瞬态指数为0.99-1.90；四步长扫描呈现出预测的瞬态-邻域权衡。

Conclusion: 该工作为异构架构下的去中心化异步KD提供了首个完整的收敛性理论框架，证明KD能有效缩小函数空间不一致性，并揭示出步长选择与收敛邻域之间的理论权衡关系，为实际部署提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Decentralized, serverless learning increasingly connects devices running different architectures, where the standard tool, decentralized SGD, is undefined as models with different parameter counts cannot be averaged. Knowledge distillation (KD) exchanges soft predictions rather than weights and sidesteps this obstacle, yet convergence theory for fully decentralized, asynchronous peer-to-peer (P2P) KD is lacking. We provide one, relocating consensus from parameter space to function (output) space: a KD event is a geometric contraction operator in logit space on the peers' predictive distributions, which we analyse in the Hilbert space of predictions on a reference measure. Under standard smoothness/variance assumptions and two realizability assumptions, one bridging parameter SGD to the functional step and one controlling restricted task/KD alignment, the time-averaged functional stationarity and function-space disagreement converge at rate $O(1/(ηT))$ to an $O(η)+O(B_f^2)+O(ζ_f^2)$ neighbourhood. Here $B_f$ is the distance from the task optimum to the peers' reachable classes and $ζ_f$ measures persistent local-task heterogeneity. Across homogeneous, width-heterogeneous, and mixed-family networks of the experiments, KD contracts function disagreement by $40-61\times$, while isolated training does not. The sampled stationarity diagnostic has late transient exponents $0.99-1.90$ on the shared-skeleton main runs, and the four-point step-size sweep exhibits the predicted transient: neighbourhood tradeoff.

</details>


### [157] [FlashKAN: B-Spline KANs via Truncated Power Form](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01956)
*Naveen Mysore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlashKAN通过使用截断幂形式替代Cox-de Boor递推来计算均匀三次B样条激活函数，显著加速Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks的前向传播，并提供生产就绪的开源包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KANs在边上使用可学习的B样条激活，但标准Cox-de Boor递推需要对k次样条进行k次顺序遍历，消耗超过90%的前向传播时间，成为性能瓶颈。

Method: 利用近似理论中的经典结果——截断幂形式，将每个均匀三次B样条表示为五个移位节点上的(x)_+^3项之和；结合torch.compile融合为单个GPU内核，消除递推、跨度查找和分散-聚集操作；采用有界坐标稳定化，将归一化输入钳制到[0, k+1]以防止灾难性抵消。

Result: 提供了三个贡献：编译融合实现、数值稳定化方法以及可直接使用的开源包(pip install flashkan)作为现有KAN层的即插即用替代品。

Conclusion: FlashKAN通过数学形式变换和工程优化解决了KAN中B样条激活的计算瓶颈问题，在保持数值稳定性的前提下大幅提升性能。

Abstract: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) place learnable B-spline activations on network edges rather than fixed activations on nodes. The standard Cox-de Boor recursion evaluates these activations through k sequential passes for degree-k splines, consuming over 90% of forward-pass time. FlashKAN replaces this recursion with the truncated power form, a classical result from approximation theory that expresses each uniform cubic B-spline as five (x)_+^3 terms at shifted knot positions. This paper makes three contributions: (1) a torch.compile-fused implementation that collapses these operations into a single GPU kernel, eliminating all recursion, span lookup, and scatter-gather operations; (2) a bounded-coordinate stabilization that clamps the normalized input to [0, k+1], preventing the catastrophic cancellation that historically motivated the Cox-de Boor recursion; and (3) a production-ready, open-source package (pip install flashkan) that serves as a drop-in replacement for existing KAN layers.

</details>


### [158] [A Unified Particle Filter LSTM for Data-Driven Process Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01967)
*Parvin Malekzadeh,Opher Baron,Dmitry Krass*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Unified PF-LSTM框架，通过粒子滤波器维护多个循环状态假设来解决事件日志中潜在过程状态不完整的问题，端到端训练并在三个真实急诊科数据集上优于现有数据驱动基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件日志只记录活动完成而缺乏服务开始时间，导致同一过程历史可能对应多种潜在过程状态。标准循环模型（如LSTM）将每个过程前缀压缩为单一确定性状态，无法捕获这种多模态不确定性。

Method: 提出Unified Particle Filter LSTM（Unified PF-LSTM），维持并顺序更新一组带权重的循环状态假设（粒子信念）。利用加权均值和基于矩生成函数的特征来汇总粒子信念，用于预测下一个活动的类别分布以及当前活动逗留时间的条件分位数，框架从事件日志端到端训练。

Result: 在三个真实急诊科数据集上评估，该框架在再现路由、持续时间和系统级行为方面一致优于所有考虑的数据驱动基线，在复杂过程动态仅部分反映在事件日志中的场景下增益尤为显著。

Conclusion: 通过粒子滤波器维护多重状态假设而非单一确定状态，能更有效地应对事件日志的部分可观测性问题，从而提升数据驱动过程仿真的预测与生成质量。

Abstract: Data-driven process simulation aims to generate realistic case trajectories from historical event logs without requiring an explicitly specified model of the underlying dynamics. Deep sequence models can capture complex temporal dependencies through next-activity probabilities and conditional time distributions. However, event logs provide only a partial view of the underlying process state, often recording activity completions without the corresponding service-start times. Consequently, the same observed process history may be consistent with multiple plausible latent process conditions, whereas standard recurrent models compress each process prefix into a single deterministic recurrent state. We propose a Unified Particle Filter LSTM (Unified PF-LSTM) that maintains and sequentially updates a weighted set of recurrent-state hypotheses. We summarize this particle belief using its weighted mean and learned features based on the moment-generating function. The resulting representation is used to predict a categorical distribution over the next activity and conditional quantiles of the current activity's sojourn time. The framework is trained end-to-end from event-log data and evaluated on three real-world emergency department datasets. The results show that the proposed framework consistently outperforms the considered data-driven baselines in reproducing routing, duration, and system-level behavior across all datasets, with particularly strong gains in settings where complex process dynamics are only partially reflected in the available event logs.

</details>


### [159] [CAHR-Net: Condition-Adaptive Hysteresis Reconstruction for Compact and Interpretable Magnetic Core Loss Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01991)
*Chunye Gong,Cong Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CAHR-Net，一种条件自适应的磁滞回线重建网络。它将运行条件（频率、温度、波形统计）通过特征级线性调制（FiLM）注入中间回线重建表示中，而非像现有模型那样仅作用于终端标量。在MagNet数据集上以1874个参数达到6.89%的平均p95相对误差，为所有对比方法中最低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁芯损耗源于磁滞回线，但现有模型（经验方程和数据驱动方法）只在终端标量上施加条件影响，没有保留中间磁滞回线表示供条件重塑。这使得物理上的条件作用机制无法被模型充分利用。

Method: 提出CAHR-Net，保持从磁通密度波形到磁场重建、回线面积积分、功率损耗估计的可解释链条；使用特征级线性调制（FiLM）将频率、温度和波形统计注入中间重建表示；并配套设计了基于AdamW、余弦调度和分阶段重建到损耗目标的大批量训练协议。

Result: 在MagNet A-E材料协议上，平均p95相对误差6.89%（1874参数），为所有对比方法中最低；比最强黑盒方案少约48倍参数且最低差材料p95更低；将物理重建骨干从7.47%降至6.89%，最困难材料D从16.40%降至14.87%。消融和条件切片分析证实改进来自物理回线重建、结构化条件调制和匹配优化轨迹的耦合。

Conclusion: 在物理作用位置注入运行条件而非终端标量，结合物理可解释链和匹配的训练协议，可以实现高精度且极低参数量的磁芯损耗预测，为将物理知识嵌入深度学习架构提供了有效范例。

Abstract: Magnetic core loss originates in the hysteresis loop: the energy dissipated per excitation cycle equals the loop area, and frequency, temperature, and waveform shape set the loss by reshaping the loop geometry. Most existing models let these conditions act only on a terminal scalar - empirical equations fold them into fitted exponents, and data-driven predictors append them to encoded features - so no intermediate hysteresis representation remains for the conditions to reshape. This paper proposes CAHR-Net, a condition-adaptive hysteresis reconstruction network that injects the operating conditions where they physically act. It preserves the interpretable chain from flux density waveform to magnetic field reconstruction, loop-area integration, and power loss estimation, and uses feature-wise linear modulation to inject frequency, temperature, and waveform statistics into the intermediate reconstruction representation. A matched large-batch training protocol based on AdamW, cosine scheduling, and a staged reconstruction-to-power-loss objective is also reported, because the modulation pathway takes effect only within it. On the MagNet final A-E material protocol, CAHR-Net attains an average p95 relative error of 6.89% with only 1874 parameters, the lowest among all compared methods, together with a lower worst-material p95 than the strongest black-box solution at about 48x fewer parameters; it reduces the average p95 of the physical reconstruction backbone from 7.47% to 6.89% and the p95 of material D, the most difficult material, from 16.40% to 14.87%. Ablation and condition-slice analyses attribute the improvement to the coupling of physical loop reconstruction, structured condition modulation, and the matched optimization trajectory.

</details>


### [160] [Train What You Deploy: Closing the MLP Reachability Gap in Low-Rank Clone Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02006)
*Wenhui Chen,Zhifeng Li,Jie Zhou,Navan Preet Singh,Madalina Ciobanu,Chenghua Wang,Qingqing Mao,Ritankar Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"训练你所部署的"原则，指出权重继承蒸馏器LRC存在部署矩阵与可训练权重重叠度低的问题（62.5-81.4%的自由度不可达），通过Dense-LRC与CORE-LRC两种可合并实现，使训练目标覆盖完整部署矩阵，在不改变部署形态、参数量和推理FLOPs的前提下恢复被搁置的容量。实验显示在三个教师模型上分别获得+2.36/+2.71/+10.45 Avg9提升，Qwen教师下达到约20B token精度仅需10B token（2倍token效率）。约10B蒸馏token加短SFT后，半参数的1.5B学生匹配其9T-token教师，2.7B学生在~900倍更少压缩token下超越Meta官方Llama3.1-8B压缩结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有权重继承蒸馏（如LRC）存在"部署-训练"形态不一致的根本缺陷：推理时部署全宽学生MLP矩阵，但训练仅触及教师诱导的切片，导致大量（62.5-81.4%）线性自由度虽在推理时付费却永远无法被训练优化，造成容量浪费与训练效率低下。

Method: 提出核心原则"train what you deploy"（训练你所部署的）。基于相同的LRC热启动，将训练对象扩展为整个部署矩阵，而非教师诱导的切片，同时不改变部署形态、参数数与推理FLOPs。具体通过两种可实现方案实现：Dense-LRC与CORE-LRC，二者在推理时都能合并坍缩为单个部署权重。训练过程中所有参数均可更新，使可达权重族与部署权重族完全一致。

Result: 在Llama3.2-3B、Llama3.1-8B、Qwen2.5-3B三个教师上，取每教师更强方案，相对匹配预算的plain-LRC基线分别取得+2.36/+2.71/+10.45 Avg9提升；Qwen（最宽教师）上增益最大，以10B token达到原始约20B token精度（2倍token效率），同谱系对照仍恢复+6.39。约10B token+短SFT后：1.5B半参数学生匹配9T-token教师的9任务宏平均；2.7B学生以~900倍更少压缩token超越Meta官方Llama3.1-8B压缩。对照实验支持增益归因于可达集扩大而非额外参数或配方。

Conclusion: 本文核心结论是：权重继承蒸馏的性能瓶颈不在于参数数量的多少，而在于"部署什么就必须训练什么"。当可训练权重族与部署权重族不一致时，大量已付费的推理容量无法被利用。通过在训练中触及完整部署矩阵（而非教师切片），可显著恢复搁置容量，带来实打实的精度与token效率提升，甚至能在极小压缩预算下匹敌或超越官方压缩方案。

Abstract: A compressed student has two shapes that need not agree: the weight it deploys at inference and the weight family its training can reach. We show that a state-of-the-art weight-inheritance distiller, Low-Rank Clone (LRC), deploys a full-width student MLP but ties training to a teacher-induced slice, leaving 62.5-81.4% of each deployed matrix's independent linear degrees of freedom unreachable-paid for at inference, never trainable. Our principle is one line: train what you deploy. From the identical LRC warm start, we make the training object the entire deployed matrix, with no change in deployed shape, deployed parameter count, or inference FLOPs, via two mergeable realizations (Dense-LRC and CORE-LRC) that both collapse to one deployed weight. This recovers stranded capacity: taking the stronger realization per teacher, +2.36/+2.71/+10.45 Avg9 over matched-budget plain-LRC baselines across three teachers (Llama3.2-3B, Llama3.1-8B, Qwen2.5-3B), with the largest gain on the widest teacher (Qwen), where it reaches the original recipe's approx. 20B-token accuracy at 10B tokens (2x token efficiency); there the strictly same-lineage arm still recovers +6.39, the fully controlled figure. Controls strongly support attributing the gain to the enlarged reachable set, rather than to added parameters or the recipe. From approx. 10B distillation tokens plus a short SFT, a half-parameter 1.5B student matches its approx. 9T-token teacher's 9-task macro-average, within evaluation noise and with a residual MMLU deficit, and a 2.7B student beats Meta's own official compression of Llama3.1-8B at ~900x fewer compression tokens (a token count under unmatched recipes, not a compute claim). All results are from single-seed runs on the LRC backbone.

</details>


### [161] [Source-Free Class Relearning: Diagnosing Forgetting in Class Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02018)
*Zahra Dehghani,Pablo Piantanida,Mohammadhadi Shateri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种严格源无关（source-free）的类别重学习审计方法SFRA，仅使用未学习后的模型即可恢复被遗忘类别，并引入重学习分数（RS）来量化可恢复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类别遗忘学习的目标是让模型无法识别指定的遗忘类别，但低遗忘精度并不等于类别结构已被真正抹除。现有的类别恢复方法都需要真实遗忘/保留样本、辅助数据或参考检查点，尚未有研究在严格源无关场景下探索类别是否可通过仅更新分类器头就被恢复，这关系到对遗忘学习有效性的核心质疑。

Method: 首先建立理论分析，证明在满足特定对齐条件下，对合成探针集执行单步梯度更新即可提高遗忘类别的期望logit margin。基于此提出白盒SFRA框架：在表征空间中生成候选嵌入，使用模型引导的置信度过滤来构造高置信度的保留探针和低置信度的边界邻近探针（将其重新标注为遗忘类别），默认采用高斯采样和Softmax置信度，并通过消融实验验证可恢复性不依赖于特定选择。最后引入重学习分数RS，联合衡量遗忘类别恢复程度和保留类别性能保持，并报告相对于重训练参考的类别匹配ΔRS。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和TinyImageNet上使用ResNet-18、ViT-B/16和Swin-T的实验表明，多种遗忘学习方法展现出显著的源无关可恢复性，且部分方法的可恢复性甚至超过了匹配的重训练参考模型。

Conclusion: 研究发现现有的近似遗忘方法在源无关场景下仍可能保留可恢复的类别结构，这提示类别遗忘的有效性需要更严格的审计标准，SFRA可作为评估遗忘质量的补充工具。

Abstract: Class unlearning aims to remove a model's ability to recognize designated forget classes while preserving performance on retain classes. However, low forget accuracy after unlearning does not necessarily mean the class structure has been erased. Approximate unlearning methods can alter classifier decision boundaries while leaving recoverable structure in the representation. Prior work has shown that forget classes can be recovered, but existing approaches require real forget or retain samples, auxiliary data, or reference checkpoints. We study class relearning in a strictly source-free setting, asking whether a forget class can be recovered through a classifier-head update using only the unlearned model. Our approach rests on a theoretical analysis establishing a sufficient alignment condition under which a single gradient step on a synthetic probe set increases the expected logit margin of the forget class. Building on this, we propose a white-box Source-Free Relearning Audit (SFRA), which generates candidate embeddings in representation space and uses model-guided confidence filtering to construct high-confidence retain probes and low-confidence boundary-adjacent probes that are relabelled as the forget class. Gaussian sampling and Softmax confidence are used by default, while ablations with alternative proposal distributions and uncertainty criteria show that recoverability is not specific to these choices. To quantify recoverability, we introduce the Relearning Score (RS), which jointly measures forget-class recovery and retain-accuracy preservation, and report class-matched $Δ$RS relative to a retrained reference. Experiments on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and TinyImageNet with ResNet-18, ViT-B/16, and Swin-T show that several unlearning methods exhibit substantial source-free recoverability, and that for a subset of methods this recoverability exceeds the matched retrained reference.

</details>


### [162] [Act More, Decide Less: Skill-Guided Adaptive Action Chunking for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02042)
*Yanting Yang,Can Jin,Jinman Zhao,Jiahao Wu,Yang Zhou,Zhepeng Wang,Zhendong Wang,Mu Zhou,Dimitris N. Metaxas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPACE通过从轨迹诱导的程序化技能中蒸馏块边界监督，训练变长动作块策略，在长时程交互任务中显著提升成功率并大幅减少LLM决策轮数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ReAct式LLM智能体每轮只发出一个原始动作，虽支持频繁重规划，但在长时程任务中大量轮次浪费在常规动作序列上，效率低下。变长动作块是自然替代方案，但标准强化学习训练会失败——要么退化为单动作行为，要么过度承诺过长的动作序列，根本原因在于无法学习块边界。

Method: 提出SPACE框架：(1) 从成功轨迹中诱导两级程序化技能，子技能边界作为直接的块边界监督信号；(2) 通过混合on-policy/off-policy优化与块感知的信用分配，将时间结构蒸馏到原始-块策略中。

Result: 在ALFWorld和ScienceWorld实验中，SPACE相比各场景最强基线将成功率提升7.0%-31.3%，同时将平均LLM决策轮数减少最多78.9%。

Conclusion: 显式建模并监督动作块边界是提升LLM智能体长时程任务效率与性能的关键，SPACE通过技能蒸馏有效解决了块学习中的核心难题。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents for long-horizon interactive tasks typically follow a ReAct-style protocol, issuing one primitive action per LLM round. While this enables frequent replanning, it is inefficient for long-horizon tasks where many rounds are spent on routine action sequences. A natural alternative is to let the agent emit variable-length action chunks. However, naively training such policies with standard reinforcement learning fails: the agent either collapses to single-action behavior or over-commits to excessively long sequences. Both failures share a common root cause: the inability to learn chunk boundaries. We propose SPACE, which addresses this challenge by distilling chunk-boundary supervision from trajectory-induced programmatic skills. We induce two-level programmatic skills from successful trajectories, where subskill boundaries serve as direct chunk-boundary supervision. This temporal structure is then distilled into a primitive-chunk policy via hybrid on-/off-policy optimization with chunk-aware credit assignment. Experiments on ALFWorld and ScienceWorld show that SPACE improves success rates by 7.0%-31.3% over the strongest baseline in each setting while reducing average LLM decision rounds by up to 78.9%.

</details>


### [163] [The Dynamics of Continuous Mixture Collapse in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02049)
*Ali Backour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过理论分析和受控实验研究了LLM在潜在状态推理中为何无法保持混合状态（如token嵌入的加权混合）。识别出三个独立且不同的失败来源：架构扭曲与训练放大、softmax读出与自回归反馈构成的动力系统导致的收缩或放大、以及多组件混合的精确保持需要随组件数增长的上下文依赖修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的潜在状态推理方法（用连续状态替代离散中间token）在理论上很有吸引力，因为它能保留多种推理方向而非过早承诺单一方向。然而预训练语言模型在实际中往往无法保持这些混合状态，理解其失败机制对于改进潜在推理方法至关重要。

Method: 论文采用理论分析与受控实证研究相结合的方法：1）从理论上分析transformer架构对混合几何的扭曲，并考察训练如何放大这种效应；2）将softmax读出与自回归反馈建模为动力系统，推导收缩与放大之间的理论阈值，并用实验验证；3）将分析推广到多组件混合场景，研究精确保持所需的条件复杂度。

Result: 研究发现：1）transformer架构本身会扭曲混合几何，且训练显著放大了该效应；2）即使模型完美线性传输混合，softmax读出与自回归反馈形成的动力系统也会导致混合收缩（不同混合变得不可区分）或放大（一种成分主导），实证观察到的转换点接近理论推导的阈值，预训练模型的rollout主要处于放大侧；3）多组件混合的精确保持通常需要上下文依赖的修正，所需维度可能随组件数增长。

Conclusion: 论文系统性地阐明了LLM潜在状态推理失败的三个独立根源，为理解现有方法的局限提供了理论基础，也为未来设计更能保持混合状态的模型架构或推理策略指明了方向——特别是在处理多组件混合时需要考虑可扩展性权衡。

Abstract: LLMs latent-state reasoning methods replace discrete intermediate tokens with continuous states, such as weighted mixtures of token embeddings, to retain multiple possible reasoning directions rather than committing to one. Yet pretrained language models often fail to preserve these mixtures. We study why through a combination of theoretical analysis and controlled empirical investigations on a variety of models. We identify three independent, distinct sources of failure. First, transformer architectures already distort mixture geometry, and training substantially amplifies this effect. Moreover, the failure can occur even if the model transports mixtures perfectly linearly: the softmax readout and autoregressive feedback form a dynamical system that either amplifies small differences until one component of the mixture dominates or contracts different mixtures until they become indistinguishable. We verify this theoretical prediction empirically: the observed transition between contraction and amplification occurs near the theoretical threshold derived by our analysis, and pretrained-model rollouts lie predominantly on the amplifying side. Finally, we generalize to mixtures of many components and show that exact preservation generally requires context-dependent correction, whose required dimensionality can grow with the number of components.

</details>


### [164] [DynG-Diff: A State-Aware Dynamic Guidance Diffusion Framework for Probabilistic Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02068)
*Zhente Zhang,Zhengwei Ni,Wei Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DynG-Diff提出了一种变量敏感的动态引导扩散框架，用于概率多元时间序列预测，通过无条件扩散骨干网络和轻量级状态感知策略网络来解决信息异质性（变量间噪声水平和演化模式差异）问题，在真实基准上取得了有竞争力的预测性能并在严重观测损坏下表现出更强的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于扩散的多元时间序列预测方法依赖任务特定的条件范式，缺乏灵活性，难以处理变量间显著不同的噪声水平和演化模式这一"信息异质性"问题。

Method: (1) 采用两阶段分离训练策略，使用无条件扩散骨干建模多元时间序列的联合分布；(2) 引入轻量级状态感知策略网络，从实时噪声状态和一步去噪估计中自适应推断变量可靠性，输出动态引导强度矩阵；(3) 将该动态权重数学形式化为观测分布的局部精度，实现对高置信变量的精确引导，同时过滤异常噪声干扰。

Result: 在真实世界基准上的广泛实验表明，该方法在概率预测性能上与最先进的条件扩散模型具有竞争力，并且在严重观测损坏场景下表现出改进的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DynG-Diff通过变量级别动态引导机制有效解决了时间序列预测中的信息异质性挑战，提供了一种更灵活、鲁棒的扩散框架，代码已开源。

Abstract: Probabilistic multivariate time series (MTS) forecasting is crucial for modeling complex dynamical systems. However, existing diffusion-based methods rely on task-specific conditional paradigms that lack flexibility and struggle with inherent "information heterogeneity"--the significantly varying noise levels and evolutionary patterns across variables. To address this, we propose DynG-Diff, a variable-sensitive dynamic guidance diffusion framework for probabilistic multivariate time-series forecasting: (1) DynG-Diff adopts a two-stage separated training strategy and uses an unconditional diffusion backbone to model the joint distribution of multivariate time series. (2) DynG-Diff introduces a lightweight state-aware policy network that adaptively infers variable reliability from real-time noisy states and one-step denoising estimates, outputting a dynamic guidance strength matrix. (3) DynG-Diff mathematically formulates this dynamic weight as the local precision of the observation distribution, enabling precise guidance for high-confidence variables during inference while filtering out interference from anomalous noise. Extensive experiments on real-world benchmarks demonstrate competitive probabilistic forecasting performance against state-of-the-art conditional diffusion models and improved robustness under severe observation corruption.The implementation code is available at: https://github.com/TT-20011031/DynG-Diff

</details>


### [165] [XMerge: Cross-Axis Selection and Reconstructive Layer Merging for LLM Depth Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02083)
*Jundong Hu,Shekar Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: XMerge是一种后训练深度压缩方法，通过跨轴选择和局部边界重建来移除完整Transformer层，在保持标准服务架构的同时，在Llama和Qwen等多个骨干网络上取得了优于现有基线方法的压缩质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移除完整的Transformer层可以保持标准服务架构，但现有的深度压缩方法会导致显著的质量损失，且损失在不同模型间变化不可预测。因此需要一种更稳健、质量损失更小的层压缩方法。

Method: XMerge包含两个核心组件：(1) 跨轴选择（Cross-axis selection）：识别具有低相对幅度和低角度隐藏状态变化（即两个选择轴一致）的块；(2) 局部边界重建（Local boundary reconstruction）：重新拟合相邻的幸存块以匹配原始两块的输出。该方法无需任务标签或端到端微调，不引入架构变化或额外推理时参数。

Result: 在七个Llama和Qwen骨干网络（0.5B-8B）、五个已发表基线和三个层缩减级别上，XMerge在最激进的移除（k=4）时优势最大：在CORE（22任务聚合）上七个骨干中六个排名第一，在MMLU上七个中六个排名第一（同时两个均为第一的有五个）。在14个（模型、配置）单元中是唯一从未崩溃的操作符，且在零样本和上下文学习两种配置中均排名前二。消融实验表明局部重建贡献了大部分收益。

Conclusion: XMerge是一种稳健且高质量的深度压缩方法，其局部重建是主要增益来源，跨轴融合在两个选择轴不一致时提供帮助。虽然构建成本存在，但通过每token的解码节省在约数万次请求后即可收回。

Abstract: Removing complete transformer layers preserves a standard serving architecture, but existing depth-compression methods can lose substantial quality, and the loss varies unpredictably across models. We introduce XMerge, a post-training method with two components. Cross-axis selection identifies a block with low relative-magnitude and angular hidden-state change, and local boundary reconstruction re-fits the adjacent surviving block to match the original two-block output. XMerge uses no task labels or end-to-end fine-tuning, and it introduces neither architectural changes nor additional inference-time parameters. Across seven Llama and Qwen backbones (0.5B-8B), five published baselines, and three layer-reduction levels, its advantage over baselines is largest at the most aggressive removal: at k=4 it ranks first on six of seven backbones on CORE (a 22-task aggregate) and, separately, on six of seven on MMLU (five of seven on both at once), while avoiding the large perplexity increases of several competing operators. In a task-level bootstrap, the 95% confidence intervals for the three largest CORE margins exclude zero; the remaining margins are consistent with ties. Across the 14 (model, regime) cells it is also the only evaluated operator that never collapses, ranking top-2 in both zero-shot and in-context regimes; on a first calibration probe (one backbone) it is the best-calibrated operator. Ablations show that local reconstruction provides most of the gain, while cross-axis fusion helps when the two selection axes disagree. The additional construction cost is recovered through per-token decode savings after roughly tens of thousands of requests.

</details>


### [166] [TC-Next: Zero-Shot Multimodal Cyclone Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02085)
*Zhe Wang,Sijie Chen,Yiming Luo,Daehyun Kim,Chien-Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TropicalCycloneNext (TC-Next)，一个多模态深度学习模型，利用基础模型（如GraphCast）生成的大气运动学和热力学场预测以及GridSat红外卫星图像，在6-24小时提前期内预测热带气旋路径和强度。该模型仅在西太平洋GraphCast预测上训练，但其误差比传统基于规则的追踪器TempestExtremes降低15%-44%（路径）和3-6倍（强度），并且无需重新训练即可泛化到Pangu-Weather和IFS HRES的预测场上。研究还展示了零样本迁移能力和多模态融合带来的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统热带气旋追踪方法基于规则（如TempestExtremes），在利用现代气象基础模型（如GraphCast、Pangu-Weather）生成的高分辨率大气预测场时，追踪精度不足。现有方法未能充分利用多种数据模态（大气场+卫星图像）来提高路径和强度预测的准确性。因此需要一种多模态深度学习模型，能够嵌入基础模型预测场，提升热带气旋追踪性能。

Method: TC-Next采用多模态深度学习架构：(1) 输入包括基础模型（GraphCast）预测的大气运动学和热力学场（通用气象变量），以及GridSat红外卫星图像；(2) 模型仅在西太平洋的GraphCast预测上训练；(3) 通过深度融合两种模态信息，联合预测热带气旋的路径（track）和强度（intensity）。模型设计强调使用通用大气变量以确保跨模型的迁移能力。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 在GraphCast输入下，TC-Next相较TempestExtremes将路径误差降低15%-44%，强度误差降低3-6倍；(2) 无重新训练直接应用于Pangu-Weather和IFS HRES预测场时，性能仍优于TempestExtremes；(3) 对WeatherNext Cyclones在2025年西太平洋季节的气象场进行零样本应用时，在所有提前期的强度误差更低，路径误差更低或相当；(4) 消融研究表明多模态融合在所有提前期改善追踪误差，在较长提前期改善强度预测。

Conclusion: TC-Next证明了基于通用大气变量的多模态深度追踪模型能够有效嵌入不同气象基础模型的预测场，实现优于传统基于规则追踪器的性能，并且具有良好的跨模型泛化能力和零样本迁移能力。多模态信息融合是提升热带气旋路径和强度预测的关键设计。

Abstract: We present TropicalCycloneNext (TC-Next), a multimodal deep learning model that forecasts tropical cyclone track and intensity at $6$-$24$ h leads by leveraging a foundation model's forecast fields of atmospheric kinematic and thermodynamic fields and GridSat infrared satellite imagery. Trained only on GraphCast forecasts over the Western Pacific (WP), yet reliant only on generic atmospheric variables, TC-Next on GraphCast lowers track error by $15$-$44\%$ and intensity error by a factor of $3$-$6$ relative to a conventional, rule-based tracker, TempestExtremes; applied without retraining to the forecast fields of Pangu-Weather and IFS HRES, it stays ahead of TempestExtremes on both. Applied zero-shot to the generic weather fields of WeatherNext Cyclones on the 2025 WP season, TC-Next attains lower intensity error at every lead time, and lower or comparable track error, compared to that model's specialized direct tracker in a deterministic comparison. Our ablation studies show that our multimodal model is able to utilize the additional modality to improve performance in tracking errors at every lead time and in intensity prediction at longer lead times.

</details>


### [167] [Compositional Spectral Prompts for LLM-based Online Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02093)
*Seungyoon Choi,Hyunchul Kim,Jae-Gil Lee,Chanyoung Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoSPOT是一个基于预训练大语言模型的在线时间序列预测框架，通过冻结LLM并使用基于频域基的合成频谱提示（compositional spectral prompts）实现高效在线适应。它将时间序列分解为频率基并按振幅组合提示，使未见模式可表示为已学习基提示的新组合，在长时间在线阶段和跨数据集分布偏移场景中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线时间序列预测方法主要依赖基于内存缓冲区的检索策略来适应非平稳环境，但这类框架在长期适应方面存在困难，并且无法泛化到未见过的模式，需要更鲁棒的在线预测方案。

Method: CoSPOT采用预训练LLM作为在线预测骨干并保持其冻结状态，将时间序列分解为频率基，根据幅度组合相应的频谱基提示（spectral basis prompts）来引导模型理解输入的整体分布，从而大幅减少在线阶段需要更新的参数数量。未见模式可通过已学习基提示的新组合来表示，支持低频更新与高效适应。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，CoSPOT在挑战性在线场景中（包括长在线阶段和存在显著分布偏移的跨数据集设置）展现出优越性和实用性。

Conclusion: CoSPOT验证了利用冻结LLM配合组合式频谱提示是解决在线时间序列预测长期适应与泛化问题的有效途径，代码已公开。
