Conclusion: 模型互补性的存在展示了组合多个模型的潜在价值，但其快速饱和和自我限制暴露了当前链接预测模型的集体局限。这强调需要进一步研究以构建更鲁棒的Web应用，同时提示仅靠简单模型组合可能无法完全超越个体模型的性能天花板。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs have become an important source of structured knowledge for Web applications, including search, question answering, and recommender systems. In these applications, link prediction can serve either as a prediction task itself or as a means to enrich incomplete knowledge graphs for downstream tasks. Interestingly, different link prediction models, or even different training runs of the same model, can produce substantially different predictions for the same query. This suggests a variability in the capture of the underlying knowledge by models, thus raising a fundamental question: to what extent do different models capture complementary knowledge, and how much of this knowledge could be recovered by combining them? We propose to measure model complementarity through the performance of an oracle that, for each query, selects the best prediction among a considered set of models, hence providing an upper bound on the performance achievable through model combination. Across several architectures and benchmarks, we find a substantial gap between individual models and their oracle, revealing that different models capture complementary knowledge. Yet, this complementarity rapidly saturates as more models are added, leaving a persistent subset of queries unsolved even by a large number of models. These findings reveal both the potential of model complementarity and a fundamental limit to what current link prediction models can collectively recover; thereby highlighting the need for further research to build robust Web applications.

</details>


### [202] [Unfolding the Leech Lattice: Fused Multi-Shell Decoding and VRAM Layouts for 2-Bit LLM Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02652)
*Pier-Jean Malandrino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提供了Leech-lattice矢量量化所需的multi-shell解码器的首个实现，并测量了其在batch 1下decode-phase GEMV的serving成本。结果表明：在VRAM内码率是可独立于磁盘码率的设计轴；decode阶段trellis kernel比served lattice layout读取更少字节且运行更快；但lattice方法在质量上付出代价（4B时1.38x困惑度、14.7 MMLU点），且随规模增大代价缩小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Leech-lattice矢量量化在其自身评估协议下拥有最强的2-bit质量报告，但其kernel仅解码一个shell，而rate所要求的multi-shell decoder没有现成实现。作者希望填补这一空白，并测量其在真实serving环境（decode-phase GEMV, batch 1）下的成本。

Method: 1) 为完整301-class码本构建serving路径：离线扩展到GPU布局，用fused dequantize-plus-matvec kernel读取且无warp divergence，用f64验证。2) 将in-VRAM rate与on-disk rate区分为独立设计轴，在同一进程中测试四种bit-exact布局。3) 在同一进程中部署4-bit(AWQ)与2-bit(QTIP) GEMV kernel作为对比基线。4) 通过no-weights控制实验确立launch geometry的性能下限，并在第二套memory hierarchy上验证。5) 保持output head一致或使用int8 head进行端到端对比。

Result: binary bit planes在恒定带宽下优于one-hot masks（4.80 bits/weight，较FP16加速2.15x）。Trellis kernel比served layout少读2.40x字节且快2.27x，时间差距与流量差距一致。端到端在4B/8B/14B上获得1.11x/1.29x/1.41x增益；int8 head下served 4B达87.0 tok/s（2.60 GB）。质量代价为4B时1.38x困惑度与14.7 MMLU点，且随规模缩小。

Conclusion: Lattice矢量量化在极低bit率下需要付出显著serving开销——码本过大无法放入lookup table，导致decode性能受限于traffic而非kernel本身。Trellis方法在流量与速度上均占优。不过lattice的质量代价随模型规模扩大而缩小，提示其在更大规模模型上可能更具竞争力。

Abstract: Leech-lattice vector quantization holds the strongest reported 2-bit quality under its own evaluation protocol. Its kernel decodes one shell; we found no implementation of the multi-shell decoder the rate requires. This paper supplies one and measures its serving cost for decode-phase GEMV at batch 1. First, a serving path for the full 301-class codebook: an offline expansion into GPU layouts and a fused dequantize-plus-matvec kernel reading them without warp divergence, verified against f64. Second, the in-VRAM rate is a design axis distinct from the on-disk rate. Four bit-exact layouts timed in one process show binary bit planes beating one-hot masks on size and speed at constant bandwidth (4.80 bits per weight, 2.15x FP16). Below 4.3 bits a second, irregular stream enters; at 3.6 the decode stops being shifts and masks. Third, deployed four-bit (AWQ) and two-bit (QTIP) GEMV kernels run in the same process. The trellis kernel reads 2.40x fewer bytes than our served layout and runs 2.27x faster at near-equal fractions of their byte bounds: the time gap tracks the traffic gap, the price of unfolding a codebook too large for a lookup table. Fourth, the validity envelope: the trellis kernel outruns our no-weights control, so our launch geometry sets that floor, and on a second memory hierarchy every lattice arm falls below FP16. With the output head held identical across arms, the kernel-and-format path gains 1.11x, 1.29x and 1.41x end to end at 4B, 8B and 14B; with an int8 output head the served 4B reaches 87.0 tok/s in 2.60 GB. The quality cost, 1.38x perplexity and 14.7 MMLU points at 4B, shrinks across the three sizes measured.

</details>


### [203] [H3DNAS: Hardware-Aware ONNX-Native 3D Point Cloud Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02684)
*Anchit Mulye,Rhythm Baghel,Sujay Kumar Ingle,Hardik Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: H3DNAS是一个直接作用于ONNX计算图的硬件感知模型压缩框架，无需访问原始源代码、架构类定义或梯度信息。它通过通道依赖图（CDG）证明自由参数比例是拓扑不变量，结合两阶段层次搜索和GhostConv结构突变，在ModelNet40上分别将PointNet、PointNet++和PointMLP的参数减少65.5%、43.2%和49.1%，并实现1.99倍、1.29倍和1.67倍的推理加速，精度损失可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano等边缘硬件上部署3D点云模型受限于算力和内存预算。现有压缩方法需要访问模型原始源代码，无法应用于厂商和模型仓库中广泛分发的ONNX二进制文件，因此需要一种无需源代码即可进行模型压缩的框架。

Method: (1) 通道依赖图（CDG）：将ONNX算子分为四类约束类，形式化证明自由参数比例ρ_f是拓扑不变量，可在O(|V|+|E|)时间内计算可证明的压缩上限；(2) 两阶段层次搜索：通过L1重要性通道选择剪枝候选架构，以输出保真度作为零样本无标签代理进行排序，并对Pareto最优候选应用GhostConv结构突变；(3) 全程基于ONNX图手术操作，无需原始架构定义。

Result: 在ModelNet40数据集上，PointNet、PointNet++和PointMLP分别减少了65.5%、43.2%和49.1%的参数，推理速度分别提升1.99倍、1.29倍和1.67倍，且准确率损失可忽略。

Conclusion: H3DNAS是首个面向3D点云模型的无源代码压缩流水线，完全通过ONNX图手术实现，为边缘部署场景下的二进制模型压缩提供了新路径。

Abstract: Deploying 3D point cloud models on edge hardware such as the NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano is severely constrained by compute and memory budgets. Existing compression methods require access to the model's original source code, rendering them inapplicable to the Open Neural Network Exchange (ONNX) binaries commonly distributed by vendors and model repositories. We present \textbf{H3DNAS}, a hardware-aware model compression framework that operates directly on ONNX computational graphs without requiring original source code, architecture class definition, or gradient access during search. H3DNAS makes three contributions: (1) a \textbf{Channel Dependency Graph (CDG)} that classifies ONNX operators into four constraint classes and formally establishes that the free parameter fraction $ρ_f$ is topological invariant, a provable compression ceiling computable in $\mathcal{O}(|V|+|E|)$; (2) a \textbf{Two-Stage Hierarchical Search} that prunes candidate architectures by $L_1$-importance channel selection, ranks them by output fidelity as a zero-shot label-free proxy, and applies GhostConv structural mutation to Pareto-optimal candidates; and (3) the \textbf{first source-code-free compression pipeline for 3D point cloud models}, operating entirely via ONNX graph surgery with no original architecture definition required. On ModelNet40, H3DNAS reduces the number of parameters in PointNet, PointNet++, and PointMLP by $65.5\%$, $43.2\%$, and $49.1\%$, respectively, while achieving $1.99\times$, $1.29\times$, and $1.67\times$ inference speedups with negligible loss in accuracy. The source code is publicly available\footnote{https://github.com/ClarityLab-Org/h3dnas}.

</details>


### [204] [LoRA-TSD: Tangent-Space Spectral Descent for LoRA via Muon-Style Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02734)
*Dmitrii Andriianov,Andrey Veprikov,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 LoRA-TSD，一种基于流形优化的 LoRA 优化器。它将每个 LoRA 更新步骤视为固定秩矩阵流形上的切向量，在切空间内执行 Muon 的谱范数最速下降，并通过 LoRA 参数化固有的回缩映射回因子矩阵。该方法避免了全权重矩阵的昂贵运算，回缩运算比截断 SVD 便宜达 2.8 倍。作者证明其 Frobenius 范数版本可恢复 LoRA-Pro，并给出了首个基于切投影梯度（流形的黎曼梯度）的全局收敛保证。在 Llama-3.2-1B、Llama-3.1-8B 和 Qwen3-32B 上跨越六个常识和自然语言推理基准，LoRA-TSD 优于所有对比的 LoRA 优化器，且对适配器秩保持鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 LoRA 微调中两个因子矩阵被独立训练，忽略了其诱导的低秩权重更新所处的流形几何结构。此前基于流形的方法（如使用截断 SVD 回缩）计算开销大，限制了实用性。作者旨在设计一种既尊重低秩流形几何、又计算高效的 LoRA 优化器。

Method: (1) 将每个 LoRA 更新步骤建模为固定秩矩阵流形的切向量；(2) 在该切空间内执行 Muon 的谱范数最速下降步骤；(3) 通过 LoRA 参数化内在的回缩（retraction）将结果映射回因子矩阵，该回缩比截断 SVD 回缩便宜至多 2.8 倍；(4) 识别切投影梯度即黎曼梯度作为 LoRA 训练自然的平稳性度量，可由因子梯度直接计算；(5) 推导 LoRA-Pro 与 LoRA-TSD 的全局收敛保证，收敛率驱动因子梯度范数趋于零。

Result: 在六个常识推理和自然语言推理基准上，使用 Llama-3.2-1B、Llama-3.1-8B 和 Qwen3-32B 三种模型规模，LoRA-TSD 在性能上超越所有对比的 LoRA 优化器，并对适配器秩的变化保持鲁棒性。此外，回缩操作的计算效率显著优于先前的流形方法。

Conclusion: LoRA-TSD 证明了将流形几何纳入 LoRA 优化既能提升性能又不牺牲效率，是可扩展且实用的低秩微调优化方案。其理论收敛保证和与 LoRA-Pro 的联系为该领域提供了更坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) is the standard way to fine-tune large models, yet when its two factors are trained independently, the update ignores the geometry of the low-rank weight change it induces. We introduce LoRA-TSD, an optimizer that treats every LoRA step as a tangent vector of the fixed-rank matrix manifold and takes the spectral-norm steepest-descent step of Muon inside that tangent space, mapping the result back to the factors through a retraction native to the LoRA parametrization. The step avoids expensive operations on full weight matrices, and its retraction is up to $2.8\times$ cheaper than the truncated-SVD retraction used by prior manifold methods. We prove that the Frobenius-norm version of our surrogate recovers LoRA-Pro, and we identify the tangent-projected gradient, the Riemannian gradient of the manifold, as the stationarity measure natural to LoRA training and computable from the factor gradients alone. Under this measure we give the first global convergence guarantees for both LoRA-Pro and LoRA-TSD, with rates that drive the factor-gradient norms to zero. Across six commonsense and natural-language-inference benchmarks with Llama-3.2-1B, Llama-3.1-8B and Qwen3-32B, LoRA-TSD outperforms every competing LoRA optimizer and stays robust to the adapter rank. Code is available at https://github.com/brain-lab-research/LoRA-TSD.

</details>


### [205] [Do Tabular Foundation Models Know Physics? Contamination, Units, and the Deterministic Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02766)
*Wassim Tenachi,Yashar Hezaveh,Laurence Perreault Levasseur,Pierre-Luc Bacon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了四种表格基础模型（TFMs）是否在填充表格的过程中学习到了物理学知识。通过在316个物理方程采样的数据集上进行测试，TFMs在所有基线上表现优异，但研究发现它们的先验无法表示无噪声机制和物理单位，因此只能内插物理规律而无法充当真正的物理模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格是大多数物理测量数据的存储格式，表格基础模型（TFMs）在填充表格时可能隐式学习了物理规律。由于TFMs本质上是贝叶斯模型，其先验内容决定了它们对物理的理解程度，因此需要直接探究其先验是否包含物理学知识。

Method: 作者直接探测TFM的先验，评估四种模型（TabPFN-3、TabICLv2、TabDPT和Real-TabPFN-2.5），将其与六个基线模型进行比较，使用从316个物理方程中采样的数据集进行域内和域外测试。评估涵盖开箱即用和调优后的表现。

Result: TFMs在域内和域外测试中均显著优于所有基线，无论是在开箱即用还是在调优后。然而，研究发现这些模型的先验无法表示无噪声的确定性机制，也无法编码物理单位信息。

Conclusion: 尽管TFMs在物理数据上表现优异，能够有效地内插物理规律，但它们的先验结构限制使其目前无法作为真正的物理模型使用——无法处理无噪声机制和物理单位概念，这揭示了TFMs在物理建模能力上的根本局限。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) learn to fill in tables the way language models fill in text, and tables are arguably the format in which most physical measurement arrives. Did they learn any physics in the process? They are Bayesian by construction, so the question is what their prior contains. We probe it directly, evaluating four of them (TabPFN-3, TabICLv2, TabDPT and Real-TabPFN-2.5) against six baselines on datasets sampled from 316 physical equations, in and out of domain. TFMs dominate, out of the box and after tuning. But we show that their prior can represent neither a noiseless mechanism nor physical units, which is why they interpolate physics without yet being able to act as physical models.

</details>


### [206] [Cliff: Learning Process Rewards from the First Mistake](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02817)
*Peixuan Han,Runhui Wang,Ketan Ramaneti,Jie Hao,Gerald Friedland,Chris Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Cliff是一种奖励塑形策略，利用现成的LLM教师识别每个rollout中的第一个错误，将推理过程自然分解为正确前缀和错误后缀，并在token级别分配正负优势信号，从而为RLVR提供更细粒度的中间过程监督。实验表明该方法在12种场景下优于on-policy distillation 15%和标准GRPO 7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR虽然是大语言模型后训练的强大范式，但依赖粗粒度的结果奖励，无法为中间推理过程提供充分指导。现有改进方法（如过程奖励建模、on-policy蒸馏）引入额外约束，如需要专门的奖励模型，或假设师生推理模式一致。

Method: 作者观察到一旦推理过程首次出错，后续推理评估信息价值有限（因为它基于无效前缀）。基于此，Cliff利用现成的LLM作为教师，识别每个rollout中的第一个错误位置，将rollout自然分解为正确前缀和错误后缀两部分，然后将这一信号转化为token级优势——对正确前缀赋予正优势，对错误后缀赋予负反馈。

Result: 在12个不同场景的实验中，Cliff一致性地提升了推理性能：相比on-policy distillation提升15%，相比标准GRPO提升7%，即使使用能力一般的教师也能取得良好效果。作者还分析了"ground truth"在Cliff中的作用并研究了其训练动态。

Conclusion: Cliff被证明是一种简单、通用且有效的RLVR改进方法，通过利用"首个错误识别"机制提供更丰富、细粒度的监督信号，突破了现有方法对专门奖励模型或师生同构假设的依赖。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for large language model (LLM) post-training, but its reliance on coarse outcome rewards leads to limited guidance on intermediate reasoning processes. Existing approaches such as process reward modeling and on-policy distillation introduce additional constraints, such as reliance on a specialized reward model or assuming identical reasoning patterns between teacher and student. Nevertheless, we observe that once a reasoning process first goes wrong, evaluating the subsequent reasoning provides limited additional information, as it is already conditioned on an invalid prefix. Therefore, we propose Cliff, a reward shaping strategy that utilizes an off-the-shelf LLM as a teacher to identify the first mistake in each rollout. As a result, the rollout is naturally decomposed into two parts: a correct prefix and an incorrect suffix. Cliff then converts this signal into token-level advantages, assigning positive advantages for the correct prefix and negative feedback afterward. Experiments across 12 different scenarios demonstrate that Cliff consistently improves reasoning performance, outperforming on-policy distillation by 15% and standard GRPO by 7%, even with teachers of modest capability. Furthermore, we analyse the role of ``ground truth'' in Cliff and investigate its training dynamics. These results establish Cliff as a simple, general and effective approach for improving RLVR with richer, fine-grained supervision.

</details>


### [207] [UE5M3 FP4 Block Scaling for Stable Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02846)
*Robert Hu,Carlo Luschi,Paul Balanca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种更简单的FP4（4位浮点）预训练方案，将E2M1有效载荷与无符号E5M3块缩放配对，替代NVIDIA Transformer Engine的RHT和BF16最终层方案。在Nemotron-H 8B模型上预训练近1900亿token，该方法在最终训练损失和验证损失上均优于基线，且吞吐量提升21.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定的4位浮点（FP4）预训练具有挑战性，因为E2M1有效载荷只能表示较窄范围的量级。NVIDIA的Transformer Engine方案虽然有效，但依赖当前张量缩放、随机化Hadamard变换（RHT）和bfloat16（BF16）最终层，这些操作增加了FP4矩阵乘法之外的计算负担和复杂度。

Method: 将E2M1有效载荷与无符号E5M3块缩放配对，利用其更宽的范围实现周期性张量缩放；对反向梯度应用选择性随机舍入；省略RHT变换；在所有符合条件的内部线性层中使用FP4。采用块大小为16的缩放方案。

Result: 在Nemotron-H 8B模型上预训练近1900亿token，与Transformer Engine方案相比：最终窗口训练损失更低；在各自量化推理策略下验证损失（保持集负对数似然）更低；三个报告的聚合指标上量化推理下游点估计均更高。去除RHT和BF16最终块豁免的本机执行消融实验使模型体token吞吐量提升21.2%。

Conclusion: 通过更简单的配方实现了端到端软件模拟的FP4预训练，证明了E5M3块缩放的可行性，并激励对E5M3块缩放的原生硬件支持。

Abstract: Stable 4-bit floating-point (FP4) pretraining is difficult because the E2M1 payload represents only a narrow range of magnitudes. NVIDIA's Transformer Engine \nv{} recipe addresses this with current-tensor scaling, a randomized Hadamard transform (RHT), and bfloat16 (BF16) final layers, adding work outside the FP4 matrix multiplications. We instead pair E2M1 payloads with unsigned E5M3 (\ue{}) block scales. Their wider range permits periodic tensor scaling, while our recipe applies selective stochastic rounding to backward gradients, omits RHT, and uses FP4 in all eligible internal linears.
  We pretrain a Nemotron-H 8B model for nearly 190 billion tokens. Compared with Transformer Engine \nv{}, the proposed block-16 recipe finishes with lower final-window training loss and, under their respective quantized-inference policies, lower validation loss measured as held-out negative log-likelihood. Its quantized-inference downstream point estimates are also higher on all three reported aggregates. A native \nv{} execution ablation that jointly removes RHT and the BF16 final-block exemption increases measured model-body token throughput by 21.2\%. These results demonstrate end-to-end software-emulated \uefp{} pretraining with a simpler recipe and motivate native support for \ue{} block scaling.

</details>


### [208] [Post-Training Language Models for Gold-Medal Performance in Coding Competitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02849)
*Aleksander Ficek,Sean Narenthiran,Mehrzad Samadi,Somshubra Majumdar,Boris Ginsburg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套端到端的专业化pipeline，结合大规模题目筛选、合成推理轨迹、SFT和RL，训练了Nemotron-3-Nano-CC（30B-A3B）和Nemotron-3-Ultra-CC（550B-A55B）模型，并引入GenCorrect这一反馈驱动的测试时计算策略。在IOI 2025上，Nano-CC得分从130提升至468（超过金牌线438.3），Ultra-CC达502分；进一步构建的竞赛专用系统在IOI 2026前瞻性评估中以535.4分超越最高分人类选手498.27分，成为首个在IOI题目集上超越人类顶尖选手的AI系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 国际信息学奥林匹克竞赛（IOI）和ICPC代表了大语言模型推理最具挑战性的场景，此前尚无AI系统能在与人类选手相同约束下超越顶尖人类选手，作者希望构建能够在IOI竞赛中超越人类最高分的专业化系统。

Method: 采用端到端专业化pipeline：(1) 使用22,000道精选竞赛题目；(2) 生成合成推理轨迹；(3) 对Nemotron-3-Nano-CC（30B-A3B）进行SFT和RL训练，对Nemotron-3-Ultra-CC（550B-A55B）进行SFT训练；(4) 提出GenCorrect反馈驱动的测试时计算策略，迭代生成、评估和精炼多样化解决方案；(5) 基于实验结果构建竞赛专用Ultra-CC系统并在IOI 2026前瞻性验证。

Result: IOI 2025上，Nano-CC从130分提升至291分（后训练后），再提升至468分（配合GenCorrect），超过金牌线438.3；Ultra-CC达到502分。竞赛专用Ultra-CC系统在IOI 2026上与人类选手相同的时间、网络和提交限制下取得535.4/600分，超过金牌线361.12和人类最高分498.27，成为首个超越IOI人类最高分的AI系统。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，通过大规模题目筛选、合成数据、SFT和RL的组合以及测试时计算策略，AI系统能够在最具挑战性的竞赛编程场景中超越人类顶尖表现，标志着LLM推理能力的重要里程碑。

Abstract: Competitive programming has become a key test of large language model reasoning, with international competitions such as IOI and ICPC representing its most challenging settings. We present an end-to-end specialization pipeline combining large-scale problem curation, synthetic reasoning traces, supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and reinforcement learning (RL). Using 22,000 curated problems, we train Nemotron-3-Nano-CC (30B-A3B) with SFT and RL and Nemotron-3-Ultra-CC (550B-A55B) with SFT alone. We further introduce GenCorrect, a feedback-driven test-time compute strategy that iteratively generates, evaluates, and refines diverse solutions. On IOI 2025, Nano-CC improves from 130 points to 291 after post-training and to 468 with GenCorrect, exceeding the gold threshold of 438.3 while Ultra-CC reaches 502. Guided by these results, we develop a competition-specific Ultra-CC system and evaluate it prospectively during IOI 2026. Under the same time, internet-access, and submission constraints as human contestants, it scores 535.4 out of 600, exceeding both the gold threshold of 361.12 and the top human score of 498.27. To our knowledge, this is the first AI system to outscore the highest-scoring human contestant on an IOI problem set.

</details>


### [209] [The Implications of Linguistic Illegibility for LLM Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02852)
*James Mickens*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"语言不可读性"（linguistic illegibility）概念，指LLM外化的语言输出或机制性探针提取的语言特征无法真实反映其内部计算的场景。由于LLM内部计算本质上是激活空间上的数学运算，与自然语言之间存在有损转换，因此语言不可读性不可避免。这导致依赖模型语言自报的安全机制（如思维链监控、宪法自我批判、激活探针）无法完全可靠，沙箱需要不依赖读取模型语言状态的隔离技术。作者主张污点跟踪（taint tracking）是构建有效沙箱的有前景方法，并结合稳健虚拟化、第三方审计等机制，为语言监控提供关键兜底。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有证据表明LLM的外部语言输出和机制性提取的语言特征可能无法可靠反映模型内部计算，这动摇了依赖语言自报内容的安全机制的根基。作者旨在揭示这一问题的必然性，并提出不依赖语言解读的替代安全方案。

Method: 作者从概念层面论证语言不可读性的不可避免性——LLM内部计算是激活空间上的数学运算，与语言之间存在有损转换。在此基础上提出基于污点跟踪的沙箱策略：预先定义不应受模型输出数据影响的系统状态，无论模型如何语言自报都能保证隔离，并辅以稳健虚拟化、第三方审计等机制。

Result: 论证了依赖语言自报的安全机制（思维链监控、宪法自我批判、激活探针）不可能完全健全；提出污点跟踪作为不依赖读取语言状态的沙箱方法，并认为结合虚拟化与审计等措施可弥补语言监控的不足，能缓解前沿模型的沙箱逃逸问题。

Conclusion: LLM的语言不可读性是根本性的、不可避免的特性。安全体系必须从依赖语言解读转向不依赖语言状态的隔离技术（如污点跟踪、稳健虚拟化），从而为语言监控建立可靠的安全底线。

Abstract: LLMs are trained to generate natural language. However, various strands of evidence indicate that an LLM's externalized linguistic outputs and mechanistically-extracted linguistic features can be an unreliable lens for understanding internal model computation. We introduce the term ``linguistic illegibility'' to broadly refer to scenarios in which an LLM's externalized or mechanistically-probed language artifacts fail to represent how the model actually thinks. We argue that the specter of linguistic illegibility is unavoidable for LLMs whose internal computations are not directly expressed via language, but rather math over activation spaces (with lossy translations between activation spaces and natural language happening at the bookends). If linguistic illegibility is always possible, then security mechanisms that rely on a model's linguistic self-reporting (e.g., chain-of-thought monitoring, constitutional self-critique, activation probing for linguistically-defined feature vectors) can never be completely sound; the model sandbox will always need isolation techniques whose guarantees do not depend on reading a model's linguistic state at all. We argue that observing a model's outputs using taint tracking is a promising approach for an effective sandbox: regardless of how a model linguistically self-reports, a taint tracking policy can define, a priori, various pieces of system state that should never be influenced by model-produced data. We also discuss several additional sandboxing mechanisms (e.g., robust virtualization, third-party auditing of sandboxing configurations) which collectively provide a critical floor beneath linguistic monitoring, and would have mitigated recent sandbox exploits by frontier models.

</details>


### [210] [Graph Machine: Towards Better Pretraining via Edges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02881)
*Lintai Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了图机器(GM)架构，通过稀疏动态路由维护O(n)大小的状态，区别于固定大小状态或静态稀疏路由方法。GM用可微分更新的"边"（类似指针追逐机制）替代了Transformer中75%的稠密层，在Qwen3-0.6B模型上从头预训练15.7B token。当每个KV头仅检索4,096个token中的2个时，损失仅略有退化；检索4个时性能甚至略优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏注意力方法存在局限：要么状态大小固定（难以扩展容量），要么稀疏路由是静态的（缺乏灵活性），且限制了可访问状态大小到O(1)。作者希望同时保持稀疏层的O(n)复杂度，又不限制理论上可访问的状态规模。

Method: 提出图机器(GM)架构：维护O(n)大小的状态，通过稀疏动态路由访问。核心创新在于"边"这一指针式对象，通过类似指针追逐的推荐机制进行可微分更新，实现动态、稀疏且可扩展的状态访问。实验设计为将Qwen3-0.6B中75%的稠密Transformer层替换为GM稀疏层，从头预训练15.7B tokens。

Result: 在Qwen3-0.6B上，替换75%稠密层后：每个KV头仅检索4,096个token中的2个时，损失仅轻微退化；检索4个token时，最佳模型的损失略优于基线。

Conclusion: GM架构证明了以极小精度代价实现极端稀疏检索（仅检索全部token的0.05%-0.1%）是可行的，动态可微的路由机制有望成为高效Transformer稀疏化的新方向。

Abstract: We introduce the Graph Machine (GM), an architecture that maintains an $O(n)$-sized state and accesses it through sparse, dynamic routing. Unlike methods with fixed-size states or sparse but static routing, GM preserves $O(n)$ complexity in its sparse layers without restricting the potentially accessible state size to $O(1)$. Instead, GM uses edges - pointer-like objects updated differentiably by a referral mechanism resembling pointer chasing. We replace 75% of the dense Transformer layers in Qwen3-0.6B with GM sparse layers and pretrain from scratch on 15.7B tokens. With only 2 of 4,096 tokens retrieved per KV head in each sparse layer, loss degrades only slightly; with 4, the best model marginally improves loss.

</details>


### [211] [A Common Measure of Communication for Speech Brain-Computer Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02887)
*Dulhan Jayalath,Benjamin Ballyk,Oiwi Parker Jones*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出开放词汇互信息（OVMI），一种基于信息论的度量方法，用于统一比较不同语音脑机接口（speech BCI）系统的性能。OVMI 通过衡量解码器相对于用户可能想要沟通的词语参考分布所传达的信息量，使得在不同词汇、数据集和记录条件下测量的系统能够在共同的沟通尺度上进行比较，并展示了其改进词汇设计、提升准确率（最高相对提升16.3%）的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音脑机接口领域缺乏统一的进展衡量标准，因为不同系统使用不同的数据集、记录方法、语音类型和词汇表，导致报告的分数难以相互比较。这一测量问题源于两个未解决的核心问题：（i）语音BCI应让用户沟通什么样的词语分布；（ii）系统能从此分布中传达多少信息。传统的准确率、词错误率等指标仅基于系统支持的词汇计算，可能高估系统的实际沟通能力。

Method: 作者推导出开放词汇互信息（OVMI）——一种信息论度量，衡量解码器相对于用户可能希望沟通的词语参考分布所传达的信息量。该方法允许在不同条件下（如不同词汇表）测量的系统能力在共同的沟通尺度上评估。同时展示了如何通过选择最大化OVMI的词汇表来改进系统设计。

Result: 研究表明：（1）传统报告的准确率、词错误率等仅在系统支持词汇上计算的指标可能高估系统的沟通能力；（2）使用OVMI比较现有系统，揭示了系统支持多少用户语言与其解码准确性之间的权衡；（3）这些比较取决于用户预期沟通的内容；（4）选择最大化OVMI的词汇表在三个语音领域上带来高达16.3%的相对准确率提升。

Conclusion: OVMI为语音BCI社区提供了一种有原则的方法来比较异构系统、改进词汇设计并衡量领域进展，解决了该领域长期缺乏统一度量标准的问题。

Abstract: Speech brain-computer interfaces (speech BCIs) translate neural activity into language, offering a path towards restoring speech for people with paralysis and, more broadly, enabling new forms of natural human-computer interaction. Despite this promise, the field lacks a common measure of progress because systems use different datasets, recording methods, types of speech, and vocabularies, so their reported scores are rarely comparable. Underlying this measurement problem are two unresolved questions: (i) what distribution of words should a speech BCI enable a user to communicate, and (ii) how much information from this distribution can a system convey. We address both by deriving open-vocabulary mutual information (OVMI), an information-theoretic quantity that measures the information conveyed by a decoder relative to a reference distribution over the words a user may wish to communicate. This allows capabilities measured under different conditions, such as distinct vocabularies, to be evaluated on a common communication scale. We show that ordinarily reported accuracy, word error rate (WER), and other metrics computed only over the words a system supports can overstate how much of a user's intended speech the system can communicate. We then use OVMI to compare existing systems, expose trade-offs between how much of the user's language a system supports and how accurately it decodes those words, show that these comparisons depend on what the user is expected to communicate, and demonstrate that selecting a vocabulary to maximise OVMI yields up to 16.3% relative improvement in accuracy across three speech domains. OVMI therefore provides the speech BCI community with a principled way to compare heterogeneous systems, improve vocabulary design, and measure progress in the field.

</details>


<div id='q-fin.PM'></div>

# q-fin.PM [[Back]](#toc)

### [212] [Switching Frictions, Heterogeneous Trading Horizons, and Long-Memory Order Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02525)
*Alejandro Rodriguez Dominguez*

Main category: q-fin.PM

TL;DR: 本文提出一种机制，通过组合选择中表征的昂贵变化来解释持续的有符号订单流，并建立了一个统一的统计框架，将表征停留时间、订单流记忆指数和有限市场推断时限联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解为什么订单流会表现出持续的有符号流（persistent signed order flow），以及这种持续性如何源于投资者在组合选择中更新认知表征的成本与异质性切换行为。

Method: 采用异质切换阈值与机会波动率构建异质停留时间分布，通过更新集合（renewal aggregation）将执行加权尾部与聚合流协方差的衰减联系起来。利用首达时间（first-passage）更新分析建立模型参数的联合约束，并通过结构模拟验证这些约束。

Result: 同一尾部决定了表征停留时长指数、订单流记忆指数和有限市场缩放终止的时域。结构模拟恢复了真实路径中的这些约束，量化了权重失配带来的扭曲，并显示有限截面如何缩短可用的推断时域。

Conclusion: 作者提出了一个经验协议，将聚合持久性拟合转化为跨数据集约束，以判断基于时长的核函数是否适用于独立的执行成本模型。

Abstract: This paper develops a mechanism through which costly changes in the representations used for portfolio choice can contribute to persistent signed order flow. Heterogeneous switching thresholds and opportunity volatility generate heterogeneous residence times, and renewal aggregation maps their execution-weighted tail into the decay of aggregate flow covariance. Under common execution weights, the same tail determines the exponent of representation-spell durations, the order-flow memory exponent, and the horizon at which finite-market scaling must end. First-passage renewal analysis establishes these joint restrictions. Structural simulations recover them from realized paths, quantify the distortion created by mismatched weights, and show how finite cross sections shorten the usable inference horizon. The resulting empirical protocol converts an aggregate persistence fit into cross-dataset restrictions that can determine whether a duration-based kernel is suitable for a separate execution-cost model.

</details>


### [213] [Uniform Inference and Certified Capacity at a Reflexive Stability Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02535)
*Alejandro Rodriguez Dominguez*

Main category: q-fin.PM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一推断与认证容量决策方法，用于估计金融稳定性边界，该方法在存在简并或缺陷碰撞等复杂谱结构下仍能提供可靠的推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的逐点推断在分离的简单谱根处是可靠的，但在半简并或缺陷碰撞附近可能失效，导致金融稳定性边界的推断不准确。

Method: 联合估计条件风险、临时交叉影响与有效风险承受能力，通过将底层输入的联合置信区域进行投影来避免局部近似，从而得到带有弃权机制的三向制度决策和单侧容量边界，并辅以验证过的二维实现来控制数值误差。

Result: 结构模拟恢复了覆盖与分辨率之间的预期权衡；观察到的风险压力测试能够区分统计弃权与计算预算不足。

Conclusion: 金融结论的可靠性依赖于交叉影响的识别、容量归一化以及输入在行动时间范围内的稳定性等前提条件。

Abstract: This paper develops uniform inference and certified capacity decisions for an estimated financial stability boundary. Conditional risk, temporary cross-impact, and effective risk-bearing capacity are jointly estimated from dependent observations. Conventional pointwise inference is reliable at a separated simple spectral root but can fail near semisimple or defective collisions. Projecting a valid joint confidence region for the underlying inputs avoids this local approximation and yields a three-way regime decision with abstention and a one-sided capacity bound. A verified two-dimensional implementation keeps numerical error from creating a resolved sign. Structural simulations recover the predicted tradeoff between coverage and resolution, while observed-risk stresses distinguish statistical abstention from an insufficient computational budget. The financial conclusions remain conditional on the identification of cross-impact, the normalization of capacity, and stability of the inputs over the action horizon.

</details>


### [214] [Eliciting ESG Preferences for Reinforcement Learning-Based Portfolio Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02677)
*Giovanni Dispoto,Marcello Restelli,Carmine Ventre*

Main category: q-fin.PM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将ESG意识投资组合优化建模为多目标强化学习（MORL）问题的框架，整合三家不同ESG评级机构的数据，并通过高斯过程偏好引导帮助投资者表达潜在效用函数，使算法交易与多样化的人类可持续性偏好对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL方法通常只优化单一ESG提供商的数据，忽略了不同评级机构方法论之间的显著分歧；同时，手动设定相互冲突目标的权重不直观且困难，难以弥合高维算法权衡与人类决策之间的鸿沟。

Method: 1) 将ESG投资组合优化建模为多目标RL问题，同时纳入三家不同ESG评级机构的评分；2) 集成基于高斯过程的偏好引导框架，通过候选投资组合的夏普比率与综合ESG得分成对比较来推断投资者的潜在效用函数；3) 使用LLM人格（Personas）模拟不同地区背景下的投资组合经理进行系统评估。

Result: 使用历史市场数据的实证结果表明，地区背景从根本上影响推导出的偏好权重：欧洲背景的人格更倾向于优先考虑ESG一致性而非财务回报，而德克萨斯州背景的人格则更偏爱风险调整后的业绩表现。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个高度自适应的框架，成功地将多目标算法交易与多样化、真实世界的人类可持续性偏好相协调，克服了单一ESG提供商局限性和权重设定的主观性难题。

Abstract: Modern portfolio management increasingly demands a balance between traditional risk-adjusted returns and strict Environmental, Social, and Governance (ESG) mandates. Current Reinforcement Learning (RL) approaches typically optimize for a single ESG provider, neglecting the significant divergence in rating methodologies across the industry and the unintuitive nature of manually weighting conflicting objectives. This paper addresses these limitations by formulating ESG-aware portfolio optimization as a Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning (MORL) problem that simultaneously incorporates ratings from three distinct ESG agencies. To bridge the gap between high-dimensional algorithmic trade-offs and human decision-making, we integrate a Preference Elicitation framework using Gaussian Processes. This system enables practitioners to infer their latent utility functions through intuitive pairwise comparisons of candidate portfolios based on their Sharpe ratios and aggregate ESG scores. We systematically evaluate our framework by employing Large Language Model (LLM) personas to simulate Portfolio Managers operating under varied regional contexts. Empirical results using historical market data reveal that regional backgrounds fundamentally shift the derived preference weights. For instance, European-based personas tend to prioritize ESG alignment over financial returns, while Texas-based personas favor risk-adjusted performance. This work offers a highly adaptable framework that successfully aligns multi-objective algorithmic trading with diverse, real-world human sustainability preferences.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [215] [From Open Standards to Openly Governed: Standards-Setting Organizations as Stewards of Openness amid Platformization and Digital Sovereignty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01773)
*Begoña G. Otero,Stefaan G. Verhulst*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探讨开放地理空间标准何时作为公共基础设施运作、何时沦为圈地渠道的问题，提出两个核心主张：标准制定治理（制度设计）决定开放规范的公共产品属性；地理空间协调正转向专有的位置技术栈、API和云平台，主权的云与AI战略只是转移这种权力而非消除。作者提出4Ps框架（Purpose, Principles, People and Processes, Policies and Practices）分析内部治理，同时强调内部治理无法独自解决外部结构性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放地理空间标准虽被广泛倡导，但其开放性不仅取决于规范本身，还取决于制定标准的机构和运作的基础设施。标准可能作为数字公共产品或数字公共基础设施运作，但这两者角色可能背离。作者希望厘清开放地理空间标准在何种条件下仍是公共基础设施而非沦为圈地渠道。

Method: 采用概念性/分析性论证方法，提出双重复合论点：一是标准制定治理（制度设计）影响公共产品属性的保持；二是地理空间协调的平台化趋势（专有location stacks、API、云平台）与弱治理形成相互强化的动态。在此框架下提出4Ps分析框架（Purpose、Principles、People and Processes、Policies and Practices），并强调内部治理的结构性局限。

Result: 论文揭示了弱治理与平台化之间的恶性循环机制：弱治理将协调推向实现层（implementation layer），平台化又降低投资开放标准化的激励，削弱可移植性和FAIR复用。主权云和主权AI战略只是权力重新定位而非消除。标准机构虽无法扭转平台化，但仍有能力掌控规范层（specification layer）。

Conclusion: 在缺乏法律保障的情况下，内部治理成为关键杠杆。但必须认识到内部治理无法单独解决外部结构性问题——这是一个根本性的局限。标准机构的治理改革（通过4Ps框架审视）是必要的但非充分条件。

Abstract: Open geospatial standards let data, services, and systems work across platforms. But openness is not just a property of specifications. It also depends on the institutions that produce them and the infrastructures in which they operate. Standards may function as digital public goods and, once embedded in public systems, as digital public infrastructure. Those roles can diverge. This paper asks when open geospatial standards remain public infrastructure rather than becoming channels of enclosure. It makes two claims. First, standards-setting governance matters: institutional design shapes whether open specifications retain their public-good character. Second, geospatial coordination is shifting toward proprietary location stacks, APIs, and cloud platforms, where roadmaps, pricing, and service terms increasingly set the rules. Sovereign-cloud and sovereign-AI strategies relocate this power rather than remove it. These dynamics reinforce each other. Weak governance pushes coordination toward the implementation layer; platformization, in turn, reduces incentives to invest in open standardization, weakening portability and FAIR reuse. Standards bodies cannot reverse platformization, but they still control the specification layer. Where legal safeguards are limited, internal governance becomes key. We frame this through four dimensions: Purpose, Principles, People and Processes, and Policies and Practices (the 4Ps framework) while stressing the limit that internal governance cannot solve alone an external structural problem.

</details>


### [216] [Accurate in space, unreliable in time: how LLMs represent national cultural change](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01902)
*Yalda Daryani,Miranda Bogen,Madeleine I. G. Daepp*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型（LLMs）在文化对齐评估中缺失的时间维度。利用超过二十年的世界价值观调查数据，比较了40个国家的文化轨迹与四个SOTA LLM在Inglehart-Welzel文化地图上产生的轨迹，发现模型虽然能将国家定位在接近最新调查位置，但其表征在时间上滞后数年，仅捕捉部分变化幅度，且在无变化处引入变动，很少再现国家轨迹的反转。这些发现指向"时间扁平化"现象，表明快照准确性可能对文化意识评估给出不完整的图景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文化对齐评估将文化视为单一快照，仅考察模型是否准确表征社会当前状态。但文化心理学研究表明文化价值观随时间的推移以不同速率和方向变化，因此"有文化意识"的模型应同时捕捉文化的当前状态和历史变迁轨迹，这是现有评估中缺失的重要维度。

Method: 使用超过二十年的世界价值观调查（WVS）数据，在Inglehart-Welzel文化地图框架下，比较40个国家的真实文化轨迹与四个SOTA LLM生成的文化轨迹，考察模型表征在时间维度上的准确性。

Result: 研究发现：模型虽能将国家放置于接近其最新调查位置，但这些表征在时间上滞后数年；模型仅捕捉到实际变化幅度的一部分；在几乎没有文化变化的国家引入了虚假变动；很少能再现国家轨迹方向的逆转。整体呈现出"时间扁平化"的特征。

Conclusion: 快照准确性无法完整反映LLM的文化意识水平，文化评估需要纳入时间维度。这一发现对模型评估方法、表征危害以及文化意识AI系统的治理均具有重要启示。

Abstract: Assessments of cultural alignment have become an important part of the development and improvement of large language models (LLMs). However, the majority of the evaluations treat culture as a single snapshot, investigating only whether a model represents a society accurately at the current time. Research in cultural psychology shows that cultural values change at different rates and directions over time. Therefore, a "culturally aware" model should capture not only where a culture is today but also how it has changed over time. We examine this missing dimension of cultural awareness using more than two decades of the World Values Survey data. We compare the cultural trajectories of 40 countries with the trajectories produced by four state-of-the-art (SOTA) LLMs on the Inglehart-Welzel cultural map. Our findings show that while models generally place countries close to their most recent surveyed positions, these representations tend to lag several years behind that position. They also capture only part of the magnitude of the observed change, introduce movement where little occurred, and rarely reproduce reversals in countries' trajectories. These findings point to temporal flattening and suggest that snapshot accuracy can give an incomplete picture of cultural awareness in LLMs and have implications for model evaluation, representational harms, and the governance of culturally aware AI systems.

</details>


### [217] [Privacy Washing: Detecting Internal Contradictions in Privacy Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02055)
*Thomas Brackin*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出"隐私洗涤"（privacy washing）概念，指隐私政策内部存在矛盾——承诺被同一政策其他部分的实践描述所削弱。作者构建了一个四阶段流水线（陈述抽取、兼容性过滤与NLI筛选、多模型法官验证、主题分析），通过三模型LLM小组多数投票确认矛盾。应用于2026年收集的123份（OPPT）与2015年收集的115份（OPP-115）网站隐私政策，发现类别模式在11年间反复出现，第三方共享矛盾占多数。OPPT中12.2%的公司至少有一个确认矛盾，OPP-115为36.5%。七个月后的稳定性复跑（完全分离配置）复现了OPPT流行率（13.0% vs 12.2%）。两个注意事项：法官裁决未与人类专家判断验证（精度未知，流行率为下限）；两次主运行过滤配置不同，流行率差异不能解释为语料或时代效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私政策内部可能存在自相矛盾的承诺会被削弱的现象，此前缺乏系统性可操作的检测方法。作者希望在多年跨度的大规模语料上验证这一现象是否持续存在，并探究其是否为结构性因素（而非有意欺骗）所致，同时检验检测方法的稳定性与可复现性。

Method: 四阶段流水线：(1) 陈述抽取（statement extraction）；(2) 兼容性过滤与自然语言推理（NLI）筛选；(3) 多模型法官验证——三模型LLM小组以多数投票确认矛盾；(4) 主题分析。研究在两个语料上进行：2026年收集的123份（OPPT）与2015年收集的115份（OPP-115）。另设稳定性复跑，使用完全分离配置（新抽取模型、三家中国供应商的法官、匹配过滤器、无法官-提交相似度阈值）。

Result: 类别模式跨越11年的语料间隔反复出现；第三方共享矛盾在每次主运行中占确认案件多数。OPPT中至少一个小组确认矛盾的公司占12.2%（15/123，排除遗留对后为9.8%）；OPP-115为36.5%（42/115）。七个月后的稳定性复跑在原始协议下复现OPPT流行率（13.0% vs 12.2%）；阈值以下配对以同等量级比率被确认（流行率升至20.3%与40.9%）；第三方多数对小组配置敏感，而相同类别对的复发则不受影响。

Conclusion: 隐私洗涤现象在11年间持续存在且类别模式稳定，第三方共享是主要矛盾来源，这更可能源于政策撰写的结构性因素而非有意欺骗。检测流水线具有跨时段、跨配置的可复现性（流行率稳定复现），但存在两点局限：缺乏人类专家验证导致精度未知（流行率应视为下限）；两次主运行的过滤配置不同，故其流行率差异不能解读为语料或时代效应（匹配复跑虽将差距缩小至约两倍但未消除）。

Abstract: Privacy policies may contain internal contradictions in which commitments are undermined by practices documented elsewhere in the same policy. We operationalize this phenomenon, privacy washing, through a four-stage pipeline: statement extraction, compatibility filtering and natural language inference screening, multi-model judge verification, and thematic analysis, with contradictions confirmed by majority vote of a three-model LLM panel. Applied to two corpora of website privacy policies, 123 collected in 2026 (OPPT) and 115 collected in 2015 (OPP-115), the pipeline finds the same category patterns recurring across the 11-year gap, with third-party sharing contradictions the majority of confirmed cases in each primary run, consistent with structural factors in policy composition rather than necessarily intentional deception. At least one panel-confirmed contradiction appears in 12.2% of OPPT companies (15/123; 9.8% excluding legacy pairs) and 36.5% of OPP-115 companies (42/115). A stability re-run seven months later, with a fully separated configuration (new extraction models, judges from three Chinese providers absent from both corpora, matched filters, no judge-submission similarity threshold), reproduces the OPPT prevalence under the original protocol (13.0% vs. 12.2%), finds sub-threshold pairs confirm at rates of the same order as those above (raising prevalence to 20.3% and 40.9%), and shows the third-party majority is panel-sensitive while the recurrence of the same category pairs is not. Two caveats govern all figures: panel verdicts are not validated against human expert judgment, so precision is unknown and prevalence figures are lower bounds; and the two primary runs used different filter configurations, so their prevalence difference is not interpretable as a corpus or era effect (the matched re-run reduces the gap to roughly twofold but does not eliminate it).

</details>


### [218] [Fairness-Aware Multimodal Transformer Modeling for Real-Time Student Attention Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02232)
*Christoforos Fragkiadakis,Seyed Sahand Mohammadi Ziabari,Ali Mohammed Mansoor Alsahag*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究在DIPSER自然课堂数据集上评估了面向公平性的多模态时序模型（Visual GRU、Sensor GRU、Residual Fusion Transformer）。多模态模型取得最佳平均性能（MAE 0.283, RMSE 0.363）和最低最差组误差，但公平性正则化的优势未能稳定迁移到留出被试上，强调公平性评估需要子群体感知验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化的学生注意力估计可支持学习分析，但聚合性预测指标可能掩盖人口统计学层面的差异。现有研究缺乏对公平性感知多模态时序模型的系统评估，且验证层面的公平性改进是否能在真实泛化场景中保持仍未得到充分研究。

Method: 该研究使用DIPSER自然课堂数据集，结合面部图像、可穿戴传感器测量、注意力标注及自动推断的人口统计元数据。比较三个基线模型（Visual GRU、Sensor GRU、Residual Fusion Transformer），跨10个训练种子评估，并引入性别与年龄定向的MAE-gap正则化来衡量公平性差距。在NVIDIA A100 GPU上测量端到端管线与模型推理效率。

Result: 多模态模型平均测试性能最优（MAE 0.283, RMSE 0.363），且在最差组误差上低于其他基线，但对Visual GRU的优势有限。MAE-gap正则化在验证集上降低了性别和年龄相关差距，但在留出被试及重复的被试级划分中改善并不一致。推理效率上，端到端管线均50.65ms/预测窗口（1秒步长），纯时序模型仅需1.02ms。

Conclusion: 多模态融合能适度改善预测与最差组性能，但验证层面的公平性提升不应假定可以泛化。稳健的公平性评估需要子群体感知评估、重复的被试级验证，以及更大、更均衡的人口统计样本。

Abstract: Automated student-attention estimation can support learning analytics, but aggregate predictive metrics can conceal demographic disparities. This study evaluates fairness-aware multimodal temporal models on DIPSER, a naturalistic classroom dataset combining facial images, wearable-sensor measurements, attention annotations, and automatically inferred demographic metadata. Three baselines are compared across 10 training seeds: a Visual GRU, a Sensor GRU, and a Residual Fusion Transformer. The multimodal model achieves the best mean test performance (MAE 0.283, RMSE 0.363) and the lowest worst-group error among the evaluated baselines, although its gain over the Visual GRU is modest. Gender- and age-targeted MAE-gap regularization reduces disparities on validation data, but these gains do not consistently transfer to held-out subjects or repeated subject-level splits. On an NVIDIA A100-SXM4-40GB GPU, the warm end-to-end pipeline averages 50.65 ms per prediction window at a one-second stride, while the temporal model itself requires 1.02 ms. The findings show that multimodal fusion can modestly improve prediction and worst-group performance, but validation-level fairness gains should not be assumed to generalize. Robust fairness assessment therefore requires subgroup-aware evaluation, repeated subject-level validation, and larger, better balanced demographic samples.

</details>


### [219] [Meeting the Coming Wave: The Emerging Politics of AI and Work across 33 Parliaments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02296)
*Juliana Chueri,Petter Törnberg*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 基于33个议会2023-2026年共151.5万份议会演讲的分析，本文绘制了人工智能与工作的新兴政治图景，发现各政党围绕AI的争议并非关于颠覆后的补偿（仅占2.3%），而是关于赋能与投资（55.2%）、监管与限制（21.8%）及培训（20.6%）。主流及激进右翼支持无限制赋能，左翼持批判态度但内部在补救方案上分歧明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比较政治学界尚未系统绘制人工智能与工作这一正在各政党体系中形成的新政治图景，而现有政治经济学理论预期技术颠覆会引发补偿性需求，这一假设需要实证检验。

Method: 对33个议会在2023-2026年间的1,514,950篇议会演讲进行大规模文本分析，通过响应框架（response-frame）分类统计各政党对AI工作的立场取向。

Result: 补偿性话语仅占响应框架提及的2.3%，赋能与投资占55.2%，监管与限制占21.8%，培训占20.6%。主流与激进右翼支持无限制赋能；社会民主党保持采纳导向，绿党立场对半分，激进左翼是最明确的限制力量。

Conclusion: 当代AI冲突的核心并非颠覆后的补偿分配问题，而是政治应否赋能技术变革或对其轨迹进行治理。政党分歧体现在对AI之于工作的意义认知以及技术应受多大程度的约束上，这与传统政治经济学的预期逻辑存在显著差异。

Abstract: A new politics of artificial intelligence and work is taking shape across party systems, but comparative politics has yet to map it. Using 1,514,950 parliamentary speeches from 33 parliaments (2023-2026), we show this politics follows a different logic than political economy expects. Research anticipates that technological disruption generates demands for compensation; instead, compensation accounts for just 2.3% of response-frame mentions, while enablement and investment dominate (55.2%), regulation and restriction follow (21.8%), and training (20.6%) appears at similar rates across families. Parties disagree instead over what AI means for work and how far this technology should be restrained. The mainstream and radical right support unrestricted enablement; the left is critical but divided on remedy. Social democrats stay adoption-oriented; greens split evenly. The radical left is the clearest force for restriction. The AI conflict thus concerns not compensation after disruption, but whether politics should enable technological change or govern its trajectory.

</details>


### [220] [Addressing Trust in AI Systems through Education: A Didactic Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02453)
*Pierre Haritz,Hendrik Krone,Thomas Liebig*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出ICE-T教学框架，通过Bruner表征模式、Use-Modify-Create计算思维进阶和解释性思维三重互补维度，将信任校准作为显式教育目标，以解决ML教育中黑箱化导致的浅层理解和信任失准两大问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML教育面临两个密切相关障碍：一是教育工具将ML呈现为不透明黑箱，导致学习者理解肤浅；二是这种不透明性阻碍用户形成校准的信任（calibrated trust），而这恰是恰当依赖AI系统的前提。作者试图将信任校准提升为显式教育目标。

Method: 提出ICE-T框架，包含三个相互强化的侧面：(1) 跨模态迁移，基于Bruner的动作、图像、符号三种表征模式；(2) 计算思维，通过Use-Modify-Create进阶路径操作化；(3) 解释性思维，由过程模型支撑。作者将该框架与算法厌恶、AI素养、心智模型形成的实证文献以及K-12 ML活动系统性综述相连接，论证三个侧面提供了信任校准文献所识别的恰当依赖驱动机制：表征丰富性、渐进式过程控制和错误情境化能力。

Result: 本文提出将信任校准作为ML教育的显式目标，并以ICE-T作为实现该目标的原则性且可扩展的教学途径，声称其三个侧面为信任校准提供了认知机制支撑。

Conclusion: 作者认为ICE-T框架能够成为实现信任校准教育目标的可行手段，但本文主要是理论论证与框架提出，尚需实证验证。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) education faces two persistent and connected obstacles: many educational tools present ML as an opaque black box, which leaves learners with a superficial understanding, and this same opacity prevents users from forming the calibrated trust that appropriate reliance on AI systems requires. We present ICE-T, a didactic framework that integrates three mutually reinforcing facets: intermodal transfer grounded in Bruner's enactive, iconic, and symbolic modes of representation, computational thinking operationalized through the Use-Modify-Create progression, and explanatory thinking supported by a process model. Connecting the framework to the empirical literature on algorithm aversion, AI literacy, and mental model formation, and to systematic reviews of the K-12 ML activity landscape, we argue that the three facets supply the cognitive mechanisms that the trust calibration literature identifies as drivers of appropriate reliance: representational richness, graduated process control, and the capacity to contextualize errors. On this basis, we propose that trust calibration be treated as an explicit educational objective, with ICE-T as a principled and scalable means of achieving it.

</details>


### [221] [Rights by Architecture: A Human-Compatible Sociotechnical Layer for Digital Protection Across Regulatory Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02455)
*Soheil Human*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文从批判-概念性信息系统视角，解释数字权利在法律中存在但在信息系统中难以行使的鸿沟，并提出"人与兼容权利层"这一理论框架，作为标准化、机器可读、双向、跨司法辖区的社会技术能力，以支撑权利行使。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字权利虽然日益获得法律承认，但公民行使这些权利仍需依赖中介这些权利的信息系统，而现有系统难以支撑权利的落实，导致法律权利与实操能力之间存在显著鸿沟。

Method: 采用系统性概念综合与问题化方法，分析法律异质性、组织与商业激励冲突、碎片化架构及权利相关行为控制不对称性；并通过比较加州式选择退出信号、欧盟混合合法基础制度，以及P3P、DNT、GPC和ADPC等机制，推导出七项规范性要求。

Result: 提出"权利即架构"的有界解放政策论点，并将拟议的GDPR自动化和机器可读隐私管理条款作为政策案例，论证应从横幅式合规转向支持权利的数字基础设施。

Conclusion: 需要一种受治理、人类兼容的权利层来弥合法律与信息系统之间的鸿沟，推动隐私治理从"通知同意"的横幅式合规转向可互操作、权利支持型基础设施。

Abstract: Digital rights increasingly exist in law but remain difficult to exercise through the information systems that mediate them. Using disciplined conceptual synthesis and problematization, this critical-conceptual IS paper explains the gap through the interaction of legal heterogeneity, conflicting organizational and commercial incentives, fragmented architectures, and asymmetrical control over rights-relevant acts. It then theorizes a human-compatible rights layer: a governed sociotechnical capability for standardized, machine-readable, bidirectional, and jurisdictionally plural communication of requests, consent, refusal, withdrawal, objection, records, and support. Comparing California-style opt-out signals, the EU's mixed lawful-basis regime, and P3P, DNT, GPC, and ADPC, the paper derives seven normative requirements, develops rights by architecture as a bounded emancipatory policy argument, and treats a proposed GDPR provision on automated and machine-readable privacy management as a policy case for moving from banner-based compliance toward rights-supporting digital infrastructure.

</details>


<div id='q-fin.ST'></div>
