### [222] [Modeling Trade Durations under Temporal Granularity Effects in Forex Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02660)
*Vladimír Holý*

Main category: q-fin.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"粒度调整的自回归条件持续时间（GA-ACD）模型"，用于处理高频外汇数据中交易持续时间在整数值附近聚集（heaping）的现象。该模型基于一个由标准广义伽马分量和局部重分配概率质量的第二分量组成的新型两分量混合分布，并在得分驱动框架内建模条件动态。模拟研究显示忽略堆积会导致参数估计有偏，实证分析表明该模型在主要货币对中优于标准广义伽马ACD模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高频外汇交易数据中的交易持续时间在整数值附近出现频率异常增加的经验现象（即堆叠效应），现有标准ACD模型无法捕捉这一特征，导致参数估计有偏且推断失真。

Method: 提出GA-ACD模型：采用两分量混合分布，标准广义伽马分量处理常规持续时间，第二个分量在整数值周围局部重新分配概率质量以捕捉堆叠效应；条件动态在得分驱动框架中建模，允许尺度参数随时间变化并响应历史持续时间，所有参数通过最大似然估计获得。

Result: 模拟研究表明忽略堆叠会导致参数估计有偏以及关于持续时间和动态的推断失真；实证分析表明整数持续时间聚集在主要货币对中普遍存在，且GA-ACD模型在拟合表现上优于标准广义伽马ACD模型。

Conclusion: GA-ACD模型能够有效捕捉高频外汇数据中交易持续时间的整数值堆叠现象，是标准ACD模型的重要改进，为高频金融数据建模提供了更准确的工具。

Abstract: Trade durations in high-frequency foreign exchange data exhibit increased occurrence near integer values. To address this empirical phenomenon, we propose the granularity-adjusted autoregressive conditional duration (GA-ACD) model. It is based on a novel two-component mixture distribution consisting of a standard generalized gamma component for regular durations and a second component that locally redistributes probability mass around integer values to capture heaping. Conditional dynamics are modeled within a score-driven framework, allowing the scale parameter to vary over time in response to past durations, and enabling maximum likelihood estimation of all model parameters. A simulation study shows that ignoring heaping leads to biased parameter estimates and distorted inference regarding both the distribution and the dynamics of durations. An empirical analysis demonstrates that integer-duration clustering is pervasive across major currency pairs and that the GA-ACD model outperforms the standard generalized gamma ACD model.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [223] [A Control Co-Design Framework to Optimize Sustainability with Application to Microgrid-Driven Data Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01912)
*Tania Rifat Jahan,Donald J. Docimo*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个以可持续性为中心的广义控制协同设计（CCD）框架，用于能源系统的物理设备特性与控制器参数的联合优化，并将可持续性指标划分为制造、运行和处置三个生命周期阶段，应用于微电网驱动的数据中心系统以验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心及为其供电的电网技术消耗了大量全球能源，增加了温室气体排放和电子废弃物的风险。现有研究存在明显空白：(i) 很少有研究同时优化设备与控制器特性；(ii) 可持续性标准未被重点关注。因此需要一种将可持续性指标纳入设计和控制优化中的系统性框架。

Method: 建立一个广义的、以可持续性为中心的控制协同设计（CCD）框架，该框架将可持续性指标定义并分类为三个生命周期阶段（制造、运行和处置），以支持不同设计方案下指标的优化与比较分析，并将其应用于微电网驱动的数据中心系统。

Result: 框架提供的分析为微电网和数据中心的控制协同设计提供了洞察，例如组件的制造阶段温室气体等效排放可能远超系统运行阶段的排放。与基线程序识别的设计相比，该框架确定的系统设计在环境可持续性类别上显示出显著的改进。

Conclusion: 可持续性导向的控制协同设计框架能够有效揭示能源系统全生命周期中的可持续性权衡，尤其是在数据中心和微电网这类现代关键能源系统中，为绿色设计与控制提供了新的优化途径。

Abstract: This work studies the optimization of physical plant characteristics and controller parameters for sustainability. Environmental sustainability is strongly correlated with the development and operation of energy systems, with data centers as the preeminent modern example. Data centers, and the grid technologies that provide their power, consume nonnegligible amounts of global energy and pose a risk to increase greenhouse gas (GHG) emissions and electronic waste. Addressing such issues requires improved plant design and control strategies, often approached through optimization-based methods. However, there are noticeable gaps regarding data center and microgrid design: (i) simultaneous optimization of plant and controller features is rarely explored, and (ii) sustainability criteria are not emphasized. This work addresses these gaps by establishing a generalized, sustainability-centric control co-design (CCD) framework for energy systems. Uniquely, the CCD framework defines and categorizes sustainability metrics into three lifecycle stages - manufacturing, operation, and disposal - supporting optimization and comparative analysis of the metrics for different design options. To exemplify its use, the CCD framework is applied to a microgrid-driven data center system, providing a family of sustainability metrics correlated to plant and controller parameters. The analysis enabled by the framework provides insights into the CCD of microgrids and data centers, such as that GHG-equivalent emissions from manufacturing of components can dwarf those generated during operation of the system. The system designs identified by the proposed framework show substantial improvements to environmental sustainability categories as compared to designs identified through baseline procedures.

</details>


### [224] [Quantum-Based k-Coverage Optimization for UAV-Aided Search and Rescue Missions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01930)
*Halim Lee,Suhui Jeong,Na Young Kim,Jiwon Seo*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对大规模灾害场景中无人机RF定位搜索救援问题，提出将航点选择形式化为扩展k覆盖问题，并推导出精确惩罚QUBO公式，通过QAOA在模拟器和IBM 127量子位处理器上验证，实现了95%以上的3覆盖率和高达37%的航程缩短。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模灾害中快速定位失踪人员是搜救的关键挑战，无人机搭载RF接收器可支持基于射频的定位，但需要解决如何在分布式感知位置（航点）上进行最少部署的问题——即需要每个潜在目标位置至少有三次覆盖的航点最小集选择问题。

Method: 将该问题形式化为扩展k覆盖问题（独立定义无人机可导航区域与目标区域），推导出带充分惩罚条件的精确惩罚QUBO公式以保证可行性和最小航点基数，将QUBO映射为Ising形式代价哈密顿量，并使用QAOA在无噪声模拟器和IBM 127量子位Eagle处理器上求解。

Result: 在模拟器实例上QAOA恢复已知最小基数解；矩形硬件测试平均3覆盖率超过95%；校园规模评估中10次硬件执行达到99.3%平均3覆盖率，90%可行运行率，最短可行飞行航程比确定性网格基线短37.0%。并与经典优化和学习基线进行比较，提供了更大生成实例的计算与量子资源分析。

Conclusion: 本文建立了RF搜索救援航点选择的精确QUBO表示，并表征了其在当前门级量子硬件上的实现，证明了量子近似优化方法在该实际组合优化问题上的可行性与优势。

Abstract: In large-scale disaster scenarios, rapid localization of missing persons is a critical challenge for search-and-rescue (SAR) operations. Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) equipped with radio frequency (RF) receivers can support RF-based localization by collecting signals emitted from mobile devices at spatially distributed sensing locations. This paper addresses the resulting waypoint-selection problem: determining a minimum set of UAV waypoints that provides at least threefold coverage of every potential target location. We formulate this task as an extended k-coverage problem that independently defines the UAV-navigable and target regions, and derive an exact-penalty quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) formulation with a sufficient penalty condition that preserves feasibility and minimum waypoint cardinality. The QUBO is mapped to an Ising-form cost Hamiltonian and evaluated using the quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA) on both a noise-free simulator and IBM's 127-qubit Eagle processor. On the tested simulator instances, QAOA recovers the known minimum-cardinality solutions. Across the rectangular hardware test cases, the mean 3-coverage ratio exceeded 95%. In the campus-scale evaluation, ten hardware executions achieved 99.3% mean 3-coverage with a 90% feasible-run rate, while the shortest feasible flight path was up to 37.0% shorter than those of the deterministic grid-based baselines. Additional comparisons with classical optimization and learning-based baselines are provided, together with computational and quantum-resource analyses for larger generated instances. These results establish an exact QUBO representation for RF-based SAR waypoint selection and characterize its implementation on current gate-based quantum hardware.

</details>


### [225] [Dimension-Reduced ADP for Real-Time Microgrid Operation with Massive Air-Conditioning Loads under Multiple Uncertainties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01972)
*Jingguan Liu,Xiaomeng Ai,Shichang Cui,Jiakun Fang,Jinyu Wen*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种降维近似动态规划（ADP）方法，用于在多重不确定性下对包含海量空调负荷的微电网进行实时运行优化。通过引入决策后值函数，并开发基于一致性约束的值函数投影技术将高维状态空间映射到可处理的聚合状态空间，结合分段线性近似进行值函数训练，实现了低计算成本下接近最优的运行性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微电网实时运行面临海量空调负荷带来的维度灾难问题，传统动态规划方法难以处理高维状态空间，需要一种既能应对多重不确定性又能降低计算复杂度的方法来实现实时优化运行。

Method: 将微电网运行问题建模为多阶段Markov决策过程；引入决策后值函数刻画当前决策对未来运行成本的影响；开发基于一致性约束的值函数投影方法，将节点处的高维状态空间映射为可处理的聚合状态空间；在降维状态基础上采用分段线性近似进行高效值函数训练。

Result: 在33节点和123节点系统中进行案例研究表明，该方法在确定性和随机性条件下均能实现接近最优的运行性能，同时具有较低的计算成本和良好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 提出的降维ADP方法有效解决了海量空调负荷下微电网实时运行的维度灾难问题，在计算效率和运行性能之间取得了良好平衡，为大规模微电网在线优化运行提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes a dimension-reduced approximate dynamic programming (ADP) method for real-time microgrid operation with massive air-conditioning loads under multiple uncertainties. The operation problem is formulated as a multi-stage Markov decision process, and a post-decision value function is introduced to characterize the impact of current decisions on future operating costs. To address the curse of dimensionality caused by massive air-conditioning loads, a consistency-based value function projection is developed to map the high-dimensional state space at each node into a tractable aggregated state space. Based on the reduced states, piecewise linear approximation is further employed for efficient value function training. Case studies on 33-bus and 123-bus systems show that the proposed method achieves near-optimal operation performance with low computational cost and good scalability under both deterministic and stochastic conditions.

</details>


### [226] [Efficient Sensor Fusion Through Covariance-Constrained Observation Decimation (CCOD)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02010)
*Andres Enriquez Fernandez,Erik Blasch,Angel Flores-Abad,John J. Bird*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种协方差约束观测抽取（CCOD）框架，通过使用等效抽取系统矩阵和过程噪声矩阵重新表述离散代数Riccati方程（DARE）的输入，无需增加系统维度或求解耦合周期Riccati方程，即可通过单次DARE评估直接预测采用抽取测量更新估计器的稳态估计误差协方差，并用于确定满足特定不确定度约束的最大观测抽取因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观测抽取常用于降低传感、通信和计算需求，但降低测量同化频率会增加估计不确定性。选择适当的观测抽取因子需要准确预测估计器性能，而标准DARE不适用于采用抽取测量更新的估计器，现有方法（提升系统表示或周期Riccati方程）又引入了额外的计算复杂度。

Method: 提出CCOD框架，通过等效抽取系统矩阵和过程噪声矩阵重构DARE输入，捕获测量更新之间的协方差增长，从而通过单次DARE评估直接预测稳态估计误差协方差，无需增加系统维度或求解耦合周期Riccati方程。

Result: 通过高维线性时不变系统和空间目标跟踪应用的验证，所提方法能准确预测稳态估计器性能，同时减少满足指定协方差约束所需的测量同化频率。

Conclusion: CCOD框架能够在降低计算复杂度的同时，准确预测抽取测量估计器的稳态性能，并确定在满足协方差约束前提下的最优观测抽取因子，为观测降采样下的状态估计设计提供了高效工具。

Abstract: Observation decimation is frequently employed in state estimation to reduce sensing, communication, and computational requirements, but decreasing the measurement assimilation frequency increases estimation uncertainty. Selecting an appropriate observation decimation factor therefore requires accurately predicting the resulting estimator performance. While the discrete algebraic Riccati equation (DARE) provides the steady-state estimation-error covariance for standard linear time-invariant Kalman filters, it is not directly applicable to estimators employing decimated measurement updates. Existing approaches address this limitation through lifted system representations or periodic Riccati equation formulations, both of which incur additional computational complexity. This paper presents a covariance-constrained observation decimation (CCOD) framework that reformulates the DARE inputs using equivalent decimated system and process-noise matrices that capture covariance growth between measurement updates. The proposed reformulation enables direct prediction of the steady-state estimation-error covariance through a single DARE evaluation without increasing the system dimension or solving coupled periodic Riccati equations. The resulting covariance prediction is used to determine the maximum observation decimation factor that satisfies a prescribed estimation uncertainty bound. Validation using a high dimensional linear time-invariant system and a space object tracking application demonstrates that the proposed approach accurately predicts steady-state estimator performance while reducing the measurement assimilation frequency required to satisfy specified covariance constraints.

</details>


### [227] [Seed-Anchored Budget-Bounded Graph Rendering for Question Answering on Industry-Standard Power-Grid Information and Exchange Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02011)
*Jayakumar Manoharan,Yamini Sehgal*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出"种子锚定图渲染"（seed-anchored graph rendering）方法，用于在固定上下文预算下进行电力电网模型的大语言模型问答。该方法确定性优先渲染查询局部图证据，无需额外调参，在CIM/CGMES电网模型上相比朴素描述优先渲染显著提升了局部证据保留率和问答准确率（在8,000字符预算下从0.450提升至0.970），并优于LightRAG、Microsoft GraphRAG和HippoRAG等抽取式图表示方法，同时避免了LLM图构建token开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电网模型上的大语言模型问答必须遵守固定上下文预算限制，而传统的图渲染方式（如描述优先）在预算受限时会丢失多跳局部证据，导致问答准确率大幅下降。作者希望在不引入额外学习参数的前提下，找到一种确定性的图渲染策略，确保与查询相关的局部图证据能被保留在有限的上下文窗口内。

Method: 提出种子锚定图渲染方法：以查询相关的种子节点为核心，按确定性策略优先渲染种子局部邻域的证据，并通过共享跳数上限与上下文预算提供可检验条件，保证预定义的种子局部答案承载渲染单元在贪心有界上下文前缀中得以保留。与朴素描述优先渲染不同，该策略将查询局部性置于渲染优先级之首，且不依赖任何已训练或微调参数。

Result: 在两个预算受限的CGMES编码上：朴素描述优先渲染对多跳项的局部证据保留率仅为0.12和0.00，而种子锚定渲染完整保留所有证据。在SmallGrid拓扑族预先注册的100项新测试集上，固定8,000字符上下文预算下准确率从0.450提升至0.970。在与LightRAG、Microsoft GraphRAG、HippoRAG的对比中，种子锚定图匹配或超越这些方法，且避免了LLM图构建token消耗。

Conclusion: 种子锚定图渲染是预算受限情境下电网图问答的有效确定性策略，其在保留局部证据和下游准确率方面表现优异且实现简洁。但结论局限在CIM/CGMES模型、特定reader与上下文预算范围内，关注的是预算受限的检索问题，而非通用问答场景。

Abstract: Large language model question answering over power-grid models must respect a fixed context budget. We introduce seed-anchored graph rendering, a deterministic method that prioritizes query-local graph evidence without adding method-specific tuned or learned parameters beyond the shared hop bound and context budget. The method provides a checkable condition under which predefined seed-local answer-bearing render units are preserved in a greedy bounded-context prefix. We evaluate the approach on Common Information Model (CIM) network models exchanged through the Common Grid Model Exchange Standard (CGMES). On two budget-binding CGMES encodings, naive descriptions-first rendering retains local evidence for every single-hop item but only 0.12 and 0.00 of multi-hop items, whereas seed-anchored rendering retains all such evidence. On a preregistered fresh 100-item bank from the SmallGrid topology family, accuracy rises from 0.450 to 0.970 under a fixed 8,000-character context budget. Under a common retrieval and rendering pipeline, the standards-native seed-anchored graph matches or exceeds extracted graph representations produced by LightRAG, Microsoft GraphRAG, and HippoRAG, while avoiding LLM graph-construction tokens. The results are specific to the evaluated CIM/CGMES models, reader, and context budget; they concern budget-bounded retrieval rather than general question answering.

</details>


### [228] [Turnpike properties in nonlinear system identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02071)
*Julian D. Schiller,Matthias A. Müller*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文分析了使用仿真误差最小化（SEM）方法学习通用离散时间非线性状态空间模型的问题，重点研究了SEM优化问题的累积输出turnpike性质。作者在温和的可达性条件下，建立了累积turnpike性质、值函数强制性与严格耗散性之间的等价关系，并引入了基数turnpike性质（严格弱于累积型），最后基于增量输出稳定性、阶段成本凸性和适定性条件给出了turnpike行为的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在系统辨识中，SEM方法通常需要通过最小化仿真输出与测量输出之间的不匹配来学习模型参数，实践中常使用固定初始状态的更易计算的形式。但如果最优输出序列不具有turnpike性质，即从固定初始状态出发的最优序列不能接近无约束最优序列，那么这种简化可能会带来较大的输出偏差，缺乏理论保证。

Method: 在温和的可达性条件下，证明了累积输出turnpike性质、值函数强制性与严格耗散性之间的等价关系；提出了基数turnpike性质并证明其严格弱于累积型；基于增量输出稳定性、阶段成本凸性及适定性条件建立了turnpike行为的充分条件，并通过数值算例加以验证。

Result: 建立了累积turnpike性质与值函数强制性以及严格耗散性在温和可达性条件下的等价关系；证明了基数turnpike性质是累积turnpike性质的严格弱化形式；给出了保证turnpike行为的充分条件，并通过数值实验进行了验证。

Conclusion: 该研究为SEM方法中采用固定初始状态这一常见简化提供了理论依据，证明在合理条件下其最优输出序列能够接近无约束最优序列，从而为基于神经网络的非线性系统辨识等实际应用提供了可靠性保证。

Abstract: We analyze the problem of learning general discrete-time nonlinear state-space models using the simulation error minimization (SEM) method. In this setting, model parameters are typically learned by minimizing the mismatch between simulated and measured outputs over a training dataset, or shorter subsequences extracted from it. Specifically, we study the cumulative output turnpike property of the underlying SEM optimization problem, which requires optimal output sequences emanating from a fixed initial state to approach and remain close to an optimal output sequence of the corresponding SEM problem with free initial state. In the presence of non-unique optimal output sequences---as may arise, for instance, in fully black-box system identification using neural networks---the property is formulated with respect to the closest such sequence. Turnpike behavior is generally desirable in practice, as it provides a theoretical justification for employing computationally more tractable SEM formulations with fixed initial states while ensuring that their optimal output sequences remain close to unconstrained optimal ones. Under a mild reachability condition, we establish equivalence between the cumulative turnpike property, coercivity of the value function, and a tailored notion of strict dissipativity. We additionally introduce a cardinality turnpike property and show that it is strictly weaker than the cumulative notion. Finally, we establish sufficient conditions for turnpike behavior based on incremental output stability, convexity of the stage cost, and a suitable optimality condition, and illustrate the theory by means of a numerical example.

</details>


### [229] [Existential Opacity for Discrete-Event Systems with State Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02132)
*Zhiyuan Huang,Zhao Tong,Jiakai Li,Bingzhuo Zhong*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对离散事件系统（DES）中的不透明性（opacity）提出了一种新的松弛概念——存在性不透明性（EO）。与经典不透明性要求所有秘密行为都不可区分于非秘密行为不同，EO仅要求存在至少一条秘密行为与某一非秘密行为在入侵者的状态观测下不可区分。该概念比现有的基于状态观测的不透明性更具表达力，并配套发展了相应的验证方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典不透明性基于事件观测，但在路径规划等应用中入侵者观测的是系统状态而非事件。更重要的是，现有基于状态观测的不透明性要求"所有"秘密行为都必须满足不可区分性，然而在许多不透明性保持任务中，系统只需存在可被利用的秘密行为即可，这种性质无法被现有概念完全刻画，因此需要提出新的松弛化概念。

Method: 提出存在性不透明性（EO）概念，它只要求存在秘密行为（而非所有秘密行为）与某一非秘密行为在入侵者的状态观测下不可区分。论文证明了EO比现有基于状态观测的不透明性概念更具表达力，并系统发展了一类EO性质及其对应的验证方法。

Result: 证明了EO概念比现有基于状态观测的不透明性概念更具表达力，并开发了相应的验证方法，为不透明性保持问题的可行性判定提供了新准则。

Conclusion: 存在性不透明性（EO）为离散事件系统提供了一种更具通用性的不透明性分析框架，弥补了现有概念在捕捉不透明性保持任务可行性方面的不足，为相关应用提供了新的分析工具和判断标准。

Abstract: Opacity is a fundamental system property for confidentiality in discrete-event systems (DES). Classical opacity is typically defined under event-based observations, requiring that any secret system behavior remains indistinguishable from some non-secret behavior to an external intruder. However, in many applications such as path planning or opacity-preserving tasks, the intruder observes system states rather than events. Moreover, it often suffices that the system exhibits secret behaviors that can be exploited for opacity-preserving task execution, but such a system property cannot be fully captured by existing notions of state-observation-based opacity. Motivated by this limitation, we propose a relaxed notion of existing state-observation-based opacity, called existential opacity (EO), which only requires the existence of secret behaviors (instead of all secret behaviors) that are indistinguishable from a non-secret behavior under the state observations of the intruder. We show that the notion of EO is more expressive than existing state-observation-based opacity notions. In addition, a class of EO properties together with their corresponding verification approaches are developed, enabling the analysis of existential opacity in discrete-event systems and providing a new criterion for determining the feasibility of opacity-preserving problems.

</details>


### [230] [Prosumer Synchronisation Risk: Impacts of Time-Varying Tariffs on Distribution Network Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02174)
*Dillon Zadoks,Alfredo Oneto,Carlo Tajoli,Yi Guo,Philipp Heer,Giovanni Sansavini,Gabriela Hug*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究瑞士低压配电网中，从固定电价转向分时电价（ToU）和实时电价（RTP）对电网扩容需求的影响，发现适当采用可分时电价可降低超过50%的扩容投资成本，但过度采用（ToU超过50%、RTP超过40%）反而会因用户行为同步化导致扩容需求大幅反弹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着热泵、电动汽车、光伏和电池储能等分布式能源资源快速部署，低压配电网运行方式正在改变。配电系统运营商希望通过制定随时间变化的电价来激励网络友好行为并推迟电网改造，但存在引发产消者（prosumer）行为同步化的风险——用户跟随价格信号会避开原有负荷高峰，却可能在其他时段制造新的不期望高峰。目前缺乏对这一问题在电网扩容需求层面影响的量化评估。

Method: 采用混合整数线性规划模型，对瑞士471个低压参考配电网进行研究。模型设定产消者根据其订阅的电价合约（ToU或RTP）优化运行其分布式能源资源以最小化成本，随后配电系统运营商优化电网扩容方案。研究基于2050年分布式能源资源部署预测，对比固定电价、ToU和RTP三种电价机制下的扩容投资需求变化。

Result: 采用时变电价可将扩容投资成本降低超过50%；但高采用率会逆转这一下降趋势。当产消者采用率超过最优阈值（ToU为50%、RTP为40%）时，电价采用率每额外增加20%，扩容需求分别增加39%（ToU）和134%（RTP）。

Conclusion: 时变电价在适度采用率下能显著降低低压配电网扩容需求，但存在"最优采用率拐点"现象——过度推广时变电价将因产消者行为同步化而产生新的负荷高峰，反而加剧电网扩容压力，尤其RTP机制下的反弹效应更为剧烈。

Abstract: The rapid deployment of distributed energy resources, including heat pumps, electric vehicles, photovoltaics, and battery storage, is reshaping the operation of low-voltage networks. While distribution system operators often aim to develop time-varying tariffs to incentivise network-friendly behaviour and defer reinforcements, they risk triggering prosumer synchronisation. As prosumers follow price signals, their behaviour may synchronise, avoiding existing load peaks while creating undesirable peaks at other times. This paper quantifies the impact on low-voltage network reinforcement needs when shifting from flat tariffs to two time-varying tariffs: Time-of-Use and Real-Time Pricing. Applying a mixed-integer linear model across 471 low-voltage reference networks in Switzerland, we evaluate expansion requirements under projections of distributed energy resource deployment for 2050. In the model, prosumers operate their distributed energy resources in accordance with their subscribed electricity tariff to minimise costs, and the distribution system operator subsequently optimises reinforcements. Our results demonstrate that while adopting time-varying tariffs can reduce reinforcement investment costs by more than 50%, high adoption rates can reverse this declining trend. Specifically, once prosumer adoption exceeds the optimal thresholds of 50% for Time-of-Use and 40% for Real-Time Pricing, reinforcement needs increase by 39% and 134%, respectively, for an additional 20% of tariff adoption.

</details>


### [231] [Continuous-Time Aggregation of Massive Flexible HVAC Loads Considering Uncertainty for Reserve Provision in Power System Dispatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02408)
*Jingguan Liu,Xiaomeng Ai,Jiakun Fang,Shichang Cui,Shengshi Wang,Wei Yao,Jinyu Wen*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对HVAC负荷参与电力系统备用服务的需求响应问题，提出了一种新型连续时间聚合模型，通过仿射变换处理异质性、分布鲁棒机会约束处理室外温度不确定性，并设计了Bernstein多项式样条、多面体投影和线性化变换等求解技术，最终构建分层调度框架以实现大规模HVAC高效调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HVAC负荷具有快速响应能力，可为可再生能源的快速波动提供日内小时级灵活性备用，但大规模调度面临计算复杂性和室外温度不确定性的双重挑战。

Method: 1) 提出连续时间聚合模型揭示HVAC的日内小时级灵活性潜力；2) 设计新的仿射变换处理高维可行域中的异质性；3) 通过分布鲁棒机会约束构建室外温度不确定性并将其集成到聚合模型中；4) 提出级联重构技术（Bernstein多项式样条、多面体投影、线性化变换）实现可解性；5) 构建定制化分层调度框架，将聚合模型纳入电力系统备用调度。

Result: 案例研究验证了所提连续时间聚合模型在聚合精度、日内小时级灵活性利用和不确定性处理方面的有效性与可扩展性。

Conclusion: 所提出的连续时间聚合模型与分层调度框架能够高效调度大规模HVAC负荷，以应对可再生能源不确定性，为需求侧灵活性资源参与系统备用提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Heating, ventilation, and air conditioning (HVAC) loads, with their rapid response capabilities, can provide considerable intra-hour flexibility on the demand side for reserve provision in order to follow the fast variations of renewables. However, scheduling massive HVACs is challenging due to computation complexity and the uncertainty of outdoor temperature. In this paper, we first introduce a novel continuous-time (CT) aggregation model to reveal the potential intra-hour flexibility of HVACs. For accurate aggregation, a new affine transformation is designed to handle the heterogeneity in high-dimensional feasible region. Further, for reliable aggregation in practical environment, the outdoor temperature uncertainty is constructed by distributionally robust chance constrains and integrated into the aggregation model. Secondly, for the tractable calculation of the proposed CT aggregation model, a cascade of tailored reformulation techniques is proposed, including the Bernstein polynomial spline, polytope projection, and linearization transformation. Thirdly, a customized hierarchical dispatch framework is proposed via incorporating the proposed CT aggregation model into reserve provision in power system dispatch, so as to efficiently schedule massive HVACs to cope with the renewable uncertainty. Case studies verify the effectiveness and scalability of the proposed CT aggregation model in aggregation accuracy, intra-hour flexibility utilization, and uncertainty handling.

</details>


### [232] [Leveraging Time-Causal State Variable Aggregation for Real-Time Schedule of Massive Air Conditioners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02410)
*Jingguan Liu,Xiaomeng Ai,Shichang Cui,Xizhen Xue,Shengshi Wang,Jiakun Fang,Jinyu Wen,Yang Shi*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于时间因果聚合的近似动态规划算法（TCA-ADP），用于大规模空调负荷的实时经济调度。该方法通过分析时间因果性要求、构建增强聚合模型、将聚合过程重构为线性规划，并利用离线训练的价值函数实现并行闭式解聚合，在计算效率与经济性之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空调负荷为主动配电网应对可再生能源发电、电价和负荷需求等不确定性提供了灵活的调节资源。然而，由于空调系统具有海量时间耦合约束和时间因果不确定性，其实时调度极具挑战性，现有方法难以在计算效率和调度经济性之间取得良好平衡。

Method: 1) 首先分析聚合状态变量的时间因果性要求，使其与实时序贯决策过程一致；2) 开发增强聚合模型以保证高精度和时间因果性；3) 将聚合过程重构为线性规划以优化聚合参数并实现可解算的计算；4) 利用聚合状态变量以新方式近似价值函数；5) 通过历史数据离线训练价值函数，并通过并行闭式解聚合实现大规模空调的近最优实时调度。

Result: 案例研究验证了TCA-ADP算法的有效性和可扩展性，展示了其聚合精度、不确定性处理能力以及在经济性与可解算性之间的权衡表现。

Conclusion: TCA-ADP算法通过时间因果聚合与近似动态规划的结合，为大规模空调负荷的实时调度提供了一种兼顾计算效率与经济性的新方案，能够有效平衡海量价值函数空间带来的计算负担与调度性能。

Abstract: Air conditioner (AC) loads offer promising flexibility for active distribution networks to manage uncertainties, such as those in renewable energy generation, electricity prices, and load demand. However, real-time scheduling of ACs is challenging due to their massive temporal coupling constraints and time-causal uncertainties. To address this, a novel time-causal aggregation-based approximate dynamic programming (TCA-ADP) algorithm is proposed for efficient scheduling. The time-causality requirements for aggregating state variables are first analyzed to align with the real-time sequential decision-making process. Subsequently, an enhanced aggregation model is developed to ensure both high accuracy and adherence to time causality. The aggregation process is further reformulated as a linear program to optimize aggregation parameters and enable tractable computation. Accordingly, the TCA-ADP leverages aggregated state variables to approximate the value function as a new way, balancing computational efficiency and economy against the large value function space of massive ACs. By training the value function offline using historical data, the TCA-ADP efficiently achieves near-optimal real-time scheduling of massive ACs through parallel and closed-form disaggregation. Case studies demonstrate the effectiveness and scalability of the TCA-ADP, highlighting its aggregation accuracy, uncertainty handling, and the trade-off between economy and tractability.

</details>


### [233] [Multivariable Geometric Laplace Transform and Fault Detection in Distributed-Converter Lines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02431)
*Francisco Manuel Arrabal-Campos,Francisco G. Montoya,Santiago Sánchez-Acevedo,Raymundo E. Torres-Olguin,Alfredo Alcayde*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于二维几何拉普拉斯变换（定义在几何代数Cl(4,0)的交换子代数，同构于Segre双复数）的方法，用于分布式变流器直流线路的时空故障诊断。该变换将时间和空间相位解耦，使故障信号可分解为时间因子和空间因子e^{-s_x x_f}，从而同时实现故障的检测、定位和分类，并支持多故障同时诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直流线路配备大量分布式功率变流器时，监控本质上是一个时空问题：局部故障信息沿导体传播，到达测量点时已与线路自身动力学混合。传统方法难以同时解耦时间与空间信息，因此需要一个能在代数上区分两个相位维度的数学框架。

Method: 1) 在几何代数Cl(4,0)的交换子代数（同构于Segre双复数）上定义二维几何拉普拉斯变换，其中两个双向量B_t和B_x分别充当时间和空间的独立虚数单位；2) 基于该表示构建传输线模型及空间-时间色散关系；3) 设计基于导纳整形的分布式控制，精确处理离散变流器站点（空间采样、混叠、次Nyquist频带内精确的逐变流器下垂实现）；4) 构建完整的故障诊断链，可检测、定位并分类注入损耗、并联、传感器和本地控制器故障，支持多故障自动阶数选择和电源中断与电缆缺陷的区分。

Result: 故障在位置x_f处的变换残差可分解为F_f(s_t)e^{-s_x x_f}，时间特性保留在第一因子中，位置可从第二因子的几何参数读取。所有结果均由配套开源实现复现。

Conclusion: 该变换作为积分对象在双复分析中已知，单变量时退化为复拉普拉斯变换；本文的贡献在于其几何嵌入及其在分布式变流器网络故障诊断中的操作性应用。

Abstract: Monitoring a DC line with many distributed power converters is a genuinely spatio-temporal problem: the information about a localized fault travels along the whole conductor and reaches a few measurement points mixed with the dynamics of the line itself. This paper develops a two-dimensional geometric Laplace transform (t,x) -> (s_t,s_x) over a commutative subalgebra of the geometric algebra Cl(4,0), isomorphic to Segre's bicomplex numbers, in which two bivectors B_t and B_x act as independent imaginary units for the temporal and the spatial phase. Because the two phases live in algebraically distinguishable planes, a fault at position x_f leaves a transformed residual that factorizes as F_f(s_t) e^{-s_x x_f}: its temporal nature stays in the first factor and its location can be read as a geometric argument of the second. On this representation we build a transmission-line model of the converter line and its space-time dispersion relation, a distributed control by admittance shaping, including an exact treatment of discrete converter sites (spatial sampling, aliasing, and a per-converter droop realization that is exact on the sub-Nyquist band), and a fault diagnosis chain that detects, localizes and classifies injection-loss, shunt, sensor and local-controller faults, extends to multiple simultaneous faults with automatic order selection, and distinguishes the outage of a plant from a cable defect. As an integral object the transform is known in bicomplex analysis, and with a single independent variable it reduces to the complex Laplace transform; the contribution lies in its geometric embedding and in its operational use for fault diagnosis in distributed-converter networks. All results are reproduced by an accompanying open implementation.

</details>


### [234] [Effective Range and Optimal Frequency of Through-the-Earth Magnetic Induction Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02485)
*Honglei Ma,Erwu Liu,Wei Ni,Jun Zhu,Zhijun Fang,Rui Wang,Xinyu Qu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对穿地（TTE）磁感应通信（MIC）中以往被忽视的地下介质涡流损耗问题，推导了有效MIC距离的解析表达式，并给出了预测最佳载波频率以最大化通信距离的闭式公式。有限元仿真验证了分析结果，表明优化载波频率可显著提升MIC距离，同时优化天线半径也能有效扩展TTE和车载MIC应用的通信范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MIC距离分析忽略了地下材料引起的涡流损耗。在TTE MIC中，显著的涡流损耗使有效MIC距离的分析变得复杂，而该分析对性能优化至关重要，但此前文献中从未被解决。

Method: 考虑地下介质的电导率和介电常数，推导TTE MIC中的有效MIC距离，并给出预测最优载波频率以最大化该距离的闭式表达式。采用有限元仿真对理论分析进行验证。

Result: 有限元仿真验证了理论分析的正确性，证明优化载波频率能显著增强MIC通信距离；同时发现优化天线半径对于扩展TTE和车载MIC应用的通信距离也是有效手段。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种考虑涡流损耗的TTE MIC有效距离分析方法，并通过最优载波频率和天线半径的联合优化来最大化通信范围，为该领域提供了此前缺失的理论基础。

Abstract: Magnetic induction communication (MIC) is a promising technology for through-the-earth (TTE) communication. Previous studies on the MIC range have often overlooked the impact of eddy losses caused by underground materials. For TTE MIC, significant eddy losses complicate the analysis of the effective MIC range, which is vital for optimizing performance but has never been addressed in the literature. Accounting for the conductivity and permittivity of the underground medium, this paper derives the effective MIC range in TTE MIC, along with a closed-from expression that predicts the optimal carrier frequency to maximize this range. Finite element simulations validate the analysis, demonstrating that the optimal carrier frequency can significantly enhance the MIC range. It is also revealed that optimizing the antenna radius is effective in extending the MIC range for TTE and vehicle MIC applications.

</details>


### [235] [Physics-based Online Adaptive Koopman Model Predictive Attitude Control for Combined Spacecraft with Dynamic Uncertainties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02534)
*Yicheng Sun,Yueyong Lyu,Yuhan Liu,Yanning Guo,Wei Pan*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于物理的自适应Koopman模型预测控制（MPC）策略，用于组合航天器在惯性不确定性和目标机动下的姿态镇定。该方法利用基于四元数运动学的解析提升函数构建紧凑且物理可解释的线性Koopman模型，配合梯度下降更新律在线辨识时变惯性参数，并将问题转化为高效二次规划（QP）求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合航天器姿态控制在惯性不确定性（如燃料消耗、对接后质量参数变化）和目标主动机动情况下具有挑战性，传统非线性MPC计算负担大，而现有黑箱EDMD方法缺乏物理可解释性且DCM-based模型维度过高。

Method: 1) 从四元数运动学推导解析提升函数，构建紧凑的四元数Koopman线性模型；2) 利用线性标称结构，采用基于梯度下降的自适应更新律在线估计时变惯性不确定参数；3) 将自适应线性模型集成到MPC框架中，将最优控制问题转化为QP；4) 通过设计终端分量正式建立递归可行性和区域输入-状态稳定性。

Result: 通过高保真3D模拟器中的组合航天器姿态镇定任务比较仿真，验证了所提策略的有效性和优越性，相比非线性自适应MPC显著降低了在线计算负担。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种兼顾计算效率与物理可解释性的自适应姿态控制方案，理论上保证了稳定性，实践上证明了在组合航天器姿态控制场景中的优越性能。

Abstract: This paper proposes a physics-based adaptive Koopman Model Predictive Control (MPC) strategy for combined spacecraft attitude stabilization under inertia uncertainties and active target maneuverability. A novel, quaternion-based Koopman model is constructed from a set of analytical lifting functions derived from the quaternion kinematics, which provides a more compact and physically interpretable linear representation of the nonlinear dynamics compared with the conventional black-box EDMD and higher-dimensional DCM-based model. Leveraging the linear structure of this nominal model, a gradient descent-based update law is employed to efficiently identify time-varying inertial uncertainties from real-time input/output data. By integrating this adaptive linear model into the MPC framework, the optimal control problem reduces to a computationally efficient Quadratic Program (QP), thereby significantly lowering the online computational burden compared to nonlinear adaptive MPC. Recursive feasibility and regional input-to-state stability are formally established through the design of terminal ingredients for the MPC. The effectiveness and superiority of the proposed strategy are validated through comparative simulations of an attitude stabilization task for combined spacecraft in a high-fidelity 3D simulator.

</details>


### [236] [Zonotope-Based Active Exposure of Stealthy Deception Attacks in Sensor-Fusion Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02587)
*Meiqi Tian,Shuo Li,Bingzhuo Zhong*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究传感器融合信息物理系统中针对未知但有界噪声的隐蔽攻击检测问题，通过控制信道注入有界辅助控制扰动（暴露输入），扩大安全传感器输出集与攻击输出集之间的分离，从而检测可能同时被攻陷的多个可疑传感器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在传感器融合CPS中，多个传感器可能同时被隐蔽攻击者攻陷，且系统存在未知但有界噪声，使得传统检测方法难以区分攻击与正常噪声影响，因此需要一种能主动暴露攻击的检测框架。

Method: 先利用安全传感器构建许可输出集，并为每个攻击假设构建攻击输出集；随后引入滚动时域优化框架设计暴露输入（通过控制信道注入的有界辅助扰动），以根据分离趋势增大两组集合之间的分离；推导出充分检测条件证明集合分离保证传感器可检测性；并提供离线暴露预算指导。

Result: 在无人机导航系统中模拟GNSS和LiDAR隐蔽攻击，实验验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 集合分离是实现传感器融合CPS中隐蔽攻击可检测性的关键条件，基于滚动时域优化的暴露输入设计能有效检测同时被攻陷的多传感器攻击。

Abstract: This paper investigates the stealthy attack detection for sensor-fusion cyber-physical systems with unknown-but-bounded noises through the control channel. The detection framework is particularly applicable to sensor-fusion scenarios in which multiple suspicious sensors contributing to the fused estimate may be compromised simultaneously. First, we construct an admissible output set using secure sensors and an attack output set for each attack hypothesis. Then, we introduce a receding-horizon optimization framework to design exposure inputs, namely bounded auxiliary control perturbations injected through the control channel, so as to enlarge the separation between the admissible output set and the attack output sets according to the separation tendency. A sufficient detection condition is further derived, showing that set separation guarantees detectability of the compromised sensors. Moreover, an offline exposure budget guidance is developed to support budget selection before online exposure starts. Simulations on a UAV navigation system under stealthy GNSS and LiDAR attacks validate the proposed method.

</details>


### [237] [Economic Model Predictive Control with Policy-Guided Terminal Ingredients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02628)
*Salim Msaad,Robert D. McAllister*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种称为"策略引导MPC"(policy-guided MPC)的新型模型预测控制框架，通过使用已知的次优控制策略来构建终端代价和终端约束，从而摆脱了传统MPC对稳态或参考轨迹的依赖。该方法建立了相对于引导策略的闭环性能保证，适用于有限和无限时域问题，并通过能源管理数值仿真验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模型预测控制（MPC）的设计通常依赖稳态推导出的终端代价和终端约束来保证闭环稳定性和性能。然而，这种对稳态假设的依赖性限制了该方法在缺乏固定工作点或不需要固定工作点的系统中的应用。作者希望克服这一局限性，使得MPC能够应用于不依赖稳态假设的更广泛系统。

Method: 提出了一种称为"策略引导MPC"的新框架：利用一个已知的次优控制策略来构造终端代价和终端约束。具体而言，终端区域围绕该策略展开（rollout）所确定的中心来定义，并以偏离该中心的惩罚来定义终端代价。这种方法无需稳态或参考轨迹。该框架同时适用于有限时域和无限时域问题，并建立了相对于引导策略的闭环性能保证。

Result: 论文建立了该框架在有限和无限时域问题中相对于引导策略的闭环性能保证，并通过一个能源管理数值仿真示例验证了所提出框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 策略引导MPC为无需稳态假设的模型预测控制提供了一种可行的新设计范式，通过利用已知的次优控制策略构建终端组件，扩展了MPC在无固定工作点系统中的适用范围，同时保持可量化的闭环性能保证。

Abstract: Conventional designs for model predictive control typically rely on terminal costs and constraints derived from a steady state to guarantee closed-loop stability and performance. However, this dependence on a steady-state assumption limits the applicability of this control method to systems in which such a fixed operating point is either not available or not desirable. This work introduces a novel framework, termed policy-guided MPC, to address this limitation. Our approach constructs terminal costs and constraints using a known sub-optimal control policy. Specifically, the terminal region is defined around a center determined by a rollout of the policy, and a penalty on deviation from this center is used to define the terminal cost. This method obviates the need for a steady state or reference trajectory. Closed-loop performance guarantees are established relative to the guiding policy, for both finite and infinite horizon problems. The effectiveness of the proposed framework is demonstrated through numerical simulations on an energy management example.

</details>


### [238] [Guidelines for the implementation of power oscillation damping controllers in power converters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02810)
*Javier Renedo,Macarena Martín Almenta,Sergio Martínez Villanueva,Agustín Díaz-García,Antonio Cordón,Davide Gotti*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文为电压源换流器（VSCs）中功率振荡阻尼（POD）控制器的实际应用提供了实施指南。作者提出了合规准则，并采用合成测试系统和西班牙技术标准（NTS）的系统化方法来验证POD控制器的有效性，分析了基于有功功率调制（POD-P）、无功功率调制（POD-Q）及二者联合（POD-PQ）三种策略，并在大规模电力系统中验证了结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: POD控制器在抑制电力系统区间振荡方面潜力巨大，但其有效性强烈依赖于参数整定，正确实施并非易事。现有研究缺乏针对实际电力系统应用的系统化实施指南和合规准则，亟需建立一套可操作的评估方法。

Method: 作者提出：(1) 基于合成测试系统的POD控制器合规准则；(2) 借鉴西班牙NTS技术标准的系统化监控合规方法论；(3) 采用具有电网跟随（GFL）控制的通用功率换流器进行仿真分析；(4) 对POD-P、POD-Q和POD-PQ三种控制器类型分别评估；最后在大规模电力系统中进行验证。

Result: 数值算例展示了合成测试系统中POD控制器的合规标准。三种配置（POD-P、POD-Q、POD-PQ）均在分析中评估，且结果在大型电力系统中得到验证，表明所提出的方法论和准则可行且有效。

Conclusion: 通过使用合适的合成系统、方法论和合规准则，POD控制器在功率换流器中能够有效抑制大规模电力系统的机电振荡，为实际工程应用提供了可参考的实施框架。

Abstract: One of the most effective ways to damp electromechanical oscillations in power systems is by means of supplementary controllers attached to the different devices in the power system is by means of power system stabilizers (PSS) in synchronous machines or by means of power oscillation damping (POD) controllers in facilities with power converters. In the recent years, the use of POD controllers in voltage source converters (VSCs) with grid-following (GFL) control has been investigated. Although the potential of POD controllers to help to damp inter-area oscillations in power systems is enormous, their correct implementation is not trivial, because their effectiveness is strongly linked to their settings. This paper provides guidelines for the implementation of POD controllers in power converters for application in real-world power systems. The paper proposes compliance criteria for POD controllers using a synthetic test system and a systematic methodology used in Spanish technical standard for monitoring compliance (NTS), considering practical considerations. The paper also includes numerical examples to illustrate compliance criteria for POD controllers in a synthetic test system. A generic power converter with grid-following (GFL) control is used for the analysis by simulation and POD controllers using modulation of active-power injection (POD-P), reactive-power injection (POD-Q) or both simultaneously (POD-PQ) will be analysed. Results were validated in a large-scale power system. The paper concludes that by using appropriate synthetic systems, methodologies and compliance criteria, POD controllers in power converters could be effective to damp electromechanical oscillation in large-scale power systems.

</details>


### [239] [Tests on the POD-P controller of INELFE Spain-France VSC-HVDC interconnector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02838)
*Javier Renedo,Agustín Diaz-García,Gilles Torresan,Eduardo Lorenzo Cabrera,Antonio Cordón,Silvia Sanz Verdugo,Juan Peiró,Javier Pérez Castro,Gorka Calvo,Aitor Hernández Sautua,Ambroise Petit,Hamza Oukhayi,Samy Akkari,Hani Saad*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文介绍了INELFE VSC-HVDC互联线路中POD-P控制器（功率振荡阻尼控制器）的改进工作，包括提高增益以及使其能够与角度差控制（ADC）配合使用，并通过仿真研究和现场试验验证了不同运行模式下的系统稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机电振荡（功率振荡）是电力系统面临的主要问题之一。INELFE VSC-HVDC互联线路（法国-西班牙，2x1000 MW）虽然已有POD-P控制器来阻尼功率振荡，但其有效性有待提高。因此需要改进控制器以提高其对欧洲大陆电力系统区域间振荡阻尼的贡献。

Method: 西班牙和法国输电系统运营商（TSOs）联合开展工作：（1）提高INELFE VSC-HVDC线路POD-P控制器的增益；（2）使POD-P控制器能够与角度差控制（ADC）同时使用。通过广泛的仿真研究和不同运行模式下的现场试验来验证系统的正确行为。

Result: 论文展示了POD-P控制器在不同运行模式下的仿真研究和现场试验结果，验证了改进后的控制器在增强区域间振荡阻尼方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过提高增益并允许POD-P控制器与ADC配合使用，成功增强了INELFE VSC-HVDC互联线路对欧洲大陆电力系统区域间功率振荡的阻尼贡献，且经过仿真与现场测试验证了系统的可靠行为。

Abstract: INELFE interconnector consists of a 2x1000 MW high voltage direct current system based on voltage source converters (VSC-HVDC) interconnecting France and Spain. INELFE VSC-HVDC link is embedded into the high voltage alternating current (HVAC) system. Electromechanical oscillations, also known as power oscillations, are a major concern worldwide. INELFE VSC-HVDC link has specific controllers to damp power oscillations by modulating active (P)- and reactive (Q)-power injections of the VSC converters (POD-P and POD-Q controllers, respectively). The Spanish and French Transmission System Operators (TSOs) carried out a join task force to - Increase the gain of POD-P controller of INELFE VSC-HVDC link. - Make it possible to use the POD-P controller together with angle difference control (ADC) of INELFE VSC-HVDC interconnector. The objective of these modifications is to improve the effectiveness of POD-P controller and, therefore, to increase its contribution to the damping of inter-area oscillations in the Continental Europe (CE) power system. Such changes require (a) extensive simulation studies and (b) extensive tests in different operation modes, in order to ensure the correct behavior of the system. This paper presents simulation studies and field tests on the POD-P controller of INELFE VSC-HVDC interconnector in different modes of operation.

</details>


<div id='econ.EM'></div>

# econ.EM [[Back]](#toc)

### [240] [Robust Variance Estimation in Linear Regression: A Projection-Geometry Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01804)
*Yanping Chen*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于投影几何的框架用于稳健方差估计。传统OLS残差作为未观测误差的代理，在回归投影相对于误差依赖结构是非局部时可能失效。作者将方差估计表述为线性逆问题，提出Riesz方差估计器，结合簇内和簇间残差矩，通过迭代算法计算，无需显式矩阵求逆，在模拟和实际应用中均优于传统HC和CRVE方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性回归推断中，OLS残差被当作未观测误差的代理，但当回归投影相对误差依赖结构是"非局部"时，这种近似会失败——残差化会在观测和簇之间转移协方差信息。传统HC和CRVE仅保留对角或簇内残差矩，可能低估抽样不确定性，导致推断不可靠。

Method: 1) 提出投影几何框架：将OLS估计量的方差精确表示为潜在协方差块的Riesz泛函；2) 通过由完整回归投影决定的线性算子，将可观测残差矩与目标方差联系；3) 将方差估计转化为线性逆问题并求解；4) 提出Riesz方差估计器，结合簇内与跨簇残差矩；5) 采用迭代算法计算，避免显式矩阵求逆，且在簇级杠杆矩阵奇异时依然良定义。

Result: 模拟实验表明，在投影溢出（projection spillovers）存在时，传统HC和CRVE方法存在显著的低覆盖问题，而提出的估计器能恢复接近名义水平的覆盖。在殖民地总督晋升的实际应用案例中，该修正改变了五个报告系数中四个的显著性。

Conclusion: 传统HC和CRVE本质上是对投影溢出可忽略情况下的受限近似。本文提出的Riesz方差估计器能更准确地刻画投影非局部性带来的方差影响，在理论和实证中均显著改善推断可靠性。

Abstract: Inference in linear regression commonly treats OLS residuals as proxies for unobserved errors. This approximation can fail when the regression projection is nonlocal relative to the error-dependence structure. Residualization then shifts covariance information across observations and clusters, while conventional heteroskedasticity-consistent (HC) and cluster-robust variance estimators (CRVE) retain only diagonal or within-cluster residual moments and may therefore understate sampling uncertainty.
  This paper develops a projection-geometry framework for robust variance estimation. The variance of the OLS estimator is represented exactly as a Riesz functional of latent covariance blocks, and observable residual moments are linked to the target through a linear operator determined by the full regression projection. This formulation reduces variance estimation to a linear inverse problem. I propose a Riesz variance estimator that combines within- and cross-cluster residual moments. Conventional HC and CRVE emerge as restricted approximations whose validity depends on negligible projection spillovers.
  The estimator remains well defined when cluster-specific leverage matrices are singular and is computed by an iterative algorithm that avoids explicit matrix inversion. Simulations show substantial undercoverage by conventional methods under projection spillovers, whereas the proposed estimator restores near-nominal coverage. In an application to colonial governor promotions, the correction changes the significance of four of five reported coefficients.

</details>


### [241] [Headline Estimation with Multiple Research Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01943)
*Vod Vilfort*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文研究当研究者面对多个研究设计时，如何选择"头条估计值"（headline estimate）作为标量参数的汇总报告。当不清楚哪个设计最适合该参数时，作者提出一种基于最小化最坏情况后验风险（minimax posterior risk）的决策框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者面对多个研究设计时，往往需要从中选择一个结果作为参数的头条估计。但当哪个设计真正有效并不明确时，如何选择头条估计缺乏系统性指导。作者希望为这种"设计不确定性"提供一套决策理论框架。

Method: 作者建模假设：(i) 恰好有一个设计对参数有效；(ii) 研究者对哪个设计有效存在模糊性，由一组先验分布类来刻画。在这些假设下，提出选取最小化最坏情况下后验风险的头条估计作为决策准则。

Result: 在三个实际应用中，作者展示了考虑模糊性在某些情况下会实质性改变定量结论，而在另一些情况下现有的头条估计已接近最优。

Conclusion: 该框架为跨设计汇总估计提供了一种稳健的决策方法，考虑了设计不确定性（design ambiguity），其实际影响因应用场景而异——有时显著，有时有限。

Abstract: To study a scalar parameter, a researcher may consider multiple research designs. Based on the evidence across designs, the researcher may wish to formulate a headline estimate of the parameter. I examine how to choose this headline when it is unclear which design is most appropriate for studying the parameter. I model this setting by assuming that (i) exactly one of the designs is valid for the parameter and (ii) the researcher has ambiguity about which design is valid, represented by a class of priors over the candidate designs. To account for ambiguity, I propose reporting the headline estimate that minimizes the worst-case posterior risk over the class of priors. In three applications, I show cases where accounting for ambiguity materially affects the quantitative conclusion and cases where an existing headline is already close to optimal.

</details>


### [242] [Estimation risk in conditional expectiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02673)
*Marcelo Fernandes,Vitor Henriques,Eduardo Fonseca Mendes*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文建立了条件尺度模型下两步条件期望分位数（expectile）估计量的一致性和渐近正态性。首先通过准极大似然估计条件方差参数，再利用标准化残差的经验分布计算创新的无条件期望分位数。研究揭示了用标准化残差替代真实创新对条件和非条件期望分位数估计量渐近方差的影响，并通过实证分析表明条件期望分位数比基于分位数的传统风险度量（如VaR和ES）更稳健地评估加密市场的尾部风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 期望分位数（expectile）相比传统分位数风险度量在尾部风险评估中具有更优的性质，但在条件尺度模型框架下的统计推断性质尚不明确，需要进行严格的渐近理论分析。

Method: 采用两步估计方法：第一步用准极大似然估计条件方差参数，第二步用标准化残差的经验分布来估计创新的无条件期望分位数，并推导相应的渐近理论。

Result: 建立了条件期望分位数估计量的一致性和渐近正态性，量化了标准化残差替代真实创新对渐近方差的影响；实证表明条件期望分位数在加密市场尾部风险评估中比VaR和ES更稳健。

Conclusion: 条件期望分位数在条件尺度模型框架下具有完备的统计推断理论基础，并且在加密市场尾部风险度量中展现出优于传统分位数方法的稳健性，为风险度量提供了更具吸引力的替代方案。

Abstract: We establish the consistency and asymptotic normality of a two-step estimator of conditional expectiles in the context of conditional scale models. We first estimate the conditional variance parameters by quasi-maximum likelihood and then compute the unconditional expectile of the innovations using the empirical distribution of the standardized residuals. We show how replacing true innovations with standardized residuals affects the asymptotic variances of both conditional and unconditional expectile estimators. Finally, our empirical analysis reveals that conditional expectiles assess tail risk in cryptomarkets in a more robust manner than traditional quantile-based risk measures, such as value at risk and expected shortfall.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [243] [New strategies for some pursuit-evasion differential games with Gronwall-type constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01922)
*Jamilu Adamu,Mehdi Salimi,Farouq Abba Wabi,Jewaidu Rilwan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了定义在序列空间l₂中的一类非合作追逃微分博弈。追捕者和逃避者的运动由一阶微分方程描述，其控制函数受Gronwall型约束限制。文章分别构建了追捕者策略和逃避者策略，并给出了追捕完成与成功逃避的充分条件，将经典几何与积分约束下的微分博弈结果推广到了更广泛的Gronwall型约束系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典微分博弈通常考虑几何约束或积分约束下的控制限制，但实际工程与博弈场景中控制资源往往具有历史依赖性（即过去控制消耗会影响当前可用控制），因此需要将博弈理论推广到Gronwall型约束这类更宽泛的限制条件之下，以覆盖更一般的控制资源分配问题。

Method: 在序列空间l₂中建立追逃微分博弈模型，控制函数满足Gronwall型（历史相关）约束。对于追捕问题，构造了追捕者的新策略并推导追捕完成的充分条件；对于逃避问题，设计了逃避者的可行策略并导出成功逃避的充分条件。

Result: 分别针对追捕问题和逃避问题获得了相应的充分条件，证明在给定策略下追捕能够完成或逃避能够成功，从而将几何约束和积分约束下的经典结果推广至Gronwall型约束系统。

Conclusion: 论文成功扩展了微分博弈理论的应用范围，使具有历史依赖控制约束（Gronwall型）的博弈场景也能得到系统的追捕与逃避策略分析，具有更强的建模一般性和理论推广价值。

Abstract: This paper studies a class of non-cooperative pursuit-evasion differential games in the sequence space $l_{2}.$ The motions of the pursuer and the evader are described by certain first-order differential equations while their control functions are subject to Gronwall-type constraints which impose history-dependent bounds on their control resources. Two fundamental problems are investigated. For the pursuit problem, new strategy for the pursuer is constructed and sufficient conditions guaranteeing the completion of pursuit are established. For evasion problem, an admissible strategy of the evader ensuring avoidance of capture is obtained, and corresponding sufficient conditions for successful evasion are derived. The proposed approached extends existing results on differential games with classical geometric and integral constraints to a broader class of systems governed by Gronwall-type restrictions.

</details>


### [244] [A Hereditary Property of Cutting Plane Procedures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02038)
*Gérard Cornuéjols,Vrishabh Patil*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文系统地研究了凸集在切割平面程序下闭包的遗传性质（hereditary property），即对K的每个面F，有F' = K' ∩ F。作者建立了两个充分条件：第一个适用于可表示为从格自由凸集族导出的交集割闭包的程序，涵盖了split、lift-and-project、Lovász-Schrijver、Sherali-Adams和Lasserre闭包在一般凸集上的情形（包括非暴露面）；第二个适用于割为Gomory角多面体有效不等式的程序，涵盖了所有基导出的Dantzig割闭包。同时，文章展示了四种遗传性质不成立的经典程序：Gomory分数割闭包、混合整数Chvátal闭包、+割闭包以及可行基导出的Dantzig割闭包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遗传性质是切割平面程序的核心理论性质，它支撑着有限秩和闭包多面体性的归纳证明，也是抽象切割平面框架中的可容许性要求。然而，这一性质从未被系统地研究过，不同经典切割平面程序是否满足该性质缺乏统一的认知。

Method: 本文通过两个充分条件来统一处理：第一个条件适用于可表达为从一族格自由凸集导出的交集割的闭包，要求单一族同时实现K及其每个面的闭包；第二个条件适用于割为Gomory角多面体有效不等式的程序。通过正面验证满足条件的程序，并构造反例来证明显式的不满足案例。

Result: 证明了split、lift-and-project、Lovász-Schrijver、Sherali-Adams和Lasserre闭包（在一般凸集和非暴露面上）以及所有基导出的Dantzig割闭包满足遗传性质；同时证明了Gomory分数割闭包、混合整数Chvátal闭包、+割闭包和可行基导出的Dantzig割闭包不满足该性质。

Conclusion: 该研究首次系统地划分了切割平面程序中满足遗传性质与不满足遗传性质的边界，为后续在抽象框架中正确应用遗传性质以及理解各类闭包的结构特性提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Let $K'$ denote the closure of a convex set $K$ under a given cutting-plane procedure. The procedure satisfies the \emph{hereditary property} if $F' = K' \cap F$ for every face $F$ of $K$. The property underlies inductive proofs of finite-rank and the polyhedrality of closures, and it is an admissibility requirement in abstract frameworks for cutting-plane procedures. Yet, it has not been studied systematically across well-known cutting-plane procedures. We establish two sufficient conditions for the property. The first applies to procedures that can be expressed as closures under intersection cuts from a family of lattice-free convex sets, when a single family realizes the closure of $K$ and of each of its faces. It yields the hereditary property for the split, lift-and-project, Lovász--Schrijver, Sherali--Adams, and Lasserre closures, applied over general convex sets and for faces that need not be exposed. The second applies to procedures whose cuts are valid inequalities for Gomory's corner polyhedron, and it yields the hereditary property for the closure of Dantzig cuts derived from all bases. We complement these results with four classical procedures for which the hereditary property fails, namely the closure of Gomory's fractional cuts (derived from either all bases or feasible bases only), the mixed-integer Chvátal closure, the $+$-cut closure, and the closure of Dantzig cuts derived from feasible bases.

</details>


### [245] [Stochastic Optimal Control Problem under Inside Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02164)
*Yuxin Ye,Jingtao Shi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究内幕信息下的随机最优控制问题。控制过程依赖于一个基于F_T0可测随机变量Y的静态内幕信息，并适应于由布朗运动和Y生成的扩展滤波。由于非适应设定下传统随机积分失效，论文采用前向积分来表述系统中的随机积分项。通过Donsker delta函数，将原受控系统转化为y参数化系统，并建立了前向SDE解的存在性，利用流变换技术证明解的唯一性。在Y满足高斯假设下，推导了必要和充分最优性条件，进而构建内幕信息下的LQ最优控制问题，通过y参数化变换转化为随机系数LQ控制问题，最后给出数值算例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在内幕信息场景下，控制过程依赖于未来的可测随机变量，导致系统不再适应于原始滤波，传统Itô随机积分无法适用。这需要新的数学框架来处理非适应随机控制系统，并推导相应的最优性条件。

Method: 采用前向积分处理非适应随机积分项；借助Donsker delta函数将受控系统转化为y参数化系统；通过流变换技术证明前向SDE解的存在唯一性；在高斯假设下利用变分方法推导必要与充分最优性条件；将LQ问题转化为带随机系数的LQ控制问题以求解。

Result: 建立了前向SDE解的存在性与唯一性；在Y为高斯随机变量条件下获得了内幕信息随机最优控制问题的必要条件（如伴随方程与变分不等式形式）和充分条件；LQ问题通过y参数化转化为随机系数LQ问题后给出了显式数值算例，验证了理论结果的有效性。

Conclusion: 该论文为内幕信息下的随机最优控制问题提供了系统的数学框架，解决了非适应随机积分困难，给出了可行的最优性条件并验证了LQ情形的实用性，对金融等领域中利用内幕信息进行最优决策具有理论支撑意义。

Abstract: This paper is concerned with a stochastic optimal control problem under inside information. The control process depends on an $\mathcal{F}_{T_0}$-measurable random variable $Y$, representing the static inside information, and is adapted to the enlarged filtration generated by the underlying Brownian motion and the random variable $Y$. Accordingly, the traditional stochastic integral fails to be well-defined in this non-adapted setting; we adopt forward integrals to formulate the stochastic integral terms in the system. By means of the Donsker delta function, the original controlled system is transformed into a $y$-parameterized system. We further establish the existence of solutions to forward \textit{stochastic differential equations} (SDEs), and prove the uniqueness of solutions via flow transformation techniques. Under a Gaussian assumption on $Y$, we derive both necessary and sufficient optimality conditions for the aforementioned control problem. Subsequently, we formulate the \textit{linear-quadratic} (LQ) optimal control problem under inside information. Through the $y$-parameterized transformation, the original problem is converted into an LQ control problem with random coefficients. A numerical example for the LQ case is provided at the end to validate our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [246] [Characterizations and Complexity of Minimum Forward and Integer Cycle Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02317)
*Gabor Riccardi,Niels Lindner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了有向图中前向环基（forward cycle bases）的最小权重问题，给出了弱基本与基本前向环基存在的完整结构刻画，证明了最小权重前向弱基本环基问题是APX难的，并引入了"opt-in"图族（任意权重下最小环基均为整数的图），证明该图族是minor-closed的，可通过有限个禁止子图刻画，且K_n是opt-in当且仅当n≤7。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决最小环基问题相关的多个开放问题，包括最小权重整数环基问题，以及最小权重弱基本和严格基本前向环基问题的计算复杂度。

Method: 首先给出允许弱基本（因此也是整数）前向环基的有向图的完整结构刻画；证明强连通有向图存在前向基本环基当且仅当有向环的基数等于环秩，且此时基唯一；通过L-归约证明最小权重前向弱基本环基问题是APX难的。其次引入opt-in图族，证明其minor-closed性质，利用Robertson-Seymour定理得到有限禁止子图刻画，并提出算法检测图是否为opt-in及识别其禁止子图。

Result: 给出了前向环基存在的结构刻画；证明了当存在时最小权重前向基本环基可在多项式时间内求解，而最小权重前向弱基本环基问题是APX难的；证明了opt-in图族是minor-closed的；提出算法验证了完全图K_n是opt-in当且仅当n≤7。

Conclusion: 本文系统地解决了前向环基的几个开放复杂度问题，并在图族的minor-closed结构与有限禁止子图刻画方面取得了重要进展，为图论中环基问题的理论和算法研究提供了新的视角与工具。

Abstract: The cycle space of a directed graph is generated by a cycle basis, where, in general, cycles are allowed to have both forward and backward arcs. In a forward cycle, all arcs must follow the given direction. Several open questions remain regarding the complexity of the minimum cycle basis problem, in particular the minimum-weight integral cycle basis problem, and the minimum-weight weakly and strictly fundamental forward cycle basis problems. In this paper, we address these open questions. First, we study the existence, structure, and computational complexity of minimum-weight forward cycle bases. We give a complete structural characterization of digraphs that admit weakly fundamental (and hence integral) forward cycle bases. We further provide a characterization when a strongly connected digraph admits a forward fundamental cycle basis, proving that such a basis exists if and only if the set of directed cycles has cardinality equal to the cycle rank; in this case, the basis is unique. Lastly, we show that while minimum-weight forward fundamental cycle bases can be found in polynomial time whenever they exist, the minimum-weight forward weakly fundamental cycle basis problem is APX-hard via an L-reduction from the minimum-weight weakly fundamental cycle basis problem on digraphs with metric weights. Second, we introduce opt-in graphs, i.e., the family of graphs for which minimum cycle bases are integral for any weight function. We show that this family is minor-closed and hence, by the Robertson-Seymour theorem, is characterized by a finite set of forbidden minors, so that the opt-in recognition problem is solvable in polynomial time. Lastly, we present an algorithm to check whether a graph is opt-in, and if not, to identify which of its minors belong to the set of forbidden minors. Applying this algorithm, we show that the complete graph $K_n$ is opt-in if and only if $n \leq 7$.

</details>


### [247] [A simple derivation of the Kalman filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02332)
*Marco Chiani,Giovanni Petris,Moe Z. Win*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提供了一种简洁且自包含的离散时间卡尔曼滤波器方程推导方法，仅需最小二乘估计的基础知识，在降低数学复杂度的同时保持严谨性和通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的卡尔曼滤波器推导通常涉及较高的数学门槛（如高斯分布、马尔可夫性质等），作者希望提供一种更易理解、数学开销最小的推导方式，使读者仅凭最小二乘估计的基础知识即可掌握卡尔曼滤波器的核心方程。

Method: 从最小二乘估计的基本原理出发，以自包含的方式逐步推导离散时间卡尔曼滤波器的方程，设计上刻意最小化数学辅助工具的引入，同时保持推导的严格性和一般适用性。

Result: 给出了一套完整、简洁的离散时间卡尔曼滤波器方程推导流程，降低了学习门槛，使具备最少数学预备知识的读者也能理解。

Conclusion: 该讲稿证明了卡尔曼滤波器方程可以通过最小二乘估计这一基础工具独立推导出来，在减少数学复杂性的同时不牺牲推导的严谨性与通用性。

Abstract: In this lecture note, we present a concise and self-contained derivation of the discrete-time Kalman filter equations that requires only a basic understanding of least squares estimation. The treatment is designed to minimize mathematical overhead while preserving both rigor and generality.

</details>


### [248] [On the invariance of risk-sensitive LQR gain under input randomization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02363)
*Yeongjun Jang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明风险敏感线性二次调节器（LQR）问题的最优增益在输入随机化下保持不变，即控制器向名义控制输入中主动注入噪声不会改变最优增益。尽管确定性等价原理在此类问题中不成立且输入噪声会放大有效过程噪声，但增益仍被保留，因为输入噪声同时进入系统动态和代价函数，其总效应最终抵消。因此最优增益及其Riccati递推无需重新计算，最优代价的增量可闭式评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风险敏感LQR在输入随机化应用（如隐私保护的水印、差分隐私、路径积分控制）中广泛使用，但输入随机化是否会改变最优控制增益尚不明确。直觉上由于确定性等价不成立且输入随机化放大噪声，增益似乎应改变，因此需要严谨的理论分析。

Method: 通过理论分析证明风险敏感LQR最优增益在输入随机化下的不变性。关键洞察在于：输入噪声不仅进入系统动力学方程，也进入代价函数，这两方面的总效应在求解最优增益时相互抵消，从而使增益保持不变。

Result: 严格证明了风险敏感LQR的最优增益与相关Riccati递推在输入随机化下保持不变，且最优成本的增量可通过闭式表达式直接计算，无需重新求解优化问题。

Conclusion: 该发现使风险敏感LQR能够直接应用于需要输入随机化的场景（如重放攻击检测的水印、差分隐私和路径积分控制），无需重新计算最优增益，显著降低计算成本并简化实现流程。

Abstract: This paper shows that the optimal gain of the risk-sensitive linear quadratic regulator (LQR) problem is invariant under input randomization, i.e., when the controller deliberately injects noise into the nominal control input. This appears counterintuitive at first glance because certainty equivalence does not hold for risk-sensitive LQR and input randomization inflates the effective process noise. Nonetheless, the gain is preserved because the input noise enters not only the system dynamics but also the cost functional, and its total effect on the gain eventually vanishes. Consequently, the optimal gain and its associated Riccati recursion need not be recomputed, and the increment in the optimal cost can be readily evaluated in closed form. This result facilitates the use of risk-sensitive LQR in applications that employ input randomization for privacy or exploration, such as watermarking for replay attack detection, differential privacy, and path integral control.

</details>


### [249] [On the Duality between Feature and Sample Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02418)
*Thu-Le Tran*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过引入Fenchel-Rockafellar表示（一类包含Lasso和SVM的凸优化问题族），建立了特征筛选与样本筛选之间的严格等价关系。作者证明这两者在对偶变换下构成等变对：先对偶化再特征筛选等于先样本筛选再对偶化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特征筛选和样本筛选分别通过消除无关特征和非信息样本来降低机器学习成本。尽管它们被认为是原-对偶对应关系，但这种关系此前是非正式的且依赖特定模型，缺乏统一的理论框架来阐明两者之间的深层联系。

Method: 作者将筛选和对偶视为目标函数的变换，引入Fenchel-Rockafellar (FR)表示——一类包含Lasso和SVM的凸问题类，该类在这两种变换下封闭。基于此框架，证明特征筛选和样本筛选构成等变对。

Result: 严格证明了：对偶化后进行特征筛选等价于先进行样本筛选再对偶化。这一结果将此前模糊的、模型依赖的对应关系提升为形式化的数学定理。

Conclusion: 通过FR表示建立了特征筛选与样本筛选的统一理论框架，将两者之间的非正式关联转化为严格的等变性证明，为各种判别式机器学习模型中的筛选理论提供了普适基础。

Abstract: Feature and sample screening reduce the cost of machine learning by eliminating irrelevant features and noninformative samples, respectively. Although recognized as primal-dual counterparts, their relationship remains informal and model-dependent. Viewing screening and duality as transformations of objective functions, we introduce Fenchel-Rockafellar (FR) representations, a class of convex problems encompassing the Lasso and SVM that is closed under both transformations. We then prove that feature and sample screening form an equivariant pair: dualization followed by feature screening is equal to sample screening followed by dualization.

</details>


### [250] [Accelerated Convergence of a Second-Order Dynamical System and its Application to Splitting Algorithms for Comonotone Inclusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02479)
*Yan Tang,Jun Dong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种由前向-后向分裂算子驱动的二阶动力系统，用于求解实Hilbert空间中结构包含问题0∈(A+B)(x)，其中A为极大ρ-余单调算子，B为ν-余强制性算子。该系统具备良定性，Lyapunov分析给出了速度的o(1/t)和前后向残差的o(1/t²)加速收敛率，且轨迹弱收敛于zer(A+B)。时间离散化导出一类双惯性Halpern前向-后向分裂算法，涵盖经典前向-后向分裂法及其惯性变体。数值实验验证了算法在分裂可行性、稀疏信号恢复和图像去模糊任务中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的连续动力系统方法在处理结构包含问题时，通常在单调性与收敛速度之间存在权衡。本文旨在通过引入前向-后向分裂算子驱动的二阶动力系统，在更一般的余单调/余强制性算子假设下实现加速收敛，并桥接连续时间系统与离散时间算法之间的鸿沟。

Method: 构造二阶动力系统，其中基于前向-后向分裂算子；建立系统的良定性；通过Lyapunov能量函数分析获得加速收敛率（速度o(1/t)、残差o(1/t²)）和弱收敛性；对系统进行隐式/时间离散化，推导出双惯性Halpern前向-后向分裂算法族，涵盖经典方法作为特例。

Result: 理论结果表明：轨迹弱收敛到zer(A+B)；速度以o(1/t)收敛，前向-后向残差以o(1/t²)收敛。数值实验在分裂可行性、稀疏信号恢复和图像去模糊上展示了所提算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的二阶动力系统及其离散化算法在放宽算子假设（余单调与余强制）的同时，仍能保持加速收敛率，并通过包括双惯性机制增强算法性能，为结构包含问题的求解提供了一种新框架。

Abstract: This paper introduces a novel second-order dynamical system driven by a forward-backward splitting operator for solving the structured inclusion $0\in(A+B)(x)$ in a real Hilbert space, where $A$ is a maximal $ρ$-comonotone operator and $B$ is a $ν$-cocoercive operator. The well-posedness of the system is established, and Lyapunov analysis yields accelerated convergence rates of order $o(\frac{1}{t})$ for the velocity and $o(\frac{1}{t^2})$ for the forward-backward residual, together with weak convergence of the trajectories to $\operatorname{zer}(A+B)$. Temporal discretization further leads to a class of double inertial Halpern forward-backward splitting algorithms that encompasses the classical forward-backward splitting method and its inertial variants as special cases. Numerical experiments on split feasibility, sparse signal recovery, and image deblurring illustrate the effectiveness of the proposed algorithm.

</details>


### [251] [Learning Parametric Monotone Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02494)
*Alberto Bemporad,Tatiana Tatarenko*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究如何从数据中学习一个参数化的单调纳什均衡（NE）问题。针对存在局部和共享凸约束的情况，单调性保证可以高效计算学习到的博弈的广义纳什均衡。作者提出两种学习方法：一是基于惩罚的方法，在训练中促进博弈的单调性；二是基于作者引入的表征定理，通过参数化代理成本函数使得单调性在构造上得到保证（对所有参数值无条件成立）。通过多个数值实验验证了方法的有效性，并提供了Python库和复现代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多实际应用中，博弈参与者的成本函数未知，只能从数据中观测到其行为或成本样本。如果能够学习到一个保证单调性的参数化NE问题，就能高效求解广义纳什均衡，从而用于预测、控制和决策。然而，直接从数据学习的博弈未必保证单调性，这会导致求解困难，因此需要设计专门方法保证学习结果的结构性质。

Method: 本文提出两种方法：(i) 基于惩罚的方法——在训练过程中加入惩罚项以促进所学博弈的单调性；(ii) 基于表征定理的方法——该表征定理覆盖了一大类单调博弈，通过将代理成本参数化，使得NE问题的单调性在构造上天然成立，与训练数据无关，对所有参数值都成立。这两种方法分别对应两个学习场景：直接学习NE问题（从成本样本）和逆学习（从最佳响应样本学习代理成本）。

Result: 通过多个数值示例验证了所提方法的有效性和适用性，展示了两种方法在保证单调性的前提下均能成功地从数据中学习NE问题。同时提供了公开的Python库及文中所有示例的复现代码。

Conclusion: 本文为解决"从数据学习参数化单调博弈"这一任务提供了系统性方法，既可通过惩罚项软性促进单调性，也可通过表征定理从构造上严格保证单调性。这项工作为数据驱动的博弈论建模提供了实用工具，并开源了代码以促进可复现性。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning from data a parametric Nash equilibrium (NE) problem that is monotone (or strongly monotone) for all parameter values. In the presence of local and shared convex constraints, monotonicity enables efficient computation of generalized Nash equilibria of the learned game. We consider two learning scenarios: (i) direct learning of the NE problem from samples of the agents' costs, and (ii) inverse learning of surrogate agents' costs from samples of their best responses. We propose two methods to solve these tasks. The first is a penalty-based approach that promotes monotonicity of the learned game during training. The second, based on a representation theorem we introduce for a broad class of monotone games, parameterizes the agents' costs so that monotonicity of the NE problem is guaranteed by construction, for all parameter values, regardless of the training data used. We illustrate the applicability of the proposed methods on several numerical examples. A Python library and the examples reported in the paper are available at https://github.com/bemporad/learn_monotone_games.

</details>


### [252] [Projected Subgradient Methods for a Class of Nonsmooth and Nonconvex Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02517)
*Christian Kanzow,Jannis Krüger,Leo Lehmann*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究在非空闭集（但不一定凸）上最小化满足非光滑下降引理的非光滑函数的优化问题。目标函数属于上-𝒞²函数类，约束可促进稀疏或低秩结构。提出了两种全局化策略的投影次梯度方法，并证明了收敛到比次微分和规则预期更强的平稳点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的非光滑优化方法通常需要凸性或光滑性假设，而实际应用中（如稀疏/低秩优化、MPEC问题、MAXCUT和Robust PCA）的目标函数和约束集往往是非凸的。需要开发适用于上-𝒞²函数类且约束集非凸的强收敛性保证的优化算法。

Method: 提出投影次梯度方法，包含两种全局化策略：(a) 非单调线搜索；(b) 自条件化方法，步长由过去迭代的数据公式给出。算法针对上-𝒞²函数类在非凸闭集上的最小化问题设计。

Result: 两种方法都收敛到满足比次微分和规则预期更强的平稳性概念的解。在MPEC风格问题、MAXCUT和Robust PCA等矩阵优化问题上展示了有前景的数值结果。

Conclusion: 论文为具有非凸约束的非光滑优化提供了新算法和更强的理论保证，尤其在处理稀疏/低秩结构中表现出良好效果，拓展了投影次梯度方法的应用范围。

Abstract: We investigate the optimization problem of minimizing a nonsmooth function that satisfies a nonsmooth version of the descent lemma over a nonempty and closed but not necessarily convex set. The objective function belongs to the class of upper-$\mathcal{C}^2$ functions, whereas the constraints may promote a sparse or low-rank structure. We propose a projected subgradient method with two different globalization strategies: (a) a nonmonotone linesearch and, under additional assumptions, (b) an auto-conditioned method, where the stepsize is given by a formula depending on data from past iterations. We show that both methods converge to solutions that satisfy a stronger stationarity concept than one would expect from the subdifferential sum-rule, which is particularly important since the optimization problems of interest are inherently nonconvex. Finally, we present promising numerical results when applying the algorithm to an MPEC-style problem as well as the matrix optimization problems MAXCUT and Robust PCA.

</details>


### [253] [Insensitizing Control Problems for Coupled Stochastic Parabolic Systems with State and Gradient Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02712)
*Said Boulite,Abdellatif Elgrou,Abdelaziz Rhandi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究一类耦合线性随机抛物系统的"不敏感控制问题"。作者通过将不敏感控制问题转化为耦合正倒向随机抛物系统的零能控性问题，并利用对偶性将分析归结为相应伴随系统的可观测性不等式。主要分析贡献是建立了含零阶和二阶耦合项的耦合随机抛物系统的新型全局Carleman估计，从而得到所需的可观测性不等式，最终证明不敏感控制的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究随机抛物系统的控制问题是控制理论中的重要课题，特别是对于耦合系统。不敏感控制旨在使某个哨兵泛函（涉及状态变量及其空间梯度的局部观测）对初始数据的微小扰动不敏感，这在实际应用中具有重要意义，例如在需要系统对初始条件不确定因素具有鲁棒性的场景中。

Method: 1) 将不敏感控制问题重新表述为耦合正倒向随机抛物系统的零能控性问题，其中观测项引入了零阶和二阶耦合项；2) 通过对偶性，将问题归约为伴随系统的可观测性不等式；3) 推导含零阶和二阶耦合项的耦合随机抛物系统的新型全局Carleman估计，该估计在控制区和观测区的适当几何假设下成立；4) 根据权重参数β∈[0,1]区分不同情形：β∈{0,1}时哨兵泛函仅依赖一个状态分量，只需一个局部控制作用于第一个方程的漂移项；β∈(0,1)时两个状态分量均参与，需两个局部控制分别作用于两个方程的漂移项；此外还涉及两个作用于扩散项的额外控制。

Result: 在适当的几何假设下，通过新型全局Carleman估计推导出所需的观测不等式，从而证明了不敏感控制的存在性。具体地：当β∈{0,1}时，单个局部漂移控制即可实现不敏感；当β∈(0,1)时，需要两个局部漂移控制。所有情形下还需补充两个作用于扩散项的控制。

Conclusion: 本文成功解决了耦合线性随机抛物系统的不敏感控制问题，其核心创新在于建立了含零阶和二阶耦合项的耦合随机抛物系统的全局Carleman估计。该结果推广了已有的不敏感控制理论，为更复杂的随机耦合系统的控制设计提供了新的数学工具。

Abstract: We study insensitizing control problems for a class of coupled linear stochastic parabolic systems. We establish the existence of controls such that a sentinel functional, involving localized observations of the state variables and their spatial gradients, is insensitive to small perturbations of the null initial data. We first reformulate the insensitizing control problem as a null controllability problem for a coupled forward--backward stochastic parabolic system, in which the observation terms induce both zeroth- and second-order coupling terms. By duality, the analysis is reduced to an observability inequality for the corresponding adjoint system. The main analytical contribution is the derivation of new global Carleman estimates for coupled stochastic parabolic systems with zeroth- and second-order coupling terms, under suitable geometric assumptions on the control and observation regions. These estimates yield the required observability inequalities and, consequently, the existence of insensitizing controls. Furthermore, depending on the value of a weighting parameter $β\in[0,1]$, which determines the relative contributions of the two state components to the sentinel functional, we consider two cases. If $β\in\{0,1\}$, the sentinel functional depends on only one state component, and a single localized control acting in the drift of the first equation is sufficient. In contrast, if $β\in(0,1)$, both state components contribute to the sentinel functional, and two localized controls acting in the drift terms of the two equations are sufficient. Moreover, the control strategy in this paper involves two additional controls acting throughout the diffusion terms.

</details>


### [254] [An Adaptive Projected-Gradient Algorithm for Sample-Average Approximations of Stochastic Multi-Objective Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02722)
*Yiyang Li,Lei Wang,Xiaojun Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无线搜索、无函数值的自适应投影梯度算法（正则化多梯度步长），用于求解随机多目标优化问题的样本均值近似（SAA）。算法通过投影步长更新正则化参数，利用法锥证书获取下降估计和SAA问题Pareto平稳残差的显式复杂度界，并借助SAA梯度一致性将残差收敛传递到总体问题的Pareto平稳性。实验涵盖合成问题、分类、组合选择、多任务学习和机器人控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机多目标优化问题中，每个目标函数都是期望形式且仅能获得样本梯度信息，这使得传统多目标优化算法难以直接应用。现有方法往往需要函数值或线搜索条件，而在随机/样本平均近似场景下这些信息不可用或代价高昂。

Method: 提出一种无线搜索、无函数值的自适应投影梯度算法：每次迭代计算可行的正则化多梯度步，并根据投影步长动态更新正则化参数；利用法锥证书建立下降估计；通过SAA梯度的相合性将SAA问题残差转移到总体问题的Pareto平稳性，并借助集中不等式给出紧集上的有限样本残差界。

Result: 理论上给出了SAA问题Pareto平稳残差的显式复杂度界，以及总体问题Pareto平稳性的相合性保证和有限样本残差界；数值实验在合成问题、分类、投资组合选择、多任务学习和机器人控制任务上验证了算法的实际性能。

Conclusion: 该算法在无需线搜索和函数值的条件下实现了随机多目标优化的高效求解，兼具理论保证（复杂度和统计一致性）与良好的实际应用表现，为随机多目标优化提供了一种普适且实用的求解框架。

Abstract: We consider stochastic multi-objective optimization over a nonempty closed convex set, where every objective is an expectation and only sample-gradient information is available. We develop a line-search-free and function-value-free adaptive projected-gradient algorithm for the sample-average approximation (SAA) problem. Each iteration computes a feasible regularized multi-gradient step and updates the regularization parameter from the projected step length. A normal-cone-based certificate yields descent estimates and an explicit complexity bound for the Pareto-stationarity residual of the SAA problem. The consistency of SAA gradients then transfers vanishing SAA residuals to Pareto stationarity for the population problem, while an additional concentration argument gives a finite-sample residual bound on compact sets. Experiments on synthetic problems, classification, portfolio selection, multi-task learning, and robot control illustrate the practical performance of our algorithm.

</details>


### [255] [Improved Gradient Descent Lower Bounds Beyond Nesterov](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02855)
*Yuhan Ye,Kaizhao Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了光滑凸优化中，使用预定步长的梯度下降（GD）算法能达到的最差收敛速率下界。作者将非任意时刻（non-anytime）下界从O(n^{-1.932})改进到O(n^{-1.6342})，将任意时刻（anytime）下界从O(n^{-4/3})改进到O(n^{-1.2408})，并证明了两种设置之间存在严格的收敛指数分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Nemirovsky和Yudin下界为Ω(n^{-2})，但实际可通过预定步长（如silver schedules）超越。近期研究给出了一些改进的下界结果，但仍有提升空间。本文旨在进一步收紧这些下界，并明确non-anytime与anytime两种设置之间的能力差异。

Method: 作者通过构造更精细的对抗性优化实例（adversarial construction），分别在non-anytime和anytime两种设定下推导出新的第一阶预言机下界。通过更优的参数化分析，得到n^{-1.6342}（non-anytime）和n^{-1.2408}（anytime）的下界指数。

Result: 1) 非任意时刻下界从Ω(n^{-1.932})改进到Ω(n^{-1.6342})；2) 任意时刻下界从Ω(n^{-4/3})改进到Ω(n^{-1.2408})；3) 结合已有的silver schedules非任意时刻上界O(n^{-log2(1+√2)})（约n^{-1.2716}），证明anytime下界(1.2408)与non-anytime上界(1.2716)之间存在严格分离，即anytime设置下可实现的收敛指数严格优于non-anytime设置。

Conclusion: 本文显著推进了对预定步长GD在光滑凸优化中加速极限的理解，证明了anytime与非anytime设置之间存在严格的收敛指数分离，为理解该问题的理论边界提供了重要贡献。

Abstract: We study how far gradient descent (GD) can be accelerated by predetermined stepsizes in smooth convex optimization. Going beyond the classical $Ω(n^{-2})$ first-order oracle lower bound of Nemirovsky and Yudin, we prove an $Ω(n^{-1.6342})$ non-anytime lower bound and an $Ω(n^{-1.2408})$ anytime lower bound. These improve the recent $Ω(n^{-1.932})$ non-anytime lower bound of Ma and Chen and the $Ω(n^{-4/3})$ anytime lower bound of Tsai et al., respectively. Together with the non-anytime $O(n^{-\log_2(1+\sqrt{2})})$ rate achieved by silver schedules, our anytime lower bound establishes a strict separation between the achievable convergence exponents in the two settings.

</details>


### [256] [Fundamental Limits of Adaptive Stabilization with an Unknown Growth Exponent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02740)
*Zhaobo Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究离散时间非线性自适应控制中，当系统增长指数未知时的临界稳定性指数。研究发现，当增长指数与系数均未知时，临界指数从已知指数情形下的4变为3√3/2，展示了不确定性维度的增加如何改变可稳定增长的边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应控制的基本问题是：具有未知参数的离散时间非线性系统在保持可稳定性的前提下能够以多快速度增长。已有理论确定了当增长指数已知且仅标量系数未知时，临界指数精确为4。但实际中增长指数本身往往也是未知的，问题是如何确定这种额外不确定性下临界指数的变化。

Method: 本文通过理论分析建立临界指数的精确刻画：对于任意正扰动界，单一反馈律在所有位于标称参数对邻域内的系统上实现稳定，当且仅当标称指数低于3√3/2。论文还分别考察了有限已知指数集、已知正状态相关乘子、以及系数和指数为未知参数已知函数等不同情形下的临界指数条件。

Result: 关键结果为：当增长指数与系数均未知时，临界指数为3√3/2；在等号处，任何指数不超过该值的紧致参数集仍然可稳定化。若指数属于已知有限集，则临界指数对每个非退化紧致系数区间仍为4。此外，在未知参数函数的参数族情形下，秩条件和内部参数点的Jacobian条件决定了可稳定化与否。

Conclusion: 研究表明，增长速率的不确定性不仅扩大了不确定性集合的大小，更根本地改变了反馈能够稳定的增长范围。有限指数集与任意短的指数区间可以有不同的临界指数，这揭示了不确定性本质（离散vs连续）对可稳定性的深刻影响。

Abstract: A basic question in adaptive control is how rapidly a discrete-time nonlinear plant with unknown parameters may grow while remaining stabilizable. When the nonlinear growth exponent is known and only a scalar coefficient is unknown, existing theory identifies 4 as the exact critical exponent for stabilizability. We determine how this critical exponent changes when the growth exponent is also unknown. For any positive disturbance bound, one feedback law stabilizes all plants in some neighborhood of a nominal parameter pair if and only if the nominal exponent is below $3\sqrt{3}/2$. At equality, every compact parameter set whose exponents do not exceed this value remains stabilizable. If the exponent belongs to a known finite set, the critical exponent remains 4 for every nondegenerate compact coefficient interval. Hence finite exponent sets and arbitrarily short exponent intervals can have different critical exponents. Uncertainty in the growth rate therefore changes the range of growth that feedback can stabilize, not merely the size of the uncertainty set. The critical exponent remains $3\sqrt{3}/2$ under a known positive state-dependent multiplier bounded above and away from zero. For a system whose coefficient and exponent are known functions of an unknown parameter, a compact parameter family is stabilizable when its exponents do not exceed this value. At an interior parameter point where the Jacobian of these functions has rank two and the exponent is at least this value, no compact neighborhood is stabilizable.

</details>


### [257] [Gaussian-Restricted Barycenters for KL-Unbalanced Optimal Transport: Variational Theory and Fixed-Point Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02870)
*Jiaping Yang,Yunxin Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有独立边际惩罚且无耦合熵的二次双边KL非平衡最优传输问题下的高斯限制重心。通过精确刻画重心质量的剖面，将问题简化为带有内源Gibbs权重的光滑高斯形状泛函。建立了全局可达性，推导了平稳矩方程，并提出了一种反向KL的MM迭代算法，该算法从任何非退化高斯初始值出发均收敛到平稳不动点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解并求解非平衡最优传输框架下的高斯限制重心，特别是当边际惩罚相互独立且不存在耦合熵时的情形，填补该设定下理论分析与算法求解的空白。

Method: 通过质量剖面简化问题以获得光滑的高斯形状泛函；推导平稳矩方程；构造反向KL的MM（majorization-minimization）迭代；分析对角二阶变分引出平行和（parallel-sum）张量耦合Bures-Wasserstein与Fisher-Rao度量；利用有限质量扩展的径向锥表示；研究共同惩罚尺度下的极限行为。

Result: 证明了全局最小解在共同惩罚尺度下收敛到具有有效权重的Gaussian Wasserstein重心；惩罚足够大时解唯一且有一阶解析展开；小惩罚区域中，全局最小解到加权Chernoff亲和泛函的紧致极大值集的距离按均值-协方差参数距离趋于零。数值实验验证了MM下降、局部收缩及两个惩罚极限的行为。

Conclusion: 该工作为二次双边KL非平衡最优传输下的高斯重心提供了完整的理论刻画（存在性、收敛性、唯一性与极限行为）以及有效的MM数值求解方案，揭示了非平衡重心在大/小惩罚极限下与经典Wasserstein重心及Chernoff亲和泛函之间的深层联系。

Abstract: We study Gaussian-restricted barycenters for quadratic two-sided Kullback--Leibler unbalanced optimal transport with independent marginal penalties and no coupling entropy. Exact profiling of the barycenter mass reduces the problem to a smooth Gaussian shape functional with endogenous Gibbs weights. We establish global attainment, derive the stationary moment equations, and construct a reverse-KL majorization--minimization (MM) iteration whose full sequence converges from every nondegenerate Gaussian initialization to a stationary fixed point. The diagonal second variation induces a parallel-sum tensor coupling the Bures--Wasserstein and Fisher--Rao metrics; its finite-mass extension admits a radial cone representation. Under common penalty scaling, global minimizers converge to a Gaussian Wasserstein barycenter with effective weights; for sufficiently large penalties, the minimizer is unique and admits a first-order analytic expansion. In the small-penalty regime, the distance of every global minimizer to the compact maximizer set of a weighted Chernoff affinity functional vanishes with respect to the mean--covariance parameter distance. Numerical experiments illustrate MM descent, local contraction, and the two penalty limits.

</details>

### [258] [Price manipulation in nonlinear transient impact models: rigidity before memory and complete positivity after memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02447)
*Minhyeok Lee*

Main category: q-fin.TR

TL;DR: 本文系统分类了瞬时冲击模型两种组合顺序下的价格操纵判定条件：当瞬时律作用于交易率（记忆前）时，非负往返成本迫使该律为仿射（线性）；当单调读出作用于冲击状态（记忆后）时，安全性等价于记忆核的完全正性。研究覆盖了奇异核、幂律冲击与幂律衰减的组合，并精确刻画了各种记忆模型的操纵区域、摩擦、相位与切换复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于无价格操纵的刚性定理要求记忆核在零处有界，且Gatheral提出的慢速率两区块界δ+γ≥1无法覆盖奇异核情形。作者希望针对奇异Volterra核彻底分类两种组合顺序下的操纵判定，填补理论空白，并对实际校准的指数对给出明确结论。

Method: 针对两种组合顺序分别建立判定框架：(1)瞬时律作用于交易率、核为Volterra核时，通过构造零体积"抖动泵"配合两条薄基线交易的往返论证，证明安全性迫使f仿射/线性；(2)单调读出作用于冲击状态时，将安全性等价于核的完全正性，并给出构造性逆命题。同时引入远程补偿区块区分往返安全与全输入安全，并推广到Prony核、非时齐记忆和矩阵记忆等一般框架。

Result: 任意连续瞬时律与奇异Volterra核组合的安全性要求f仿射；对幂律冲击sgn(x)|x|^δ与幂律衰减t^{-γ}的组合，操纵当且仅当δ≠1，即平方根冲击在任意衰减指数下均可操纵，Gatheral的不等式区域δ+γ≥1坍缩为直线δ=1。记忆后情形下，完全正性保证所有读出均安全，平方根冲击+幂律记忆无操纵。经校准的指数对全部落入可操纵集合。

Conclusion: 在无摩擦条件下，市场影响函数的凹性必须置于记忆之后而非之前，否则必然引入价格操纵。该结论具有实践警示意义：常见的先冲击后记忆的平方根模型需要修正，而完全正性框架为后记忆结构提供了无操纵的理论保障。

Abstract: Transient impact models compose a nonlinearity with a memory kernel, and the order of composition determines the criterion for absence of price manipulation. We classify both orders. If an arbitrary instantaneous law $f$ acts on the trading rate before any nonzero integrable Volterra kernel, nonnegative cost on every finite piecewise-constant round trip forces $f$ to be affine, and linear for every nonzero convolution kernel. In particular, the power law $\mathrm{sgn}(x)|x|^δ$, $δ>0$, combined with power-law decay $t^{-γ}$, $0<γ<1$, admits manipulation if and only if $δ\ne1$: square-root impact is manipulable at every decay exponent, and the region left open by Gatheral's slow-rate two-block bound $δ+γ\ge1$ collapses to the line $δ=1$. Earlier rigidity theorems require a kernel that is bounded at zero; the argument here is a zero-volume chattering pump read out by two thin baseline trades, and it applies to singular kernels. If instead a monotone readout acts on the impact state after the kernel, safety for all inputs and all readouts is equivalent to complete positivity of the kernel, with a constructive converse; in particular, square-root impact after power-law memory is manipulation-free. A remote compensating block shows that round-trip safety and all-input safety coincide for kernels with uniformly vanishing tails and differ, for permanent memory, by an explicit storage quotient. These mechanisms classify every two-mode Prony kernel, first-order time-inhomogeneous memory, and stable fully actuated matrix memory, and they quantify the friction, the two-block phase, and the switching complexity behind the power-law case. Calibrated exponent pairs all lie in the manipulable set: absent friction, concavity has to enter after the memory, not before it.

</details>
